### [1] [RAFT-DVC: Resolution-Aware Machine Learning-Based Digital Volume Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01876)
*Zixiang Tong,Lehu Bu,Jin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了RAFT-DVC，一个基于递归全对场变换（RAFT）架构的、分辨率感知的数字体相关性（DVC）求解器家族，通过编码器下采样因子s=2、4、8实现三种分辨率配置。研究发现三种求解器可定位位移至约0.017个特征网格体素，对应经验原始体素误差缩放约0.017s体素；该框架在粗纹理、大位移条件下相比经典DVC具有竞争力或优势，并可通过校正采样器几何来提升原生输入精度及泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字体相关性（DVC）虽能从体积图像中提供三维全场位移测量，但基于机器学习的DVC模型内部分辨率如何影响其精度和适用范围（operating range）至今缺乏清晰理解，这一问题构成了本研究的主要动机。

Method: 作者提出RAFT-DVC，即一个分辨率感知的递归全对场变换（RAFT）家族，包含编码器下采样因子s=2、4、8三种配置，采用匹配实验设计进行比较；同时设计了频率扫描测试以量化变形空间分辨率，采用平铺推理（tiled inference）支持大规模体积的稠密估计，并引入非立方体脉冲测试（non-cubic impulse test）独立于网络训练验证采样器几何。

Result: 三种求解器均能将位移定位至约0.017特征网格体素，经验原始误差缩放约为0.017s体素；各求解器由位移范围和体积纹理兼容性共同决定的互补工作区间。合成基准表明，在细纹理、小到中位移条件下RAFT-DVC误差与调优的经典DVC同阶，在粗纹理、大位移条件下则具竞争力或优势。在压痕实验的共聚焦体积图像和微CT弹性体泡沫图像上分别验证了工作区间匹配的重要性及跨纹理迁移能力。修正采样器坐标顺序不一致后，原生输入精度和未见体积维度的泛化能力均得到提升。

Conclusion: RAFT-DVC被确立为一个快速、分辨率感知的稠密DVC框架，其精度与工作区间均得到系统表征；该工作为实现DVC求解器的分辨率控制及推广提供了理论依据和实践途径。

Abstract: Digital volume correlation (DVC) provides three-dimensional full-field displacement measurements from volumetric images, but how the internal resolution of a machine-learning-based DVC model affects accuracy and operating range remains poorly understood. Here, we present RAFT-DVC, a resolution-aware family of recurrent all-pairs field transforms (RAFT)-based DVC solvers with encoder downsampling factors s = 2, 4, and 8. Using a matched design, we find that the three solvers localize displacement to approximately 0.017 feature-grid voxel, giving an empirical raw-volume error scaling of approximately 0.017s voxel. The solvers exhibit complementary operating regimes governed jointly by displacement reach and volumetric-texture compatibility. Synthetic benchmarks show that RAFT-DVC achieves errors of the same order as tuned classical DVC under fine-texture, small-to-moderate-displacement conditions and becomes competitive or advantageous under coarse-texture, large-displacement conditions. Frequency-swept tests quantify deformation spatial resolution, while tiled inference enables dense estimation on large volumes. Evaluation on confocal volumetric images acquired during indentation illustrates the importance of matching solver operating regime to deformation magnitude and image texture. Tests on micro-CT images of elastomeric foam, despite training only on particle-labeled synthetic data, provide evidence of cross-texture transfer. We also identify coordinate-order inconsistencies in three-dimensional RAFT correlation sampling and introduce a non-cubic impulse test to verify sampler geometry independently of network training. Correcting the sampler improves native-input accuracy and generalization to unseen volume dimensions. Together, these results establish RAFT-DVC as a fast, resolution-aware framework for dense DVC with characterized accuracy and operating regimes.

</details>

### [2] [Double Gravity-Assist Rendezvous Trajectory to Halley's Comet Using Deep-Space Low Thrust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02189)
*Roberto Flores,Alessandro Beolchi,Chiara Pozzi,Mauro Pontani,Ivano Bertini,Cesare Barbieri,Elena Fantino*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合木星与土星无动力引力辅助（gravity-assist）及深空低推力（low-thrust）弧段的交会任务策略，用于在2061年哈雷彗星近日点接近时实现探测。该策略将设计参数压缩至三个，并首次证明仅凭成熟技术（标准放射性同位素热电发电机与霍尔效应推进器）即可实现交会任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1986年哈雷彗星飞掠任务因逆行高倾角轨道导致相对速度过大，在彗发内停留时间过短，限制了科学回报；交会任务可克服此局限，但受当前推进技术限制设计难度大，且考虑到航天器研制周期与星际转移时长，亟需尽早启动任务规划。

Method: 提出一种新颖的交会策略，组合无动力木星与土星引力辅助及深空低推力弧段；利用双重飞掠策略降低原本代价高昂的轨道面改变机动成本；通过闭合形式的飞掠几何最优解析表达式与显式低推力转写技术，将设计参数缩减为三个，并描述了轨迹优化策略及设计空间全面探索方法。

Result: 该方案在最小化发射能量与推进剂预算的同时，约束到达时间早于彗星高活动期。研究表明该任务概念可仅依赖成熟技术（标准放射性同位素热电发电机与霍尔效应推进器）实现，且兼容多个现有运载火箭。最后详细给出了两条有前景的概念验证轨迹。

Conclusion: 这是首个可采用经过充分验证的技术实现的哈雷彗星交会任务方案，在提高计算效率的同时克服了传统交会设计中的推进限制，为2061年哈雷彗星回归的科学探测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The perihelion of comet 1P/Halley in 2061 is an excellent chance to revisit this object of outstanding scientific and cultural relevance. During its 1986 approach to the Sun, it was targeted by several flyby missions. Due to its retrograde, highly-inclined orbit, the relative velocities during the encounter were large, limiting the scientific return due to the short time spent inside the coma. A rendezvous trajectory would overcome this limitation, but the design is challenging due to the limitations of current propulsion technology. Given the lead times of spacecraft development and the long duration of the interplanetary transfer to the comet, it is imperative to start mission planning as soon as possible. We present a novel rendezvous strategy, combining unpowered Jupiter and Saturn gravity-assists with deep-space low-thrust arcs. It minimizes launch energy and propellant budget, constraining the arrival to occur before the onset of high activity. The double flyby strategy reduces the otherwise prohibitive cost of the plane change maneuver. Closed expressions for the optimal flyby geometry, together with an explicit low-thrust transcription technique, reduce the number of design parameter to three, improving computational efficiency. Crucially, this is the first rendezvous mission concept achievable with well-proven technology (standard radioisotope thermoelectric generators and a Hall-effect thruster) and compatible with several existing launchers. We describe the trajectory optimization strategy and perform a comprehensive exploration of the design space. Finally, we present two promising proof-of-concept trajectories in detail.

</details>

### [3] [Disc-Based Estimation of the Local Dark Matter Density and Velocity Distribution in IllustrisTNG50](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02185)
*Hatsume Chujo,Shogo Masaki,Keiko I. Nagao,Takuho Nakabayashi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 该研究利用IllustrisTNG模拟套件的TNG50-1运行数据，在类银河系星系中基于银盘提取方法（disc-based extraction）研究了太阳邻域的暗物质（dark matter）密度与速度分布。研究发现局部暗物质密度约为0.47±0.02 GeV cm⁻³，与观测推断一致；速度分布可很好地由各向同性麦克斯韦分布（isotropic Maxwellian）描述，峰值速度约226 km/s，接近标准晕模型（standard halo model）假设值，且提取方式的差异不会显著影响直接探测实验解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质的局域密度与速度分布是解读暗物质直接探测实验的关键输入参数，但此前研究中不同的提取与计算方法常导致结果不一致。本研究旨在利用高分辨率宇宙学模拟，通过更接近真实银盘物理的提取方式，为太阳邻域暗物质性质提供更可靠的约束。

Method: 作者使用IllustrisTNG模拟套件中的TNG50-1运行数据，依据晕质量（halo mass）和核球与总恒星质量比（bulge-to-total stellar mass ratio）选取类银河系星系；随后在所选星系中根据恒星的位置与数量密度确定太阳邻域区域，提取与银盘相关的暗物质粒子并抑制来自暗物质晕的成分；最后对所选星系求平均，比较基于银盘的提取与基于简单距离的提取在密度和速度分布上的差异。

Result: 平均得到局部暗物质密度ρ≈0.47±0.02 GeV cm⁻³，与观测推断范围一致。基于银盘的提取给出更高的峰值密度（0.41±0.02 GeV cm⁻³），高于简单距离提取值（0.34±0.02 GeV cm⁻³），原因可能在于排除了晕中的低密度暗物质成分；密度分布的宽度则几乎不变。速度分布可用各向同性麦克斯韦分布良好描述，峰值速度约226 km/s，接近标准晕模型假设值，且两种提取方式的差异对直接探测实验的解读影响不大。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，基于银盘的暗物质提取方法可获得与观测一致的局部暗物质密度，且速度分布与标准晕模型中的麦克斯韦假设吻合良好。不同提取方式导致的密度和速度分布差异不足以显著改变直接探测实验结果的物理解释，从而为现有实验约束提供了模拟层面的支持。

Abstract: We study the dark matter (DM) density and velocity distribution in the solar neighborhood of Milky Way-like galaxies using the TNG50-1 run of the IllustrisTNG simulation suite. Milky Way-like galaxies are selected according to both their halo mass and their bulge-to-total stellar mass ratio, and the solar-neighborhood region within each galaxy is identified from the positions and number density of stars, so that DM particles associated with the galactic disc are extracted while contributions from the halo component are suppressed. Averaging over the selected galaxies, we obtain a local DM density of $ρ\simeq 0.47\pm0.02~{\rm GeV\,cm^{-3}}$, consistent with the range inferred from observations. The disc-based extraction gives a higher peak density, $0.41\pm0.02~{\rm GeV\,cm^{-3}}$, than the value $0.34\pm0.02~{\rm GeV\,cm^{-3}}$ from a simple distance-based extraction, probably because it excludes the low-density DM in the halo. The width of the density distribution, on the other hand, is nearly unchanged. The velocity distribution is well described by an isotropic Maxwellian with a peak velocity of about $226~{\rm km~s^{-1}}$, close to the value assumed in the standard halo model. The differences in the density and velocity distributions between our disc-based and simple distance-based extractions are not so large as to significantly affect the interpretation of direct-detection experiments.

</details>

### [4] [The entropy production is not always monotone for hard spheres or for Maxwell molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01753)
*Luis Silvestre*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文回答了McKean于1966年提出的开放问题：玻尔兹曼方程的熵产生（entropy production）是否必须随时间单调递减。作者证明在空间均匀（space-homogeneous）背景下，即使对于常角截面和动力学参数γ∈[0,1]的玻尔兹曼碰撞算子，熵产生也不必单调递减，从而同时否证了硬球（hard spheres）和麦克斯韦分子（Maxwell molecules）情形。例子由麦克斯韦分布的径向对称混合构成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: McKean在1966年提出疑问：玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equation）的熵产生是否必须随时间单调递减。这一问题长期悬而未决，作者旨在通过构造反例给出否定回答。

Method: 作者在空间均匀设定下，考虑具有恒定角截面的玻尔兹曼碰撞算子，动力学参数γ∈[0,1]，并构造径向对称的多麦克斯韦分布混合（mixture of Maxwellians）作为反例系统，以检验熵产生的时间单调性。

Result: 结果表明，即使在此类经典情形下（涵盖硬球和麦克斯韦分子的情况），熵产生也并非必须随时间单调递减，从而推翻了对McKean问题的肯定预设。

Conclusion: 玻尔兹曼方程的熵产生单调递减并非普遍成立的性质，即使是在空间均匀且涉及经典碰撞模型的情形下亦如此，这一发现对理解玻尔兹曼方程的熵动力学具有重要意义。

Abstract: In 1966, McKean asked whether the entropy production of the Boltzmann equation must be monotone decreasing in time. We show that this is not the case even in the space-homogeneous setting for the Boltzmann collision operator with a constant angular cross section and the kinetic parameter $γ\in [0,1]$. This recovers the classical case of hard spheres and the simplest case of Maxwell molecules. Our examples are radially symmetric mixtures of Maxwellians.

</details>


### [5] [An extension theory for fully fractional Schrödinger equations with memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01826)
*Nicola Garofalo,Gigliola Staffilani*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究带记忆的非线性薛定谔方程，通过完全分数阶薛定谔算子 $(∂_t−iΔ_x)^s$ $(0<s<1)$ 的扩张理论展开分析。该算子时空均非局部，故 Cauchy 问题需以过去历史（prescribed past history）而非经典初值给出。作者构造了该算子的半群定义，推导出附加一个空间维度的等价局部扩张问题，并显式计算了相应的振荡 Poisson 核。扩张给出历史的 Poisson 提升和能量二次型，建立了提升的加权体能量与非局部记忆能量的显式恒等式。结合伴随论文的边界非线性相互作用理论，作者获得了相应非线性记忆问题的适定性理论，在扩张诱导的温和意义下建立——一般历史在自然能量空间中，未来演化定义为半空间解的边界迹，且在附加图域假设下，分数阶方程本身在 $L^2$ 和正时间上得以恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带记忆的非线性薛定谔方程因其算子 $(\partial_t−iΔ_x)^s$ 同时具有空间和时间非局部性，缺乏经典框架下的适定性基础。作者旨在为这类方程建立严格的数学理论，包括算子定义、等价局部化以及非线性问题的适定性。

Method: 作者首先构造了完全分数阶薛定谔算子 $(\partial_t−iΔ_x)^s$ 的半群定义，然后通过引入一个额外的空间变量，将该非局部算子转化为等价的局部扩张问题（extension problem），并显式计算了对应的振荡 Poisson 核。在此基础上，扩张自然地产生历史的 Poisson 提升及内在二次型，并推导出提升加权体能量与历史定义的分数阶能量之间的显式恒等式。最后，结合伴随论文中建立的边界非线性相互作用理论，在扩张诱导的温和意义下证明非线性记忆问题的适定性。

Result: 论文给出了扩张问题的振荡 Poisson 核的显式表达式，建立了扩张提升的加权体能量与完全由历史决定的非局部能量之间的恒等关系。在附加图域假设下，作者证明了非线性记忆问题在扩展诱导的温和意义下的适定性；未来演化由半空间解的边界迹给出，方程本身在 $L^2$ 和正时间上得以恢复，且此结果适用于自然能量空间中的一般历史数据。

Conclusion: 本文通过扩张理论为带记忆的非线性薛定谔方程建立了完整的分析框架，将时空非局部问题转化为经典的局部边值问题，并通过边界迹机制给出了非线性记忆问题的适定性结果。该工作为后续进一步研究此类非局部演化方程提供了理论基础和方法工具。

Abstract: We study nonlinear Schrödinger equations with memory through an extension theory for the fully fractional Schrödinger operator \[ (\partial_t-iΔ_x)^s,\qquad 0<s<1. \] Because the operator is nonlocal in both space and time, its natural Cauchy problem is posed with a prescribed past history rather than a classical initial condition. We construct a semigroup definition of $(\partial_t-iΔ_x)^s$, derive an equivalent local extension problem in one additional spatial variable, and compute the corresponding oscillatory Poisson kernel explicitly. The extension yields a Poisson lifting of history data and an intrinsic quadratic form on histories, establishing an explicit identity between the weighted bulk energy of the lifting and a nonlocal energy determined entirely by the prescribed past evolution. As an application, and in combination with the nonlinear boundary-interaction theory developed in our companion paper, we obtain a well-posedness theory for the associated nonlinear memory problem. The well-posedness is established in an extension-induced mild sense: for a general history in the natural energy space the future evolution is defined as the boundary trace of the corresponding solution in the half-space, and the fractional equation itself is recovered, in $L^2$ and for positive times, under an additional graph-domain hypothesis.

</details>


### [6] [Inverse Semiclassical Scattering at Fixed Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02175)
*François Nicoleau*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了半经典薛定谔算子（semiclassical Schrödinger operators）在固定能量下的逆散射问题。主要结果表明：在光滑势函数下，若半经典散射矩阵在高频极限下相差$o(1)$，则对应的经典散射映射（classical scattering map）一致；结合Stefanov–Uhlmann–Vasy的经典刚性结果，可在特定virial条件下由散射矩阵唯一确定势函数。此外，对径向短程排斥势，利用Robert–Tamura的半经典散射渐近与Firsov–Abel逆散射公式，证明了在单一固定能量下的微分散射截面（differential cross section）可决定经典可及区域内的势函数；最后还得到了简单紧支撑度规扰动的类似刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆散射问题是数学物理中的核心课题，旨在从散射数据反演散射系统的势函数或几何结构。本文希望在半经典极限（$h\to0$）框架下，研究固定能量散射矩阵能否唯一决定势函数，建立量子与经典散射之间的对应关系，并回答单一能量散射数据是否足以完成势函数重建。

Method: 本文采用多种分析工具的组合。首先利用Ingremeau关于散射矩阵在相干态（coherent states）上作用的描述，建立半经典散射矩阵与经典散射映射之间的联系。其次结合Stefanov–Uhlmann–Vasy的经典光滑势刚性结果，在virial条件下推导势函数的唯一性。对于径向情形，利用Robert–Tamura的半经典散射渐近理论结合经典的Firsov–Abel逆变换公式，从微分散射截面重建势函数。同样的论证方法也被推广到度规扰动情形。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）对紧支撑光滑势，在非俘获能量下，若半经典散射矩阵之差在算子范数意义下为$o(1)$，则经典散射映射重合；在自然virial条件和能量高于势的假设下，势函数可由散射矩阵唯一决定。（2）对满足径向力单调性假设的短程排斥势，经典散射关系为单支的，单一固定能量下的微分散射截面可完全确定经典可及区域内的势函数。（3）对简单紧支撑度规扰动，获得了类似的刚性（rigidity）结果。

Conclusion: 本文的工作表明，在半经典极限框架下，固定能量的散射数据（散射矩阵或微分散射截面）在适当的条件和假设下确实能为势函数（或度规扰动）的重建提供充分信息，建立了量子散射到经典散射再到势函数反演之间的完整链条。

Abstract: We investigate inverse scattering at a fixed energy for semiclassical

  semiclassical scattering matrices differ by $o(1)$ in operator norm as
  $h\to0$, then the associated classical scattering maps coincide. The
  proof relies on Ingremeau's description of the action of the scattering
  matrix on coherent states. Combined with a classical rigidity result of
  Stefanov--Uhlmann--Vasy, this yields uniqueness of the potential under a
  natural virial condition, provided the energy lies above the potential.
  We then consider radial short-range repulsive potentials. Under a
  monotonicity assumption on the radial force, the classical scattering
  relation has a single branch. Combining the semiclassical scattering
  asymptotics of Robert--Tamura with the classical Firsov--Abel inversion
  formula, we show that the differential cross section at a single fixed
  energy determines the potential throughout the classically accessible
  region. Finally, we obtain an analogous rigidity result for simple
  compactly supported metric perturbations.

Method: 设计了两鱼群分离于水填充透明间隙的受控实验，将视觉隔离为唯一交互通道；在边界约束下观测各鱼群质心朝向的噪声双稳态动力学，并通过两个耦合随机双稳态振荡器的模型进行数学刻画，利用精确解对实验结果进行定量拟合与验证。

Abstract: We investigate how sensory coupling mediates phase coherence between spatially separated, confined active systems. Using two fish schools separated by a water-filled transparent gap, we isolate vision as the primary interaction channel. Under boundary confinement, each school's center-of-mass heading exhibits noisy bistable dynamics, spontaneously switching between two antiparallel directions along the partition wall. Turning events in one school bias the orientation of the other, driving distance-dependent phase synchronization. We model this behavior using two coupled stochastic bistable oscillators whose exact solution quantitatively reproduces the empirical results. Our work establishes a precise connection between nonequilibrium active systems and coupled stochastic oscillators, offering insight into boundary-spanning information transfer in living collectives.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑绝缘体（topological insulators, TIs）依赖相邻体区域之间的拓扑对比度，通常需要不同的对称性来实现，这限制了设计的灵活性和更广泛的应用前景。作者旨在克服这一范式限制，探索一种更灵活、可控的拓扑态诱导方案。

Method: 作者提出在时间反演对称系统中施加可控的外部场来诱导拓扑态。理论上，该外部场修正了Jackiw-Rebbi机制，在实空间中触发由局域拓扑标记（local topological marker, LTM）表征的拓扑相变；同时建立了扩展的谷体-边界对应关系（valley bulk-edge correspondence），统一解释常规情形与本文发现。

Result: 该方案诱导的拓扑态超越常规体拓扑设计范式，可存在于几何边缘、体内部，甚至可被远程诱导。由于外部场高度可控，这些态具有可编程性、可重构性，并能被便捷地形塑成理想图案。该结果在力学、电子、声学三重平台上均获得验证。

Conclusion: 本工作推进了拓扑学的理论发展，同时增强了拓扑态与拓扑材料的多样性、可调谐性和实际可实现性，展示了该方法对多种物理系统的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Topological insulators (TIs), recognized for their robust boundary states and unconventional phase transitions, have emerged as one of the most impactful discoveries in recent decades, attracting considerable interest across diverse fields. However, conventional TIs require topological contrasts between adjacent bulk regions, typically achieved by distinct symmetries, which limits their flexibility and broader applicability. In this work, we introduce a "lithography" approach to inducing topological states by applying external fields in time-reversal-symmetric systems. These states transcend the conventional bulk topology design paradigm and offer exceptional tunability: they can exist at geometric edges, within the bulk, or even be induced remotely. Because the external field is highly controllable, the induced states are programmable, reconfigurable, and can be easily tailored into desirable patterns. Theoretically, we demonstrate that the external field modifies the Jackiw-Rebbi mechanism, inducing a real-space topological transition characterized by a local topological marker (LTM). We further establish an extended valley bulk-edge correspondence, which explains both the conventional scenario and our findings. Our results, validated across mechanical, electronic, and acoustic platforms, highlight the broad applicability of this approach to various systems. This work not only advances the theory of topology but also enhances the diversity, tunability, and practical implementation of topological states and materials.

</details>

Motivation: 磁电相变已在实验中被广泛研究，但尚缺乏宏观理论框架来解释其动力学起源，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Result: 针对不同磁电材料分别推导了静置轴子角、轴子频率与相变温度之间的定量关系，计算结果与现有实验数据吻合；同时得到静置轴子角与实验测量的频率偏移（frequency shift）之间的关联。

Conclusion: 轴子凝聚类过程能够为磁电相变提供统一的动力学解释框架，且理论预测与实验观测一致，显示了该框架在电磁材料统一描述方面的解释力。

Abstract: Magnetoelectric phase transitions have been experimentally studied, but no macroscopic theory has been proposed to explain their dynamical origin. In this work, we assume that the axion quasiparticle with frequency undergoes a condensation like process. We show that these magnetoelectric phase transitions can be described within a Ginzbur Landau framework by introducing a coupled dynamic parameter, the axion angle, which is proportional to the magnetoelectric coeffcient. We derive relations between the static axion angle, the axion frequency, and the phase transition temperature for different magnetoelectric materials, respectively, and compare these calculations with existing experimental results. We also connect the artificially designed Dzyaloshinskii Moriya interaction with the axion condensate like process, so that the relation between the static axion angle and the experimentally measured frequency shift can be obtained.

</details>

*Haylen Gerhard,Wladimir A. Benalcazar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子作为复合准粒子，其Wannier函数本质上由质心位置和内部电子-空穴偶极共同刻画，但在实空间中同时分辨电子与空穴坐标需要共同对角化对应的投影位置算符，而这些算符一般不对易，导致无法建立共同的本征基且联合展宽存在态依赖的下界。已有的方法只能局域化单一的平均坐标或特定组分坐标，无法构建同时最大化局域化两个组分坐标的共同激子Wannier基。

Method: 作者构造了一个单一厄米算符——“激子空间定位算符”，该算符将电子与空穴位置算符嵌入到克利福德代数结构中，从而能够同时最大化局域化电子和空穴坐标并返回激子Wannier函数。该公式无需变分假设（ansatz-free），具有规范不变性（gauge-invariant），并适用于一般的多带情形。

Result: 在具有孤立激子能带组的相互作用双层模型中，作者证明反射对称性（reflection）、时间反演对称性（time-reversal）以及其他非辛空间对称性（nonsymmorphic symmetries）强制产生了逐点无迹但非零的量子几何偶极矩阵（quantum geometric dipole matrix），该矩阵表现为对称相关的激子Wannier函数对，它们具有相等且相反的内部电子-空穴偶极。

Conclusion: 该工作为激子Wannier函数的内部结构描述提供了统一框架，能够同时最大化局域化电子与空穴坐标，并揭示了离散对称性对激子内部偶极分布的约束规律。

Abstract: The internal electron-hole structure of excitons influences optical and electric-field responses. Exciton Wannier functions have recently provided a local real-space representation of exciton bands. Because excitons are composite quasiparticles, these Wannier functions can be characterized by both their center-of-mass location and the internal electron-hole dipole. Resolving this internal structure in real space requires simultaneously locating the constituent electron and hole coordinates. However, the projected electron and hole position operators generally do not commute, so simultaneous diagonalization of these positions is impossible: their noncommutativity precludes a common eigenbasis and imposes a state-dependent lower bound on the joint spread. Existing approaches localize a single average coordinate or a specific constituent coordinate, but generally do not construct a common exciton Wannier basis that maximally localizes both constituent coordinates. Here, we construct an ``exciton spatial localizer'': a single Hermitian operator that embeds the constituent position operators within a Clifford-algebra structure and returns exciton Wannier functions, maximally localized in their electron and hole coordinates simultaneously. Our formulation is ansatz-free, gauge-invariant, and applies to the generic multiband case. In an interacting bilayer model with an isolated group of exciton bands, we show that reflection and time-reversal symmetries, or other nonsymmorphic symmetries detailed herein, enforce a pointwise traceless but nonzero quantum geometric dipole matrix, which manifests in symmetry-related pairs of exciton Wannier functions with equal and opposite internal electron-hole dipoles.

</details>

Motivation: 反铁磁体中的非相对论性自旋劈裂（NRSS）能够实现无磁化的自旋极化，对超快自旋电子学具有重要意义；然而如何在反铁磁体中动态诱导并可控调节这一自旋劈裂仍是待解决的问题。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）计算方法对MnPS₃进行研究，通过激发Γ点红外活性声子来诱导自旋简并解除，并分析剩余的子格连接对称性（sublattice-connecting symmetries）如何决定诱导劈裂的动量空间形式。

Abstract: Nonrelativistic spin splitting (NRSS) in antiferromagnets (AFMs) enables magnetization-free spin polarization for ultrafast spintronics. Here, we demonstrate that coherent phonons can dynamically induce and control NRSS in collinear AFMs. Excitation of $Γ$-point infrared-active phonons lifts the spin degeneracy of the ground state, while the residual sublattice-connecting symmetries determine the momentum-space form of the induced splitting. Because the relevant infrared modes couple to orthogonal in-plane light polarizations, distinct spin-split phases can be selectively activated by the polarization of the driving field. Using first-principles calculations for MnPS$_3$, we show that a mode that breaks all sublattice-connecting symmetries induces an $s$-wave spin-split state with $Δ(Γ)\neq0$, whereas a symmetry-distinct mode that preserves a sublattice-connecting mirror symmetry generates a $d$-wave altermagnetic state with $Δ(Γ)=0$. In both cases, the spin splitting grows linearly with the phonon amplitude and reverses the induced spin polarization when the displacement is reversed. Our results establish coherent lattice driving as a direct, polarization- and mode-selective route to dynamically induce distinct NRSS phases in AFMs.

Here, we introduce van der Waals (vdW) confinement within two-dimensional materials as a strategy for stabilizing quantum states in MSQs by engineering their local electronic, vibrational, and symmetry environments. Using cobaltocene as a model system, we show that confinement within vdW SnS2 and CdPS3 single crystals reorganizes the single-ion energy landscape and slows spin-lattice relaxation by over two orders of magnitude relative to unconfined cobaltocene. The confined MSQs adopt deterministic orientations and self-assemble into ordered, atomically precise superlattices, establishing vdW confinement as a pathway for integrating MSQs into functional quantum devices.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Self-interstitial atom (SIA) clustering is a key early step in radiation damage evolution in face-centered cubic (FCC) metals, governing defect transport, recombination, and the long-term microstructural response of irradiated alloys. We combine molecular dynamics (MD) simulations and high-speed (>1000 frames/s) in situ transmission electron microscopy (TEM) to investigate the structure, energetics, and migration dynamics of SIA clusters in FCC Ni. MD simulations show that interstitials initially form disordered dumbbell clusters that evolve into either sessile Frank loops or glissile perfect (prismatic) loops; the latter progressively reorganize into compact ordered configurations with increasing mobility. Direct construction of both loop types over a wide size range, validated against MD-relaxed structures, shows that perfect loops are thermodynamically favored over Frank loops for cluster sizes N greater than or equal to 14, where N is the number of SIAs, with the energetic advantage increasing with cluster size. Nevertheless, substantial kinetic barriers allow Frank loops to persist as metastable defects. For perfect loops, diffusion coefficients computed over N = 16 to 400 reveal a nearly size-independent migration barrier of approximately 0.02 eV, while the diffusion prefactor decreases approximately as N^(-0.54). Trajectory analysis reveals a non-rigid, row-wise relay mechanism in which the number of participating atoms increases systematically with loop size, accounting for much of the observed prefactor scaling. Sub-millisecond in situ TEM observations reveal intermittent loop motion at velocities higher than previously observed but still several orders of magnitude below the intrinsic mobilities predicted by MD, indicating migration through a heterogeneous energy landscape of mobile and pinned states.

TL;DR: 本文提出MLLocP，一种机器学习策略，通过学习实空间网格上的自洽局域势并提供其位移导数，用于评估PAW框架下的全电子电子-声子矩阵元，显著降低大尺寸、低对称性及无序材料的计算成本，同时保持全电子形式主义的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子-声子相互作用在固体中决定载流子动力学、输运及许多光学和量子现象，但在投影缀加波（projector augmented-wave, PAW）框架内，有限位移计算需要对N原子体系进行多达6N次自洽超胞计算，对大型、低对称性和无序材料成本过高。

Method: MLLocP通过在实空间网格上学习自洽局域势（local potential）并输出其位移导数，从而评估全电子PAW电子-声子矩阵元。PAW分解分析表明局域势贡献是机器学习的自然目标，其余PAW量保留在既有的VASP/PHELEL框架中。训练采用针对性网格点采样和特征多样性选择以实现高效建模。

Conclusion: MLLocP为复杂材料中的PAW电子-声子计算提供了可扩展的路径，在保持底层全电子形式主义的同时显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: Electron-phonon interactions govern carrier dynamics, transport, and many optical and quantum phenomena in solids, but finite-displacement calculations within the projector augmented-wave (PAW) framework require up to 6N self-consistent supercell calculations for an N-atom system, making them costly for large, low-symmetry, and disordered materials. For the 32-atom Cu3Au special quasirandom structure, using 40 sampled configurations reduces the number of self-consistent calculations by about fivefold relative to the 192 displaced structures required directly; using 20 or 10 configurations increases the acceleration to approximately 10- and 20-fold, with conductivity errors of 14.3% and 18.6%, respectively. MLLocP thus provides a scalable route to PAW electron-phonon calculations in complex materials while preserving the underlying all-electron formalism.

Abstract: Detailed analysis of thermal conductivity results obtained from molecular dynamics (MD) trajectories conventionally relies on knowledge of the harmonic vibrational modes of the system. Such is the case in methods like Green--Kubo modal analysis (GKMA) and homogeneous nonequilibrium modal analysis (HNEMA). Chief among them is scalability, followed by the ambiguity associated to the degeneracy of the large vibrational subspaces defined by a common translational wave number k. We propose an alternative for quasi-one-dimensional (quasi-1D) systems: we construct the modal basis for the decomposition of the thermal conductivity from line-group projection operators, so that every projected component carries well-defined symmetry labels (including rotational information and parities) and the decomposition is unique at the level of irreducible representations (irreps). Applying the idea to a (10,0)-(20,0) WS2-MoS2 double-walled nanotube (DWNT) described by a neuroevolution potential (NEP), we find that at 300 K both HNEMA and GKMA yield statistically consistent total conductivities and allow the identification of several prominent symmetry-adapted conduction channels. Abstract: Inhomogeneous stress is a driving force for diffusion of vacancies in crystals. The other way around a non-uniform distribution of vacancies induces stress. The accepted theory of composition-stress coupling in crystals is Larché-Cahn (LC) theory. Composition is defined in terms of the population of lattice sites with a limit of one-particle per site. Coupling is modelled by adding a compositional strain term to the elastic strain in the constitutive relation for stress. An alternative mechanism, proposed here, is letting the site binding energy vary with spatial (deformed) density. This generates a chemical pressure adding to the elastic stress. The governing equations for this model are derived using non-equilibrium continuum thermodynamic methods. The gradient of the chemical pressure has a dual function acting both as the drift force for migration and as an effective internal one body force in the Cauchy equation for the elastic stress. The practical evaluation presented in the paper is restricted to equilibrium properties. We examine the effect of externally applied non-hydrostatic stress, either in the form of surface tractions or a one-body force density (gravitation). The model system is a one component crystal with a fixed number of lattice sites. The number of particles can be variable but is always smaller than the number of lattice sites. The linear elastic response is modelled by the standard Lamé stress tensor. The results for systems deformed by surface tractions are in qualitative agreement with LC theory allowing for differences in the expression for the elastic moduli. Deviations are more serious for the crystal deformed by gravitation.

Abstract: The phenomenon of spin wave (SW) freezing occurs in the Damon-Eshbach mode in thin film magnetic systems, when SW phase and group velocities both go to zero, and the wave ceases to oscillate and move, preserving its shape as a domain structure pattern. This effect is related to the spin reorientation transition, where the magnetization configuration changes between the homogeneous in-plane state and domain structure with the out-of-plane magnetization component state. The critical field, visible as the saturation field $H_s^{||}$ in the parallel static magnetization curve measured by superconducting quantum interference device (SQUID), is also manifested in the field-dependent vector-network-analyzer ferromagnetic resonance (VNA-FMR) experiment, which measures the homogeneous magnetization oscillations. Abstract: In computational materials science, numerical simulations are indispensable tools for revealing atomic-scale processes. These simulations enable the study of formation and growth mechanisms, transport phenomena, chemophysical stability, and chemical reactions, including catalytic processes. To unravel the complex structure--property relationships that characterise nanoobjects, it is crucial to develop robust and insightful representations of their atomistic structure at global and local scales. Such descriptions enhance our understanding of nanoparticle behaviour and play a key role in guiding their rational design in silico for targeted applications.

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展体系密度泛函理论（DFT）的实空间表述（real-space formulation），在自洽循环中无需显式计算布洛赫态（Bloch states），直接从局域高斯基函数构造类Wannier局域函数，并利用其构建电荷密度、静电势和每介质胞总能。该方法可纳入平移—旋转（螺旋）对称性，使得手性、螺旋体系的计算成本与纯平移体系相当，经线性与扭曲碳碳链、锂氟链、石墨烯及AA石墨等体系的验证，结果与参考周期计算高度一致。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We present a real-space formulation of DFT for extended systems in which localized Wannier-like functions are constructed directly from localized Gaussian basis functions without explicitly computing canonical Bloch-like states during the self-consistent cycle. Building on the formalism of Pederson and Lin [Phys. Rev. B \textbf{35}, 2273 (1987)], a variational set of Wannier-like functions is generated self-consistently within a finite Wannier domain and used to construct the charge density, electrostatic potential, and per cell total energy of the extended system. The occupied space can be determined entirely from the Wannier-like functions. Electronic band structures can be recovered in a post-processing step by solving the full Hamiltonian in a Bloch-like basis constructed from Gaussian orbitals. A key feature of the method is that it can incorporate combined translation--rotation, or screw, symmetries, enabling efficient simulations of chiral and helical systems with finite twist angles at essentially the same computational cost as systems described by pure translational symmetry. The approach is validated through calculations on linear and twisted $\mathrm{{-}C{\equiv}C{-}}$ and $\mathrm{{-}Li{-}F{-}}$ chains, as well as graphene, where total energies and band structures show excellent agreement with reference periodic calculations. To illustrate the ability of the method to treat three-dimensional systems, it is further applied to AA graphite, in which carbon atoms in adjacent graphene layers are aligned directly above one another, as well as helically stacked AA graphite structures. The Wannier-first framework provides a practical route for treating extended systems with nontrivial translational, rotational, and screw symmetries, and provides a natural foundation for the implementation of orbital-dependent functionals such as the Perdew--Zunger self-interaction correction.

Abstract: Oxygen vacancies are central to the functionality of oxides, yet they typically exist as randomly distributed point defects, limiting the ability to precisely manipulate their collective behavior. We demonstrate that the SMR amplitude scales with the sum of the absolute sublattice magnetizations in ferrimagnetic and antiferromagnetic materials. Electronic and magnetic properties of the materials were investigated with applied tension (negative pressure) and compression (positive pressure) to the crystal structure.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半局域PBE计算可能将窄带隙半导体误标为金属，导致在筛选前就误删可行的光催化剂。作者旨在修复计算二维材料数据库（C2DB）中这一失效模式，以准确识别绿氢光催化剂候选材料。

Conclusion: 高保真修正的有效性必须通过候选成员资格（candidate membership）而非仅凭全局回归误差来评估。该工作流展示了结合泄漏感知修复与Δ学习可显著改善C2DB中光催化剂筛选的可靠性。

Abstract: Semilocal PBE calculations can remove viable photocatalysts before screening by labeling narrow-gap semiconductors as metals. Stage~I classifies HSE06-unknown PBE metals using structural, chemical, magnetic, and stability descriptors, while excluding HSE06/GW quantities and PBE electronic shortcuts. Stage~II learns $E_g^{HSE} - E_g^{PBE}$ for the corrected insulating population. The curated XGBoost regressor reconstructs HSE06 gaps with a mean absolute error of 0.108~eV ($R^2=0.989$), compared with 1.036~eV for raw PBE. The workflow shows that high-fidelity correction must be evaluated by candidate membership, not only by global regression error.

The presence of nonmagnetic impurities leads to isotropisation and, consequently, weakens the effect; however, the paramagnetic response sustains intermediate amounts of impurities in the system. As demonstrated in recent experiments, microwave superfluid stiffness measurements can serve as a sensitive probe of gapless superconductivity.

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种信息引擎（information engine），利用测量与反馈将信息转化为可提取功，用于压缩由自推进"鬃毛机器人"（bristle bots）组成的活性气体。研究发现该活性引擎的平均每测量功与热学系统中的普遍形式存在定性差异，且随粒子密度增加，最大功输出在两种工作模式间切换，该现象归因于活性粒子在边界附近的聚集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 热学系统中$\bar{W}$遵循普遍形式$-p_1 \ln p_1$，而活性引擎中该关系发生定性改变。随着粒子密度增加，$\bar{W}(\Delta x)$的最大值在两种不同工作模式之间切换；这一转变同样出现在受干摩擦影响的有限时间循环的功率最大化解中。

Conclusion: 活性粒子在边界附近的积累（accumulation of active particles near boundaries）是导致该非平衡特征的根本机制，表明活性系统信息引擎的工作关系不再遵循热学系统中的普遍形式，体现了活性物质中的非平衡统计物理效应。

Abstract: Information engines convert information into extractable work through measurements and feedback. We realize an engine that employs information to compress a gas of self-propelled bristle bots. Its work per measurement, $\bar{W}$, is controlled by the size of the detection region $Δx$ and the probability $p_1$ that this region is vacant. In thermal systems, $\bar{W}$ follows the universal form $-p_1 \ln p_1$, whereas the active engine shows a qualitatively modified relation. As particle density increases, the maximum of $\bar{W}(Δx)$ switches between two distinct operating regimes. Notably, this transition is also found in the solutions that maximize power output for finite-time cycles with dynamics affected by dry friction. We attribute this nonequilibrium feature to the accumulation of active particles near the boundaries.

</details>

Conclusion: 研究表明微观活性物质（active matter）模型为超越均匀混合（well-mixed）人口假设的流行病动力学提供了有力研究框架，揭示了个体空间组织与个体保护如何共同决定传染动力学。

Abstract: Understanding how individual protection and population density influence epidemic spreading remains a central challenge in epidemiology. While classical compartmental models successfully describe the temporal evolution of epidemics, they do not explicitly account for the microscopic motion and spatial organisation of individuals. Here, we investigate contagion dynamics using an agent-based model of Active Brownian Particles (ABPs), where self-propelled agents interact through local contact and a prescribed fraction of the population is protected. By systematically varying the protected-agent fraction and the population density, we identify two distinct contagion regimes separated by a crossover protection of approximately $30\%$. Below this threshold, the maximum contagion rate follows the universal scaling $ν_{\mathrm{max}}\proptoφ^{1/2}$, indicating that disease transmission is governed primarily by frequency of encounters. Above the threshold, universal scaling is lost and protection becomes the dominant mechanism controlling epidemic spreading, with the strongest suppression occurring in low-density populations. These results demonstrate that microscopic active-matter models provide a powerful framework for investigating epidemic dynamics beyond the assumptions of well-mixed population models and reveal how spatial organisation and individual protection jointly determine contagion dynamics.

### [47] [Nonequilibrium corrections to conserved Ising criticality in scalar active matter: Ward identities, spectrum, and long crossovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02351)
*Piotr Zdybel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过泛函重整化群（functional renormalization-group, FRG）对三维有源标量场论中活性微扰（破坏细致平衡的扰动）进行分类，发现输运扇区的稳定性矩阵呈块三角结构，两个最慢指数的间距恰为伊辛反常维度（Ising anomalous dimension），并建立了近简并模式的有限尺寸标度。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往布朗热引擎的空间温度场通常被预先设定（a priori），并在此基础上计算输运和热力学性质。本文旨在补充对应的逆向设计问题：即确定优化特定热力学目标所需的温度剖面（temperature profile）和势垒高度（barrier height）。

Abstract: Spatial temperature fields in Brownian heat engines are commonly prescribed \emph{a priori}, and the resulting transport and thermodynamic properties are then calculated. Here we formulate the complementary inverse-design problem: determining the temperature profile and barrier height that optimize a chosen thermodynamic objective. In the quasistatic limit, the efficiency becomes an exact functional of two inverse-temperature integrals over the uphill and downhill branches. Its rigorous global maximum under the pointwise temperature bounds is $η_{\max}=1-\Tc/\Th$, attained uniquely, up to sets of measure zero, by the hot-uphill/cold-downhill piecewise-constant profile. At finite current, however, the optimization changes qualitatively because the current is determined jointly by the cycle affinity and a nonlocal transport resistance. We derive the exact functional gradient and the corresponding box-constrained optimality conditions, showing that the current- or power-maximizing profile generally differs from the quasistatic efficiency optimum. For any prescribed temperature field, the current-maximizing barrier satisfies an exact balance between the marginal gain in thermal rectification and the marginal increase in transport resistance, with the characteristic estimate $U_0^*\simeq T_{\rm act}$, where $T_{\rm act}^{-1}=(2/L)\int_0^{L/2}\dd x/T(x)$.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前文献中，从活性环境提取功需要直接测量活性自由度（active degree of freedom），本文旨在探索是否仅凭位置信息即可实现功提取。活性环境下系统存在非平衡涨落，理解位置与活性涨落之间的非平衡关联对设计信息引擎与最优控制策略至关重要。

Method: 研究对象为受移动谐振势阱（moving harmonic trap）约束的活性Ornstein-Uhlenbeck粒子（active Ornstein-Uhlenbeck particle）。在初始位置测量之后，解析推导出精确的最优反馈控制协议，分析协议随持久时间（persistence time）的依赖关系，并与基于直接测量活性涨落的反馈方案进行能量学比较。

Result: 推导得到精确最优协议，结果显示位置与活性涨落之间的非平衡关联允许仅基于位置信息即从活性中提取功。活性对输运的影响取决于持久时间：活性可促进或阻碍相对于被动系统的输运。与直接测量活性波动的反馈方案不同，基于位置的反馈即使在无限持续活性极限下仍保持能量优势。

Conclusion: 本文为在活性环境中运行的信息引擎和最优控制策略提供了设计原则，表明位置反馈方案是一种无需直接访问内部活性自由度即可高效提取能量的鲁棒策略。

Abstract: We study the minimum-work feedback control of particles subject to active fluctuations. Considering an active Ornstein-Uhlenbeck particle confined by a moving harmonic trap, we derive exact optimal protocols following an initial position measurement. Our results show that nonequilibrium correlations between position and active fluctuations allow work extraction from the activity based on positional information only, i.e., without directly measuring the active degree of freedom, which was the focus of earlier literature. We find that depending on the persistence time, activity can either facilitate or impede transport relative to passive systems. Surprisingly, unlike feedback schemes based on direct measurements of the active fluctuations, positional feedback remains energetically advantageous even in the limit of infinitely persistent activity. Our results provide design principles for information engines and optimal control strategies operating in active environments.

</details>

### [55] [HiPoly: a hierarchical polymer-native AI framework for property prediction and generative design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02746)
*Ge Sun,Gervasio Zaldivar,Yuan Tian,Gustavo Perez Lemus,Juhae Park,Dasha Safarian,Ming Han,Juan J. de Pablo*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了HiPoly——一种基于G2RINS表示的三级层级图架构（three-level hierarchical graph architecture）的聚合物原生AI框架，它直接编码单体间随机连接性、组成和分子量，实现了从实验配方数据到性质预测、生成式分子设计和物理验证的端到端工作流，并在多组分聚合物体系的热物理性质预测中达到最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物在能源、健康、交通等现代技术中占据核心地位，但其跨越多尺度（multiple length scales）的层次结构使其难以在统一且物理上有意义的方式下被表示；尽管AI在材料发现中已取得显著进展，现有方法仍难以应对聚合物特有的结构复杂性，亟需一种聚合物原生的表征与建模框架。

Method: HiPoly采用建立在G2RINS表示之上的三级层级图架构（three-level hierarchical graph architecture），将随机单体间连接性（stochastic inter-monomer connectivity）、组成（composition）和分子量（molecular weight）直接编码到模型中；框架设计了端到端的AI驱动工作流，从实验配方数据出发，串联性质预测、生成式分子设计（generative molecular design）与基于分子模拟（molecular simulations）的物理验证，全程共享统一的聚合物表示。

Result: 在多组分聚合物体系的热物理性质预测中达到当前最优（state-of-the-art）精度；消融实验（ablation studies）证实各个层级设计选择独立贡献于模型性能；将该框架应用于可持续替代持久性含氟聚合物的生成设计路径中，成功识别出并独立验证了具有目标表面能（surface energy）性质的PFAS-free候选材料。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了聚合物原生AI通过将表示、预测与设计（representation, prediction, and design）在复杂聚合物化学中统一关联，能够有效加速材料发现进程，为聚合物信息学提供了在统一表征框架下贯通多环节的应用范式。

Abstract: Polymeric materials are central to modern technologies, with applications ranging from energy to health and transportation. Although AI has made significant advances in materials discovery, the hierarchical structure of polymers across multiple length scales makes them inherently difficult to represent in a unified and physically meaningful way. Here we introduce HiPoly, a polymer-native AI framework that processes complete polymer descriptions through a three-level hierarchical graph architecture built on the G2RINS representation. HiPoly encodes stochastic inter-monomer connectivity, composition, and molecular weight directly within its architecture, using physically motivated design principles that mirror the multi-scale nature of polymeric systems. The framework establishes an end-to-end AI-driven workflow from experimental formulation data to property prediction, generative molecular design, and physics-based validation through molecular simulations, all unified by a single polymer representation. We demonstrate state-of-the-art prediction accuracy for thermophysical properties of multi-component polymer systems, with ablation studies confirming that each hierarchical design choice contributes independently to model performance. As an example, the generative design pathway is applied here to the discovery of sustainable alternatives to persistent fluorinated polymers, where it is possible to identify and independently validate PFAS-free candidates with target surface-energy properties. This work demonstrates how polymer-native AI can accelerate discovery by linking representation, prediction, and design across complex polymer chemistries.

</details>


### [56] [Low-Temperature Transport in Li-Ion Battery EC/EMC/FEC Electrolytes: Molecular Dynamics and Machine-Learning Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02824)
*İpek Yenda Çınar,Oguzhan Orhan,M. Oluş Özbek,Şener Özönder*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该论文结合经典分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）与机器学习（machine learning, ML），系统研究了含不同比例氟代碳酸乙烯酯（FEC）添加剂的LiPF₆电解液在298–233 K范围内的锂离子输运行为。结果显示低温下含EMC体系电导率下降超过98%，远大于EC-rich体系约90%的降幅；Li⁺自扩散活化能为EMC体系0.49–0.54 eV、EC体系0.27–0.29 eV。高斯过程代理模型对NE和GK电导率实现0.56和0.57 mS cm⁻¹的交叉验证RMSE，并表明温度与主体溶剂组成主导输运响应，FEC仅起次级调节作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温环境严重制约电池电解质中锂离子输运，但溶剂组成与含氟添加剂在低温区的耦合效应尚未被充分阐明，因此有必要对此进行系统性研究。

Method: 作者结合经典分子动力学（classical molecular dynamics, MD）与机器学习方法，模拟1 M LiPF₆电解液体系，涵盖EC、EMC及EC/EMC(3:7)三种溶剂组成，添加剂FEC含量为0–10 mol%，温度范围298–233 K。MD量化Li⁺自扩散、Nernst-Einstein（NE）与Green-Kubo（GK）电导率及局部配位，并以高斯过程（Gaussian process）代理模型建模电导率随组成与温度的变化。

Result: 降温导致显著输运损失：含EMC体系GK电导率在233 K下降超过98%，EC-rich体系约下降90%。Li⁺自扩散活化能对EMC体系为0.49–0.54 eV，EC体系为0.27–0.29 eV。在EC体系中，0–2 mol% FEC低温输运性能相当，而5–10 mol% FEC在最低模拟温度下电导率保持率更低。高斯过程代理模型对NE与GK电导率实现组成分离交叉验证RMSE为0.56和0.57 mS cm⁻¹。

Conclusion: 在模拟的液态轨迹范围内，温度和主体溶剂组成主导电解液的体相输运响应，FEC仅作为次级调节因素发挥有限作用。研究为低温电解液设计中添加剂用量的最优化提供了定量指导。

Abstract: Low-temperature operation imposes severe limitations on lithium-ion transport in battery electrolytes, yet the coupled effects of solvent composition and fluorinated additives in the cold-temperature regime remain insufficiently resolved. Here, we combine classical molecular dynamics (MD) and machine learning (ML) to investigate 1 M LiPF$_6$ electrolytes containing ethylene carbonate (EC), ethyl methyl carbonate (EMC), and EC/EMC (3:7), with 0-10 mol% fluoroethylene carbonate (FEC), from 298 to 233 K. MD simulations quantify Li$^+$ self-diffusion, Nernst-Einstein (NE) and Green-Kubo (GK) conductivities and local coordination, while Gaussian-process surrogates model conductivity across composition and temperature. Cooling produces a pronounced transport penalty, particularly in EMC-containing electrolytes, whose GK conductivity decreases by more than 98% at 233 K, compared with approximately 90% in EC-rich systems. Li$^+$ self-diffusion activation energies are 0.49-0.54 eV for EMC-containing systems and 0.27-0.29 eV for EC-based systems. Within the EC family, 0-2 mol% FEC gives comparable cold-temperature transport, whereas 5-10 mol% FEC shows lower conductivity retention at the coldest simulated temperature. The analyzed coordination channels remain solvent dominated, while direct Li$^+$-FEC coordination is not quantified in the present RDF set.The Gaussian-process surrogates achieve composition-disjoint cross-validated RMSE values of 0.56 and 0.57 mS cm$^{-1}$ for NE and GK conductivity. Within the simulated liquid-state trajectories, temperature and host-solvent composition dominate the bulk-transport response, with FEC acting as a secondary modifier.

</details>

### [57] [Nanohertz Gravitational-Wave Constraints on Supermassive Binary Black Holes at Cosmic Dawn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00613)
*Yiqin Chen,Shi-Yi Zhao,Xingjiang Zhu,N. D. Ramesh Bhat,Jacob Cardinal Tremblay,Małgorzata Curyło,Valentina Di Marco,George Hobbs,Wenhua Ling,Rami F. Mandow,Richard N. Manchester,Saurav Mishra,Daniel J. Reardon,Sparrow Roch,Christopher J. Russell,Ryan M. Shannon,Jingbo Wang,Shuangqiang Wang,Andrew Zic*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑宇宙学红移（cosmological redshift）的脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing array, PTA）引力波搜寻框架，并将其应用于帕克斯PTA第三次数据发布（Data Release 3），首次直接约束了宇宙黎明（cosmic dawn）时期超大质量双黑洞（supermassive binary black holes, SMBBHs）的存在，并在高红移系统中排除啁啾质量约为10¹⁰太阳质量的SMBBHs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准连续引力波搜索忽略了宇宙学红移效应，其适用范围仅限于本地宇宙，无法探测遥远星系中的超大质量双黑洞系统。为解决这一限制，作者引入红移感知的PTA分析框架，以拓宽引力波探测的宇宙学视界。

Method: 作者构建了集成红移效应的PTA搜寻框架，并将其应用于帕克斯PTA数据发布三。通过跨宽广红移区间评估约束上限，并针对高红移系统（如类星体J0100+2802，z=6.327；JWST发现的星系JADES-GS-z14-0，z=14.32）执行红移感知的目标定向搜寻（targeted search）。

Result: 观测上确立了由理论"红移偏差"（redshift bias）驱动的非单调质量-红移排除边界；对高红移系统严格排除了纳赫兹频段内啁啾质量大于约10¹⁰太阳质量的SMBBHs；证明高红移双星系统可被稳健探测与定位，从而精确测量其固有性质。

Conclusion: 研究结果为红移大于6的SMBBHs提供了迄今最严格的约束，确立了探测早期宇宙黑洞并合事件的实用框架，展示了PTA在极远距离引力波源探测方面的潜力。

Abstract: Standard continuous gravitational-wave searches with pulsar timing arrays (PTAs) neglect cosmological redshift, restricting their applicability to the local Universe. We introduce a redshift-aware PTA framework and apply it to the Parkes PTA Data Release 3, deriving the first direct constraints on supermassive binary black holes (SMBBHs) at the cosmic dawn. Evaluating our limits across a broad redshift range, we observationally establish the non-monotonic mass-redshift exclusion boundary driven by the theoretical ``redshift bias", demonstrating how PTAs can effectively probe sources at extreme distances. Redshift-aware targeted searches toward high-redshift systems, including the ultraluminous quasar J0100+2802 ($z=6.327$) and the JWST-discovered galaxy JADES-GS-z14-0 ($z=14.32$), rigorously exclude SMBBHs with chirp masses $\mathcal{M}_c \gtrsim 10^{10} M_\odot$ across the nanohertz frequency band. Finally, we demonstrate that high-redshift binaries can be robustly detected and localized, allowing for the accurate measurement of their intrinsic properties. Our results provide the tightest constraints to date on SMBBHs at $z > 6$ and establish a practical framework for probing early-Universe black hole assembly.

</details>


### [58] [An Exact Engine for Black-Hole Jets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02742)
*Yu Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文将Blandford-Znajek机制从"测试场近似"推进到精确解：通过分裂单极子场搭建时空几何，使喷流自身的引力场参与自引力，从而揭示稳态喷流不可能、通量以电荷身份进入黑洞力学定律、以及喷流寿命输出的三个有限量（上限功率、能量释放比例、视界面积增长）等一系列测试场计算无法看见的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blandford-Znajek机制自提出以来五十年来始终只是一个"机制"而非精确解：力自由电动力学（force-free electrodynamics）所依据的度规是预先给定的，喷流自身的磁场并不参与引力场方程。作者旨在让该场自身参与引力（gravitates），构建一个自洽的自引力系统。

Method: 采用分裂单极子（split monopole）场：该磁场在自引力下与磁单极子同重，其自引力表现为磁性Reissner-Nordström几何；两份几何在赤道电流片处粘合，将黑洞、吸积盘与驱动喷流的磁通量置于单一时空之中，力自由等离子体充填其中形成磁层（magnetosphere）。

Result: 得出三项测试场计算无法获得的结论：(1) 稳态喷流不可能，工作喷流意味着黑洞处于衰减中，其速率由场方程决定而非人为假设；(2) 磁通量以电荷身份进入黑洞力学定律，与自旋共享同一个极端性（extremality）预算，将喷流功率上限封顶为c^5/48G；(3) 最大自旋黑洞以9.2%质量递出能量，视界面积恰好增长√e倍，视界转动惯量以无理指数(√17−1)/2趋零，角动量最终转移至自身场中。

Conclusion: 一个理想化的精确处理表明：黑洞喷流的能量来源本质上是黑洞自身的衰减，而非稳态的电磁提取过程。喷流的总寿命输出有限，且黑洞最终把角动量完全传递给自身产生的场，留下一个角动量已转移的旋转黑洞。

Abstract: For fifty years the Blandford--Znajek mechanism has been a mechanism and not an exact solution: force-free electrodynamics on a prescribed metric, with the jet's own field carrying no weight. Here that field gravitates. A split monopole weighs as much as a magnetic monopole, so its self-gravity is the magnetic Reissner--Nordström geometry; two copies glued across an equatorial current sheet put hole, disk and jet-driving flux into one spacetime, and force-free plasma makes a magnetosphere of it. Three things then follow that no test-field calculation can see. First, a steady jet is impossible. A stationary horizon cannot be heated but a slipping magnetosphere necessarily heats it, so the only stationary state is a dead one corotating with the hole, and a working jet is a black hole in decay at rates the field equations fix rather than assume. Second, flux enters the laws of black-hole mechanics as a charge, sharing one extremality budget with spin. That budget caps the jet power at $c^5/48G$ whatever the mass. Third, the lifetime output is finite: a maximally spinning hole delivers $1-e^{1/4}/\sqrt2=9.2\%$ of its mass and grows its horizon area by exactly $\sqrt e$. The horizon's own moment of inertia vanishes with the irrational exponent $(\sqrt{17}-1)/2$ set by the near-horizon throat, and what is left is a rotating hole whose angular momentum has passed to its own field.

</details>


### [59] [Magnetized accretion onto rapidly spinning binary black holes: mini-disk thermodynamics, magnetic transport, and dual jets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02867)
*Luciano Combi,Manuela Campanelli,Sean M. Ressler,Alexander J. Dittmann,Federico Cattorini*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究通过三维广义相对论磁流体动力学（general relativistic magnetohydrodynamics, GRMHD）数值模拟，研究了吸积双黑洞系统中磁场输运与双喷流产生的物理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淹没在富含气体环境中的超大质量双黑洞是脉冲星计时阵列和未来空间引力波干涉仪的重要多信使源，其电磁辐射受双星周围的非线性等离子体动力学调控。由于活动星系核本质上具有随机性，识别稳健的双星特征需要能够将大尺度环绕双星流与黑洞联系起来的预测模型，而以往相对论模拟大多聚焦于更小间距和更低自旋的情形。

Method: 研究采用三维广义相对论磁流体动力学（GRMHD）数值模拟，设置了一个弛豫的磁化环绕双星盘（circumbinary disk），流向等质量双黑洞系统，双星间距为30个引力半径，无量纲自旋参数χ=0.9。

Result: 在小间距条件下，每个黑洞周围的迷你盘（mini-disk）是持续的质库，但表现出受偏心环绕双星盘及盘间气体的摆动流（sloshing gas）调控的显著振幅调制。磁场从环绕双星盘输运至事件视界，产生能量提取效率约40%的强大双喷流；穿越视界的磁通量与喷流光度在两黑洞间交替变化，形成开-关双喷流状态。宽喷流漏斗在双星上方相互作用形成有利于磁重联的持久电流片。此外，较热的迷你盘质量更小、相干周期性更弱，且在穿视界磁通量相当的情况下产生光度更低的喷流。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了在大间距、高自旋条件下，环绕双星盘向双黑洞输运磁通量并驱动高效双喷流的动力学机制，同时表明迷你盘的热力学状态（冷却效率）对喷流光度和相干周期性具有显著影响。

Abstract: Supermassive binary black holes embedded in gas-rich environments are promising multi-messenger sources for pulsar timing arrays and future space-borne gravitational-wave interferometers. Their electromagnetic emission is governed by nonlinear plasma dynamics around the binary and is expected to inherit variability associated with the orbital motion thereof. Because active galactic nuclei are intrinsically stochastic, identifying robust binary signatures requires predictive models that connect large-scale circumbinary flows to the black holes. Previous relativistic simulations of accreting binaries have mostly focused on smaller separations and lower spins. We perform three-dimensional general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulations of a relaxed, magnetized circumbinary disk accreting onto an equal-mass binary black hole with a separation of $30$ gravitational radii and dimensionless spin $χ=0.9$. At these separations, the mini-disks around each black hole are persistent mass reservoirs but still show pronounced amplitude modulations governed by the eccentric circumbinary disk and the sloshing gas between the mini-disks. We analyze how the magnetic flux is transported from the circumbinary disk to the horizons, launching powerful dual jets with energy extraction efficiencies reaching $\approx 40\%$. The horizon-threading flux and jet luminosity alternate between the two black holes, producing an on-off dual-jet state. The wide jet funnels interact above the binary and form a persistent current sheet favorable to reconnection. We explore the influence of mini-disk thermodynamics, comparing efficient and inefficient cooling inside the cavity. Hotter mini-disks are less massive, exhibit weaker coherent periodicity, and launch less luminous jets despite comparable horizon-threading magnetic flux.

</details>

### [60] [Yielded-region connectivity governs the onset of gravity-driven spreading in elastoviscoplastic drops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01907)
*Alice Woodbridge,Patrick O'Rourke,Cláudio P. Fonte,Anne Juel*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过直接数值模拟研究弹性黏塑性液滴（elastoviscoplastic drop）在重力作用下的铺展起始机制，发现铺展不仅取决于局部屈服，还要求屈服区域连通形成从液滴内部到自由表面的连续流化通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重力驱动的弹性黏塑性液滴铺展的起始物理机制尚不明确，因为屈服、弹性变形和黏性耗散可能同时发生在材料的不同区域，难以将各机制分离研究。

Method: 对放置在同材质薄前驱层上的轴对称大液滴进行直接数值模拟（direct numerical simulations）。液滴先于重力下达到平衡，再施加不同幅值的额外向下恒加速度，流变学采用Saramito–Herschel–Bulkley模型描述；待变形速率可忽略后移除附加加速度，使液滴再次在重力下松弛，从而将全局变形分离为可恢复与不可恢复两部分。

Result: 发现铺展并非仅因材料局部屈服而发生：在低加速度下，屈服区域被限制在以固态类黏弹性基质为主的结构内部，变形大部分可恢复；显著的铺展发生在屈服区域连通形成从液滴内部到自由表面的连续流化通道时。转变所需加速度主要受屈服应力控制，而弹性通过调节屈服区域的范围和空间分布来决定可恢复变形与铺展之间的分配。

Conclusion: 弹性黏塑性液滴的显著铺展取决于屈服区域的空间连通性，而非单纯的局部屈服；屈服应力主导铺展所需的临界条件，弹性则调控变形的可恢复性及铺展程度的分配。

Abstract: The physical mechanisms controlling the onset of gravity-driven spreading of elastoviscoplastic drops remain unclear because yielding, elastic deformation, and viscous dissipation can occur simultaneously in different regions of the material. To isolate these mechanisms, we perform direct numerical simulations of large axisymmetric drops resting on a thin precursor layer of the same material. Each drop is first equilibrated under gravity, $g$, before being subjected to an additional constant downward acceleration of varying magnitude. The drop rheology is described by the Saramito--Herschel--Bulkley model. Once the rate of deformation becomes negligible under the enhanced forcing, the additional acceleration is removed and the drop is allowed to relax again under gravity alone, enabling the global deformation to be separated into recoverable and unrecoverable components. We find that spreading does not occur simply because some portion of the material yields locally. At low imposed accelerations, yielded regions remain confined within a predominantly solid-like viscoelastic matrix, and the resulting deformation is mostly recoverable. Appreciable spreading is observed when these yielded regions connect to form a continuous fluidised pathway from the drop interior to the free surface, allowing radial material transport. The acceleration required for this transition is governed primarily by the yield stress, whereas elasticity controls the partition between recoverable deformation and spreading by regulating the extent and spatial distribution of yielded regions.

</details>

Motivation: 在材料表征中，从科学图像估计物理参数常依赖于昂贵的物理解析模拟；电子显微镜中样品厚度与晶体倾角对电子散射行为至关重要，但传统的网格搜索扩展性差，神经网络方法需要大量预训练且难以迁移到新条件。

Result: 在50次模拟器评估预算下，SBOCF在合成SrTiO3基准上优于标准贝叶斯优化，将厚样品场景的中位最终SSE最大降低290倍；在实验数据上无需任务特定预训练即可得到与已有报道一致的参数估计；在模拟斜倾样品上，SBOCF估计参数用于下游叠层衍射重建（ptychographic reconstruction）可恢复清晰的原子图像。

Conclusion: SBOCF为涉及昂贵模拟器和高维结构化输出的反问题提供了一种有前景的方法，显著提升了参数估计的模拟效率与可迁移性。

Abstract: Estimating physical parameters from scientific images is a common inverse problem in materials characterization that often relies on expensive physics-based simulations. In electron microscopy, specimen thickness and crystal mistilt are critical parameters that govern how electrons scatter through the sample, and therefore the accuracy of any atomic-scale structure recovered from it. They are commonly inferred by matching experimental position-averaged convergent-beam electron diffraction (PACBED) patterns to simulated ones, but grid searches scale poorly and neural-network methods require extensive pretraining that may not transfer to new conditions. Here, we propose scalable Bayesian optimization of composite functions (SBOCF), a simulation-efficient method that exploits the known composite structure of the image-matching objective and the intermediate information contained in simulated images. By representing PACBED images with patch-level summaries and two correction terms, SBOCF preserves the original pixel-wise objective while reducing the number of modeled outputs from 24,649 to 11. Under a budget of 50 simulator evaluations, SBOCF outperformed standard Bayesian optimization with expected improvement on synthetic SrTiO3 benchmarks with thick and thin specimens, reducing the median final SSE by up to 290x in the thick-sample case. On experimental data, SBOCF produced parameter estimates consistent with previously reported values without task-specific pretraining. For a simulated mistilted specimen, using the SBOCF estimates in a downstream ptychographic reconstruction recovered sharp atoms that were otherwise blurred. These results establish SBOCF as a promising approach for inverse problems involving expensive simulators and high-dimensional structured outputs.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将随机梯度流（stochastic gradient flow, SGF）建模为渗流过程，其中架构对称性（architectural symmetries）迫使子网络以离散的同时块（discrete simultaneous blocks）而非逐一方式合并。通过宏观序参量（order parameter）中的方差尖峰来表征这些结构性转变，类比物理相变（phase transitions）。

Result: 架构对称性导致的子网络合并以同步离散块形式发生，表现为宏观序参量中的方差尖峰信号。该捕获机制及其相关的缩放级联（scaling cascade）在显式重尾噪声模型（heavy-tailed noise model）下同样适用于Adam和AdamW优化器。

Conclusion: SGD引导神经网络的动力学可借用渗流与相变的物理框架加以刻画，结构性转变以离散方式同步发生并在序参量中留下可观测信号，且这一机制对包括Adam和AdamW在内的常用优化算法具有普适性。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD), which is known to steer deep neural networks toward invariant sets that correspond to simpler subnetworks. How this steering unfolds over time remains poorly understood. We answer this by modeling the stochastic gradient flow (SGF) as a percolation process, in which architectural symmetries force subnetworks to merge in discrete simultaneous blocks rather than one at a time. These structural transitions register as variance spikes in a macroscopic order parameter, echoing physical phase transitions. We further show this trapping mechanism and its associated scaling cascade extend to Adam and AdamW under an explicit heavy-tailed noise model.

</details>

Method: 作者扩展了微观理论以纳入CoO₆八面体畸变，并使用点电荷计算（point-charge calculations）估算BaCo₂V₂O₈的g张量，同时分析畸变对交错交换作用的重整化贡献及其与Kitaev衍生交错项的相互补偿关系。

Result: 研究表明八面体畸变不仅产生位点依赖的各向异性g张量，还通过畸变诱导的贡献对交错交换作用进行重整化，该贡献部分补偿了源自Kitaev相互作用的交错项；临界场的强各向异性源于修改后的交换作用与各向异性g张量的联合效应。

Abstract: BaCo$_2$V$_2$O$_8$ is a prototypical spin-orbit-coupled Ising-chain antiferromagnet that provides a unique platform for studying field-induced quantum magnetism. Under a transverse magnetic field, it exhibits unusual magnetic properties, including an anomalous staggered magnetization and a pronounced anisotropy of the critical field with respect to the in-plane field direction. While these phenomena have been attributed phenomenologically to a site-dependent anisotropic $g$-tensor, a recent microscopic theory has shown that spin-orbit coupling naturally generates bond-dependent Heisenberg, Kitaev, and $Γ$ exchange interactions. Here, we unify these two pictures by extending the microscopic theory to incorporate distortions of the CoO$_6$ octahedra. We show that the distortions not only generate the site-dependent anisotropic $g$-tensor but also renormalize the staggered exchange interactions through a distortion-induced contribution that partially compensates the Kitaev-derived staggered term. Using point-charge calculations to estimate the $g$-tensor of BaCo$_2$V$_2$O$_8$, we demonstrate that the strong anisotropy of the critical field originates from the combined effects of the modified exchange interactions and the anisotropic $g$-tensor. Our work provides a unified microscopic framework for understanding the magnetic anisotropy of spin-orbit-coupled Ising-chain materials.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁体（altermagnets）作为一类破坏时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）的新型共线磁体，为超越传统磁体的自旋电子学（spintronics）提供了新机遇，但其多极序的直接实验证据仍缺失，需要实空间可视化手段加以证实。

Method: 结合高能同步辐射X射线衍射与价电子密度（valence electron density, VED）分析，直接可视化交替磁体CrSb中价电子密度分布在Cr亚晶格之间的显著方向各向异性；同时利用极化中子衍射探测反铁磁磁偶极子与铁磁磁多极子的共存关系，并据此构建微观模型。

Result: 研究揭示了在g波交替磁体中预示的电子十六极子（electric hexadecapoles）的反铁有序；该有序与反铁磁磁偶极子共存并诱导铁磁磁多极子，且g波多极子与g波自旋劈裂存在直接关联，从而实现了对多极子的直接观测与定量测量。

Conclusion: 该研究为交替磁性提供了实空间指纹，并确立了探测量子材料中隐藏多极序的通用实验探针，加深了人们对交替磁体中非常规时间反演对称性破缺起源的理解。

Abstract: Over the past few years, altermagnets have emerged as a new class of collinear magnets with broken time-reversal symmetry, offering novel opportunities for spintronics beyond conventional magnets. Rather than from net magnetization, as in ferromagnets, the unconventional time-reversal symmetry breaking of altermagnets originates from antiferroic magnetic dipoles locked to higher-order multipoles. Here we report the direct visualization of a $g$-wave altermagnetic multipole in the canonical altermagnet CrSb. Combining high-energy synchrotron X-ray diffraction with valence electron density (VED) analysis, we uncover a pronounced directional anisotropy of the VED distribution alternating between Cr sublattices. This evidences the antiferroic order of electric hexadecapoles predicted in $g$-wave altermagnets. Its coexistence with antiferroic magnetic dipoles induces ferroic magnetic multipoles, as probed by polarized neutron diffraction. We further identify a microscopic model of altermagnetism that directly relates the $g$-wave multipole and the $g$-wave spin splitting. Through direct observation and quantification of multipoles, this study provides a real-space fingerprint of altermagnetism and establishes a general probe of hidden multipole order in quantum materials.

Method: 作者通过将空间变化的电场耦合到相对论性自旋诱导电极化，构造了热力学形式的电环形偶极子；所得表达式具有规范不变性（gauge invariant）。随后建立了ETD对化学势的导数与绝缘体系统中本征纵向自旋电导率的直接关系，并构建了Kane-Mele模型的最简铁轴对称扩展模型加以验证。

Abstract: Electric toroidal dipoles (ETDs) characterize ferroaxial order, yet their bulk definition in periodic crystals has remained elusive because conventional multipole operators involve the ill-defined position operator. The resulting expression is gauge invariant and provides a bulk order parameter for ferroaxial phases. We further establish a direct relation between the chemical-potential derivative of the ETD and the intrinsic longitudinal spin conductivity in insulating systems. To demonstrate the formulation, we construct a minimal ferroaxial extension of the Kane--Mele model. The ETD becomes finite exclusively in the ferroaxial phase and is strongly enhanced near a small band gap, accompanied by a sizable longitudinal spin current. Conclusion: 该工作建立了反位无序、竞争性交换相互作用与玻璃态磁性之间的直接关联。结合实验与理论结果揭示，无序程度的差异驱动体系从FeRuMnSi中反铁磁主导（AFM-dominated）态转变为FeRuMnGe中铁磁主导（FM-dominated）态，为理解无序程度对四元Heusler合金磁性性质的调控作用提供了更深层次的见解。

Abstract: In this combined experimental and theoretical study, we investigate the role of disorder in governing the magnetic ground state of the quaternary Heusler alloy FeRuMnGe. In the FeRuMnZ (Z = Ga, Si) series, chemical substitution modifies atomic ordering and electronic structure, resulting in distinct magnetic ground states. Motivated by this, we extend the series to FeRuMnGe. X-ray diffraction reveals B2-type antisite disorder, where Fe--Ru and Mn--Ge intermix. Theoretical calculations show that such disorder modifies exchange interactions, leading to competing ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic couplings, and drives the system from half-metallic to metallic. Magnetic measurements reveal competing interactions, giving rise to a cluster-glass state coexisting with long-range magnetic order. The absence of a thermodynamic signature at $T_f$, together with ac susceptibility and relaxation measurements, supports the presence of short-range magnetic interactions and a glassy magnetic state. The compound exhibits an enhanced magnetic response below $\sim 161$ K and a maximum magnetization of $\sim 1.64~μ_B$/f.u. at 5 T. Overall, this work establishes a direct correlation between antisite disorder, competing exchange interactions, and glassy magnetism in quaternary Heusler alloys. Combined experimental results and theoretical calculations reveal that disorder drives the system from an AFM-dominated state in FeRuMnSi to an FM-dominated state in FeRuMnGe, providing deeper insight into the role of the extent of disorder in governing the magnetic properties of QHAs.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出使用两个分离快度窗口之间净重子数涨落的关联。在正则系综独立源框架下，证明守恒诱导的背景项与临界信号在领头阶（leading order）可加性成立，从而可估计并扣除守恒主导项，得到产额标度的关联子。

Result: 守恒效应产生负的跨窗口关联，而共同的长波长临界涨落产生正的关联。产额标度的关联子沿冻结路径接近假想临界端点时，表现出伊辛映射（Ising-mapped）平衡关联长度的非单调增强，而更宽的快度窗口通过热模糊等效应当使响应减弱。

Conclusion: 跨快度累积量提供了一种快度微分类策略，用于减少守恒背景并增强基于涨落搜索QCD临界性的灵敏度，适用于束流能量扫描实验。

Abstract: Fluctuations of conserved charges are a primary tool in the search for the QCD critical endpoint, but their beam-energy dependence is complicated by global baryon-number conservation, whose influence changes as the experimental acceptance covers different fractions of the collision system. We propose using correlations of net-baryon fluctuations between two separated rapidity windows to exploit the distinct signatures of conservation and critical dynamics. Global conservation produces a negative cross-window correlation, whereas a common long-wavelength critical fluctuation produces a positive one. We show that, within a canonical independent-source framework, the conservation-induced background and the critical signal enter additively at leading order, allowing the leading conservation term to be estimated and subtracted. The resulting yield-scaled correlator follows the nonmonotonic enhancement of an Ising-mapped equilibrium correlation length along a freeze-out trajectory passing near a hypothetical critical endpoint, while wider rapidity windows reduce the response through thermal smearing. Cross-rapidity cumulants therefore provide a rapidity-differential strategy for reducing the leading conservation background and sharpening fluctuation-based searches for QCD criticality in beam-energy-scan experiments.

</details>


### [69] [Spin transport in intermediate-energy heavy-ion collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01994)
*Jun Xu,Bao-An Li*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文是一篇小型综述（mini-review），报告了通过测试粒子方法求解自旋和同位旋依赖的玻尔兹曼-乌林-乌伦贝克（spin- and isospin-dependent Boltzmann-Uehling-Uhlenbeck, SIBUU）输运方程，对中能重离子碰撞中自旋输运现象的研究现状。文中概述了物理基础和数值技术，总结了主要结果，并指出了进一步研究的挑战与新物理方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在对中能重离子碰撞中的自旋输运研究提供阶段性总结，梳理SIBUU框架下的物理基础与技术路线，并推动该方向在理论和实验上的进一步探索。研究动机源于核自旋-轨道势（nuclear spin-orbit potential）在致密非对称核物质中可能蕴含的新物理。

Method: 采用测试粒子方法（test-particle method）数值求解自旋和同位旋依赖的玻尔兹曼-乌林-乌伦贝克输运方程（SIBUU），以模拟中能重离子碰撞中的自旋输运过程。文章总结了该框架的物理基础与数值技术实施途径。

Result: 展示了核子的整体与局域自旋极化（global and local spin polarizations）、核子集体流的自旋劈裂（spin splitting of collective flows）以及不同自旋态轻簇流（flows of light clusters）等可观测量，可用于探测致密中子丰富介质中核自旋-轨道势相关的新物理。此外，自旋-轨道势与严格的角动量守恒（angular momentum conservation）也会影响自旋平均的可观测量。

Conclusion: 研究工作识别了在SIBUU框架内进一步研究自旋输运的关键挑战，并希望这些新发现能激励理论界和实验界在中能重离子碰撞中继续深入开展自旋输运研究。

Abstract: In this mini-review, we provide a brief status report on investigating spin transport phenomena in intermediate-energy heavy-ion collisions by simulating solutions of the spin- and isospin-dependent Boltzmann-Uehling-Uhlenbeck (SIBUU) transport equation using the test-particle method. We outline the physics foundation and technical approach, summarize our main results and identify key challenges in further studying spin transport within the SIBUU framework. As examples, we show how the global and local spin polarizations of nucleons, the spin splitting of nucleon collective flows as well as flows of light clusters at different spin states can be used to explore interesting new physics associated with the nuclear spin-orbit potential in dense neutron-rich medium. The spin-orbit potential and the rigorous angular momentum conservation are also shown to affect spin-averaged observables. Hopefully the new findings and challenges identified here will stimulate further studies both theoretically and experimentally on spin transport in intermediate-energy heavy-ion collisions.

</details>

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文在成对空间（pair space）中给出了中心构型（central configurations）方程的变分推导，并对约束Hessian矩阵进行了谱分解，建立了Morse指标不超过2的新证明，同时发展了适用于反射对称构型的广义特征值约化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心构型方程的传统表述通常以体坐标为主变量，导致约束条件复杂；作者希望改用在成对空间中利用相对位置矢量作为主变量，借助变分方法和线性三角形约束关系，获得对约束Hessian矩阵结构的更清晰刻画，并以此推导Morse指标上界。

Method: 首先在成对空间中给出中心构型方程的变分推导，引入矢量拉格朗日乘子（vector Lagrange multipliers）强制实现线性三角关系。随后将约束Hessian分解为半正定的间隙拉普拉斯算子（gap Laplacian L^Δ）与包含所有负方向的带符号横向拉普拉斯算子（transverse Laplacian ÕL）之和；通过一个显式2×2矩阵（或等价地，对称矩阵束的两个广义特征值）刻画正定性与退化性。对反射对称构型，利用反射约化将广义特征值问题分解为两个独立子问题，并针对菱形族验证了其应用。

Result: 该分解与非共线平面四体中心构型中带符号部分秩为2这一事实结合，给出了Morse指标至多为2的已有结论的新证明。对于反射对称构型（如风筝形、等腰梯形和菱形），广义特征值问题可分解为更小的独立子问题；梯形情形中反射在成对坐标上的作用非正交，因此得到了不同的非正交约化约化。对菱形族应用约化后证明了Hessian在模去旋转零模意义下正定，即每个菱形中心构型在模旋转意义下非退化且Morse指标为零。

Conclusion: 该工作为N体中心构型的二阶变分分析提供了一个系统的框架，将稳定性判定归结为低维矩阵问题，并通过反射对称性进一步降低维度，同时为已有Morse指标上界给出了新的证明路径。

Abstract: We give a variational derivation of the central configuration equations in pair space, where the relative position vectors between pairs of bodies serve as the primary variables. Vector Lagrange multipliers enforce the linear triangle relations required for these pair vectors to be realizable in the plane.
  For arbitrary $N$, we show that the constrained Hessian decomposes as $H_{\mathcal C}=L^Δ+\widetilde L$, where the gap Laplacian $L^Δ$ is positive semidefinite and the transverse Laplacian $\widetilde L$ is signed and contains all possible negative directions. For non-collinear planar four-body central configurations, the signed part has rank two. This yields a new proof of the known bound that the Morse index is at most two. We then characterize positive definiteness and degeneracy of the Hessian by the eigenvalues of an explicit $2\times2$ matrix or, equivalently, by two generalized eigenvalues of a pencil of symmetric matrices. We illustrate the general criterion using the equal-mass square central configuration.
  For reflection-symmetric configurations, the generalized eigenvalue problem decomposes into two independent smaller problems. This reduction applies to kite and isosceles trapezoidal configurations. For the trapezoid, however, the reflection acts non-orthogonally on the pair coordinates, giving a different, non-orthogonal reduction. Applying the reflection-symmetry reduction to the rhombus family, we prove that the Hessian is positive definite modulo the rotational zero mode; equivalently, every rhombus central configuration is nondegenerate modulo rotations and has Morse index zero.

</details>

Conclusion: 工作表明CNKG方程具有完整的可积结构，逆散射方法能够系统性地给出其精确N孤子解，为该耦合非线性方程的理论分析提供了数学框架。

Abstract: The exact N-soliton solutions are derived for the coupled negative first order Klein-Gordon (CNKG) equation subject to vanishing boundary conditions by using the inverse scattering method via Gelfand-Levitan-Marchenko equation. Based on the zero-curvature representation, the conservation laws, integrals of motion, and Hamiltonian structure of the aforementioned coupled nonlinear equations are constructed. The Jost functions and their analyticity properties for the Manakov spectral problem are recalled. The integral equations for the eigenfunctions are then used to formulate the Gel'fand-Levitan-Marchenko equations. Solving these equations establishes a direct correspondence between the kernel functions and the potential, yielding the general N-soliton expressions.

</details>

### [72] [Calculating the squeezed bispectrum in stochastic inflation by Monte Carlo simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02076)
*Koichi Miyamoto,Yuichiro Tada*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文基于随机δN形式论（stochastic-δN formalism）提出了一个蒙特卡洛方法来计算挤压极限（squeezed limit）下的宇宙学扰动双谱（bispectrum），该方法通过生成仅分支数次的空间场时间演化路径来避免嵌套路径生成带来的过高计算成本，并首次应用于温和级联阶段（mild waterfall）的混合暴胀（hybrid inflation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学扰动的双谱是探测暴胀（inflation）及潜在粒子物理论的重要工具。然而，当暴胀子动力学由量子扩散（quantum diffusion）主导而非慢滚（slow-roll）时，尤其在多场情况下，双谱的计算变得困难，亟需新的计算方法。

Method: 作者采用随机δN形式论（stochastic-δN formalism），提出一种基于蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）的算法来计算挤压极限（squeezed limit）下的双谱，作为此前提出的功率谱（power spectrum）方法的扩展。算法核心是仅生成分支次数有限的暴胀子时间演化路径，以避免嵌套路径生成所导致的难以承受的计算成本。

Result: 数值演示方面，方法在单场和双场混沌暴胀（chaotic inflation）上进行了验证；同时应用于具有温和级联阶段（mild waterfall）的混合暴胀（hybrid inflation），后者据作者所知是双谱在该模型上的首次计算。

Conclusion: 所提出的蒙特卡洛方法能够在量子扩散主导的暴胀模型（包括多场情形）中高效计算挤压极限双谱，并首次获得了温和级联混合暴胀的双谱结果，为该类模型的宇宙学观测检验提供了新工具。

Abstract: The bispectrum of cosmological perturbations is an important probe of inflation and the underlying particle physics. However, calculating it becomes challenging in inflation models where inflaton dynamics is dominated by quantum diffusion rather than slow-roll, especially in the multi-field case. In this paper, employing the stochastic-$δ\mathcal{N}$ formalism, we propose a Monte Carlo-based method to calculate the bispectrum in the squeezed limit, as an extension of the method for the power spectrum that we previously proposed. Our method involves generating paths of inflatons' time evolution that branch only several times, avoiding nested path generation, which incurs a prohibitive computational cost. As numerical demonstrations, we apply the proposed method to single- and double-field chaotic inflation for validation, and to hybrid inflation with mild waterfall, for which, to the best of our knowledge, the bispectrum is calculated for the first time in this work.

</details>


### [73] [Inflationary Magnetogenesis with f(R) Gravity: Dynamics, Constraints, and Observational Signatures during Reheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01977)
*Shuang Liu,Bo-yu Zhao,Yu Li,Yao-chuan Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文将Ratra模型扩展至非最小f(R)引力框架，通过在拉格朗日量中引入$f^2(η)F_{μν}F^{μν}$项打破电磁作用量的共形不变性，针对暴胀后再加热时期推导出磁场与电场能量密度谱的解析表达式，并结合理论约束（强耦合条件、反作用约束、CMB各向同性要求）与观测约束（射电观测和Fermi-LAT伽马射线数据）对模型参数空间进行联合限制，为暴胀磁生成提供了自洽的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暴胀磁生成（inflationary magnetogenesis）被认为是产生原初大尺度磁场的有前景机制，但面临理论上的挑战，需要突破标准电磁作用的共形不变性。

Method: 将Ratra模型推广至非最小f(R)引力框架，通过构建标度因子与耦合函数在暴胀到再加热过渡期的分段幂律（broken-power-law）演化，解析推导磁场和电场能量密度谱；施加三个关键理论约束后，结合磁流体力学（MHD）湍流非线性效应（含逆传递过程）进行数值计算。

Result: 在考虑MHD湍流后，相干长度可增强至约0.1 Mpc量级，而场强降低若干个数量级；通过与射电观测和Fermi-LAT伽马射线数据对比，发现暴胀能标$H_f$、再加热温度$T_r$、参数$β$及e-fold数$N_f$、$N_r$必须满足严格的联合约束。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个同时满足理论自洽条件与当前观测界限的暴胀磁生成可行理论框架。

Abstract: Inflationary magnetogenesis provides a promising mechanism for generating primordial large-scale magnetic fields, but faces challenges. In this paper, we extend the Ratra model to a non-minimal $f(R)$ gravity framework, appearing in the Lagrangian in the form $f^2(η) F_{μν}F^{μν}$, to break the conformal invariance of the standard electromagnetic action. Focusing on the post-inflationary reheating epoch, we derive analytic expressions for the magnetic and electric energy density spectra by constructing a broken-power-law evolution for the scale factor and the coupling function across the inflation-to-reheating transition. Three key theoretical constraints are imposed on the model parameter space: the strong coupling condition, the backreaction constraint, and the CMB isotropy requirement. Through numerical calculations, we obtain predictions for the present-day magnetic field strength $B_0$ and coherence length $L_{c0}$ for various combinations of the inflationary energy scale $H_f$ and the reheating temperature $T_r$. We further incorporate the nonlinear effects of magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) turbulence after reheating, including the inverse transfer process, which significantly enhances the coherence length (up to $0.1$ Mpc) while reducing the field strength by several orders of magnitude. By comparing with observational constraints from radio observations and Fermi-LAT gamma-ray data, we demonstrate that the inflationary energy scale $H_f$, the reheating temperature $T_r$, the parameter $β$, and the e-folds numbers $N_f$, $N_r$ must satisfy stringent joint constraints. This work provides a viable theoretical framework for inflationary magnetogenesis that simultaneously satisfies theoretical consistency conditions and current observational bounds.

</details>

### [74] [Kelvin-Helmholtz instability in annular superfluids across the UFG-BCS crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01831)
*Michał Śliwiński,Klejdja Xhani,Gabriel Wlazłowski*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本研究利用含时超流密度泛函理论（time-dependent superfluid density functional theory）研究了反向旋转环状费米超流体的开尔文-亥姆霍兹不稳定性（Kelvin-Helmholtz instability）。研究发现，在幺正气体和中等BCS区域，不稳定增长速率与点涡模型定性一致；但在更深的BCS区域，边界产生的反涡增殖速度快于开尔文-亥姆霍兹不稳定性本身，定性上改变了动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米超流体中开尔文-亥姆霍兹不稳定性存在实验与理论之间的偏差（experiment-theory discrepancy），需要微观层面的基准研究来理解其动力学机制并约束可能的理论解释。

Method: 采用含时超流密度泛函理论（time-dependent superfluid density functional theory）模拟两组初始分离的超流合并过程；提取模分辨的不稳定增长速率（mode-resolved instability growth rates），系统研究其对相对流速、相互作用强度和温度的依赖关系，覆盖强相互作用的幺正区域（unitary regime）和弱吸引的BCS区域。
