In the unitary gas and moderate BCS regimes, the growth rates remain qualitatively consistent with the point-vortex model, though systematically at or above its predictions, with no significant dependence on interaction strength or temperature up to approximately $0.3 T_c$. Deeper in the BCS regime, the dynamics change qualitatively: boundary-generated antivortices proliferate faster than the Kelvin-Helmholtz instability itself, destroying the initial vortex necklace before the instability can fully develop. Abstract: Hyperfine constant $A$ is a key parameter of the hyperfine structure and underpins precision spectroscopy and metrology. We measure the transition frequency between these two magnetically insensitive transitions with Hz-level resolution over a range of magnetic fields, and obtain the ground-state hyperfine constant $A = -h\times 285.730536(2)\,\mathrm{MHz}$, corresponding to an absolute uncertainty of about $2\,\mathrm{Hz}$. We measure direct experimental signatures of topology affecting quantum hydrodynamics, unveiling the inherent competition between attractive nonlinearities, that lead to the collapse, and the preservation of topological charge from a multi-charged vortex.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: This paper studies the category $\mathcal{C}$ of grading-restricted $C_1$-cofinite generalized modules for the vertex operator algebra associated to the $W$-algebra $W(2,2)$. We first show that $\mathcal{C}$ is the same as the category of finite length modules whose simple composition factors are the irreducible highest weight $W(2,2)$--modules $L[r]$ of highest weight $(\frac{1-r}{2}, 0)$ for $r \in \mathbb{Z}_{> 0}$, and hence $\mathcal{C}$ carries a braided tensor category structure. Then we prove the fusion rules for the simple objects $L[r]$ are governed by the $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ Clebsch--Gordan rule. In particular, we prove \[ L[r]\boxtimes L[s]\cong \bigoplus_{i=0}^{\min\{r,s\}-1} L[r+s-1-2i]. \] Using the fusion rules and a recent result of Etingof--Penneys, we establish the rigidity of $\mathcal{C}$. We also show the semisimple subcategory generated by the simple objects $L[r]$ is tensor equivalent to the category Rep $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ of finite dimensional $\mathfrak{sl}_2$-modules.

</details>

Abstract: We comment on the recent work [Eur. Phys. J. C 86, 988 (2026)], which investigates quantum resources in polarized hyperon--antihyperon systems using correlated dephasing channels with memory. Although the use of experimentally reconstructed spin-density matrices to characterize quantum correlations in hyperon production may be a good motivation, we argue that the interpretation of the hyperon--antihyperon pair as an open quantum system undergoing correlated environmental decoherence is not physically established. In particular, no physical environment or system--environment interaction responsible for the assumed correlated dephasing channel is identified, and hadronization cannot simply be interpreted as such an environmental dephasing process. Consequently, the claimed non-Markovianity, information backflow, and memory-assisted protection of quantum resources should be regarded as properties of a phenomenological channel rather than established physical effects in hyperon--antihyperon production. We also identify several technical and conceptual issues concerning the dephasing dynamics, entanglement quantification, basis dependence of coherence, and the claimed hierarchy of quantum resources. These issues call for a substantial revision of the physical interpretation and quantitative conclusions of the work. We hope that the present Comment will help clarify the distinction between phenomenological quantum-channel modeling and physically established dynamical mechanisms in high-energy particle systems, and thereby provide useful guidance for future studies and help avoid similar conceptual and technical issues.

</details>

*Fahimeh Bayeh,Andre Kornell*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息可局域化（localization）的观念在物理学中广泛存在——热力学中熵在系统间流动、量子引力中区域熵有界、量子信息论中信息可传输且纠缠能带来优势。经典情形下香农熵（Shannon entropy）可通过边缘化定义外测度来量化可局域化信息，而标准冯·诺依曼熵（von Neumann entropy）不具备这一性质，因此需要一个适当的替代熵来度量可局域化的量子信息。

Result: 证明了修正的冯·诺依曼熵是所有能量化可局域化信息的量子熵中的最小值；同时证明了它是唯一能够以冗余信息方式表征贝尔态并直接推广经典情形的量子熵。

Conclusion: 修正冯·诺依曼熵为量子可局域化信息提供了一种适切且具有最优性与唯一性的度量，为量子信息论与非交换几何之间建立了联系。

Abstract: The idea that information can be localized is pervasive in physics. In thermodynamics, entropy flows from one reservoir to another, and in quantum gravity, the entropy content of some spatial regions is bounded. In quantum information theory, information is transmitted from one place to another, and entanglement can provide an advantage when there are constraints on such transmission.
  Shannon entropy quantifies localizable information in the sense that marginalization defines an outer measure on the set of parts of a classical multipartite system. In contrast, von Neumann entropy does not quantify localizable information in this sense. However, a variant of von Neumann entropy, which originates in noncommutative geometry, does.
  We prove that this adjusted von Neumann entropy is the minimum quantum entropy that quantifies localizable information. We also prove that this is the unique quantum entropy that characterizes Bell states in terms of redundant information, directly generalizing the classical case. We work with finite-dimensional $C^*$-algebras throughout, modeling finite quantum systems that may have superselection sectors.

</details>

Motivation: 量子行走在半直线硬壁上表现出弹道式的传播，但初始态空间受限时，能多接近波前是一个值得探究的问题。

Method: 将优化问题转化为一个 M×M 厄米矩阵（Hermitian matrix）的主特征值问题求解；对大 M 情形，通过近前沿标度极限（near-front scaling limit）获得连续统描述。

Result: 最大平均漂移（maximal mean drift）以 inverse-square 形式逼近波前，缺失项为 M⁻² 量级，且常数前因子为 π²/4；数值结果验证了有限 M 谱公式与标度预测。

Conclusion: 证明了在有限支撑初始态约束下，最优逼近量子行走波前的问题可以解析求解，并给出了清晰的标度律行为。

Abstract: Continuous-time quantum walks on a lattice spread ballistically and converge to a limiting distribution for the rescaled position. On the hard-wall half line the boundary reflects the walker but does not change the bulk dispersion, so the ballistic front remains set by the maximal group velocity. We ask how close one can get to this front when the initial state is constrained to occupy only the first $M$ sites. We show that optimizing the moment-generating function of the limiting-velocity distribution over this finite-support class reduces to the principal eigenvalue of an explicit $M\times M$ Hermitian matrix, which yields the optimal state by direct diagonalization. For large $M$, a near-front scaling limit produces a continuum description that controls the entire peak region. In particular, the maximal mean drift approaches the front with an inverse-square deficit in $M$ and a constant prefactor $π^2/4$. Numerical results verify both the finite-$M$ spectral formulation and the predicted scaling.

### [83] [A generalized harmonic oscillator problem for a spin-1/2 fermion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02043)

Conclusion: 该广义谐振子模型为研究Dirac方程中多种耦合同时作用下的束缚态问题提供了精确解析基础，其结论统一了此前若干独立研究成果，可作为更复杂相对论性量子力学问题的可解参照模型。

Abstract: The exact bound-state wavefunctions and the corresponding energy equation are calculated for a new generalized harmonic oscillator problem describing a spin-1/2 fermion in 3+1-dimensions, involving scalar, vector, and tensor couplings acting simultaneously within a particular plane of motion. For the scalar and vector coupling, singular harmonic oscillator shapes are considered, such that the singular term is needed to allow analytical solutions for the wavefunctions while preserving binding under adequate conditions. For the tensor sector,the Dirac oscillator potential is employed, which adds another independent binding mechanism to the problem. The exact bound-state solutions are computed by specifically tuning coefficients for an appropriate pair of \textit{Ansätze} for the radial functions, which leads to wavefunctions in terms of generalized Laguerre polynomials. Although the energy equation cannot provide a general expression for the energy spectrum, specific constraints on the quantum numbers as simple functions of the external potential parameters can be derived for it, which determines the conditions for bound solutions to exist, and of what type: particle, antiparticle or both. It is shown that the results can be simply mapped to the spherically symmetric analogue problem, and this is used to show that the general result encompass several previous particular cases of spherically symmetric harmonic oscillator problems in the Dirac equation available in the literature.

### [84] [$\mathcal{N}$-bein formalism for degenerate states in the parameter space of quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01752)

TL;DR: 本文扩展了作者先前引入的$N$维标架（$\mathcal{N}$-bein）几何框架，将其应用于具有简并能谱（degenerate spectra）的量子系统参数空间分析，定义了一种非阿贝尔双态量子几何张量（non-Abelian two-state QGT）以及基于Wilczek-Zee联络的类挠率张量，并以耦合谐振子系统为例验证了新方法的实用性。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 建立了$\mathcal{N}$-bein框架在简并能谱系统中的完整推广，包括新的张量定义、微分形式表述和规范不变量的构建，并证明类挠率张量等价于双态QGT的反对称部分。将该框架应用于置于电场中的耦合谐振子系统，该系统因振荡器间的耦合而呈现简并性，新的不变量揭示出量子态之间的关联。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了$\mathcal{N}$-bein几何框架可推广至包含简并能谱的量子系统，所定义的新张量（如类挠率张量）和规范不变量为理解量子系统参数空间的内蕴结构提供了新工具，并通过耦合谐振子实例验证了该形式体系的理论价值与实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Recently, we introduced a geometric object analogous to an orthonormal frame in the Cartan formalism to study the parameter space of quantum systems; we called it $\mathcal{N}$-bein, with $\mathcal{N}$ being the number of parameters that characterize the quantum system. Acting as the ``square root'' of the quantum geometric tensor (QGT), the $\mathcal{N}$-bein allows us to define new tensors to improve our understanding of the structure beneath the parameter space of quantum mechanics. In this work, we extend this mathematical framework surrounding the $\mathcal{N}$-bein to analyze the parameter space of quantum systems with degenerate spectra. As in the non-degenerate case, we define a non-Abelian two-state QGT to identify possible transitions between degenerate states after two consecutive parameter variations. Additionally, using the Wilczek-Zee connection, we introduce a torsion-like tensor as the covariant derivative of the $\mathcal{N}$-bein. This torsion captures the noncommutativity of successive parameter variations and coincides with the antisymmetric part of the two-state QGT. We also present a geometrical formulation using differential forms and discuss the physical implications of the newly defined tensors. Furthermore, we construct several gauge-invariant observables from the $\mathcal{N}$-bein and its derivatives to highlight the utility of the new tensors. Finally, to illustrate the convenience and applications of this formalism, we apply the theoretical framework to a system of coupled harmonic oscillators immersed in an electric field. The coupling between the oscillators results in a degenerate system. Thus, using the new formalism, we found correlations among the quantum states quantified by the new invariants.

</details>


### [85] [Numerical simulation of D-Wave's quantum advantage experiment with time-dependent variational Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01719)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: King等人的近期工作认为，量子退火实验中阻挫横场伊辛模型（frustrated transverse-field Ising model）的经典模拟将需要指数级计算资源。本研究的动机在于检验这一论断，探索经典变分方法在多大程度上能够模拟此类大规模量子动力学过程。

Method: 采用时间依赖变分蒙特卡洛（t-VMC）方法，使用针对自旋玻璃动力学定制的关联子态作为变分波函数，数值模拟D-Wave自旋玻璃退火协议。针对数值上暴露的主要失败模式——马尔可夫链混合不充分（poor Markov-chain mixing）、高方差的局域能量估计器（high-variance local-energy estimators）和随机龙格-库塔（stochastic Runge-Kutta）误差估计，分别采用并行回火（parallel tempering）、模糊采样（blurred sampling）和重点加权的微分方程求解器进行修复。

Result: 在7ns和20ns的退火时间下，对于二维圆柱、三维二聚体、菱形和双团（biclique）等实例，系统增大变分拟设规模可使t-VMC逼近量子处理单元的两自旋关联误差；在一个极具挑战性的双团实例上完成了精确的大规模模拟，结果与量子退火器高度一致，而该实例此前没有其他变分方法能够处理。

Conclusion: 本研究扩展了经典模拟的前沿边界，同时对该尺度下模拟量子动力学的计算成本提供了现实评估。结果表明，通过适当的数值改进，经典变分方法能够模拟此前被认为需要指数级资源的量子退火实验，对King等人关于经典模拟困难性的论断构成直接挑战。

Abstract: Programmable quantum annealers can realize real-time dynamics of frustrated transverse-field Ising models on large, nontrivial graphs. Recent work by King et al. argued that the classical simulation of such experiments would require exponential computational resources for classical methods such as tensor networks and neural quantum states. Here, we numerically simulate the D-Wave spin-glass annealing protocol with time-dependent variational Monte Carlo (t-VMC) using a correlator state tailored to spin-glass dynamics. For the two-dimensional cylinder, three-dimensional dimer, diamond, and biclique instances considered, at annealing times of 7 and 20 ns, we show that systematically increasing the variational ansatz size enables t-VMC to approximate the final two-spin correlation errors of the quantum processing unit (QPU). We also perform an accurate large-scale simulation of a challenging biclique instance for which no other variational method is known to work, and we find close agreement with the quantum annealer. Through ablation studies, we identify poor Markov-chain mixing, high-variance local-energy estimators, and stochastic Runge-Kutta error estimates as the principal numerical failure modes. We address these numerical issues by using parallel tempering, blurred sampling and an importance-weighted differential equation solver, thereby clarifying the numerical requirements for stable, large-scale t-VMC simulations. Our results extend the frontier of classical simulation while providing a realistic assessment of the computational costs of simulating quantum dynamics at this scale.

</details>


### [86] [Bilinear correlations in Fluctuating Gaussian States with anti-unitary symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01724)
*Xu Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一类具有反幺正（anti-unitary）对称性、无符号问题（sign problem-free）的一般系统——涨落高斯态（Fluctuating Gaussian States, FGS），通过蒙特卡洛模拟论证此类系统普遍存在两种对称破缺相：鞍点相（saddle point phase）与非局域收缩相（non-local contraction phase），并讨论其与费米子PEPS描述及非平衡系统的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对一类由正定权重的FGS描述的物理系统，作者希望理解其双线性序参量（bilinear order-parameter）关联的一般行为，探究这类系统的对称破缺相结构及其与张量网络描述之间的对应关系。

Method: 作者在带反幺正对称性、权重正定的FGS一般框架下研究双线性序参量关联，并将研究建立在涨落高斯费米子与玻色子系统的蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟之上，以此支撑关于对称破缺相类型的普遍性论断。

Result: 论证此类系统一般呈现两类对称破缺相：鞍点相与非局域收缩相，二者竞争由FGS涨落鞍点的稳定性决定。由于费米子FGS中的费米液体不稳定性（Fermi liquid instability），作者提出可用特定投影像纠缠对态（Projected Entangled-Pair State, PEPS）来高效描述鞍点相，但无法有效描述非局域收缩相。

Conclusion: 本文结果可能适用于非平衡系统，并对稳定目标双线性序（stabilizing a target bilinear order）具有潜在意义。

Abstract: We study bilinear order-parameter correlations in a general class of sign problem-free systems that are described by what we call Fluctuating Gaussian States (FGS) with anti-unitary symmetries, where the weight of each Gaussian measurement in the space-time path integral is positive-definite. Supported by Monte Carlo simulations on fluctuating Gaussian fermionic and bosonic examples, we argue that such systems generally exhibit two types of broken-symmetry phases: a \emph{saddle point phase} and a \emph{non-local contraction phase}, with the competition between the two determined by the stability of the fluctuation saddle point of FGS. Due to the Fermi liquid instability in fermionic FGS with anti-unitary symmetries, we also discuss the possibility of representing fermionic FGS with a particular Projected Entangled-Pair State (PEPS) ansatz, which we argue a certain construction provides an efficient description for the saddle point phase, but not for the non-local contraction phase. Finally, we discuss the potential implication of our result in non-equilibrium systems and stabilizing a target bilinear order.

</details>

Result: 在Marmousi模型上完成了二维地震波场的计算演示，结果表明MPS表示是计算和扩展波场传播的可行方向。

Abstract: We present a cohesive framework for simulating seismic wave propagation utilizing quantum computing paradigms and their classical tensor network equivalents.
  We detail a quantum circuit-based formulation for the explicit finite-difference time-domain (FDTD) solution of the two-dimensional acoustic wave equation and
  map this quantum architecture onto a tensor train representation, namely for Matrix Product State (MPS).
  The MPS solver enables deterministic simulation of large-scale wavefield dynamics on classical high-performance computing systems.
  We demonstrate the MPS representation by computing 2D seismic wavefields on the Marmousi model.
  Our results indicate that the MPS representation is a viable direction for computing and scaling wavefield propagation.

</details>


### [88] [Universal scaling and protocol-dependent amplitude in hybrid quantum walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01951)
*Cook Hyun Kim,Jaesub Park,Namshik Han*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种以相同量子步和经典步速率、但时间排序不同的混合量子游走（hybrid quantum walk），发现任何非零经典混入都会导致扩散行为，且两种方案在近量子极限下的扩散系数一阶极点振幅相差约两倍，极点的阶数由相干时间尺度决定，而振幅由时间组织方式决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子游走在纯量子情况下呈弹道式（ballistic）扩张，而混入经典成分后变为扩散式（diffusive）传播。作者希望探究经典成分的混入程度和时间排序方式对扩散行为的具体影响机制。

Method: 对比两种混合量子游走方案，二者采用完全相同的量子步与经典步，且经典步速率相同，但两种步骤在时间上的排列顺序不同。通过解析框架分析扩散系数在近量子极限下的行为。

Result: 任何非零的经典混入都会导致扩散行为。在量子极限附近，两种方案的扩散系数均表现出第一阶极点（first-order pole），但两者振幅相差约两倍；极点阶数由相干时间尺度（coherence timescale）决定，振幅则由时间组织方式决定。

Conclusion: 作者推测该分析框架可广泛应用于一般量子—经典混合系统（quantum–classical hybrid systems）。

Abstract: Quantum walks spread ballistically but become diffusive with classical admixture. We compare two hybrid walks using identical quantum and classical steps at the same classical-step rate but ordered differently in time. Any nonzero admixture yields diffusion. Near the quantum limit, both diffusion coefficients have first-order poles, but their amplitudes differ by about a factor of two. The coherence timescale sets the pole order, while temporal organization sets the amplitude. We expect our analytical framework to apply broadly to quantum--classical hybrid systems.

</details>


### [89] [Renormalization group and long-range conditional mutual information in hierarchical models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02141)
*Yu-Hsueh Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究重新规范化群（renormalization group, RG）与条件互信息（conditional mutual information, CMI）之间的关系，证明在局域可逆RG条件下非相邻区域间的CMI是紫外有限的（UV-finite），并通过两个具有长程CMI但具有简单RG描述的层级模型揭示RG对CMI的约束机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子态偏离局域Gibbs描述的偏差通常对局域观测量不可见，但可通过条件互信息来探测，因此需要理解RG如何约束CMI，以深入认识量子态的局域性结构。

Method: 首先通过数学证明展示：当量子态具有局域可逆RG且固定格点Hilbert空间维度时，由缓冲区域B分隔的非相邻区域A与C之间的CMI是紫外有限的。随后构建并分析两个层级模型（hierarchical models）：第一个模型在所有温度0<T<∞下具有发散的马可夫长度（Markov length），但在RG下流向无限高温乘积态；第二个模型满足局域马可夫条件（local Markov condition）却违反全局条件，并验证其对弱噪声的稳定性。

Result: 第一个模型展示了尽管CMI长程非零且Markov长度在任意有限温度下发散，系统仍可在RG下流向简单的乘积态。第二个模型表明在临界噪声强度下，两点CMI随系统尺寸仅呈多项式衰减，而两点互信息（mutual information）则完全消失。

Conclusion: RG结构对CMI施加了显著约束：在局域可逆RG下CMI是紫外有限的，但层级模型表明长程CMI可以与简单的RG描述共存，且局域马可夫性质与全局马可夫性质的违反可以分离，两者对噪声具有不同的响应行为。

Abstract: A departure of a mixed quantum state from a local Gibbs description is generally invisible to local observables but can be detected by the conditional mutual information (CMI). Here we study the relationship between the renormalization group (RG) and CMI, and in particular, how RG constrains CMI. We first show that the CMI between nonadjacent regions $A$ and $C$, conditioned on the buffer region $B$, is UV-finite whenever the state admits a locally reversible RG with a fixed on-site Hilbert space dimension. We then study two hierarchical models that have long-range CMI and yet admit a simple RG description. The first model has a divergent Markov length at every temperature $0<T<\infty$ but nevertheless flows to an infinite-temperature product state under RG. The second model satisfies the local Markov condition while violating the global one and is stable against weak noise. At the critical noise strength, the two-point CMI decays only polynomially as a function of the system size, while the two-point mutual information vanishes.

</details>


### [90] [Implementation of quantum gates by Floquet analysis of kicked quantum system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02372)
*Andrea De Luca,Carola Ciaramelletti,Simone Paganelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用Floquet理论与参数优化算法，在3至7位点的超导量子链中实现了高保真度量子操作合成与态转移协议，并在七位点链中通过有限宽度高斯脉冲实现了约170纳秒的超短双激发输运门（gate，iSWAP），同时揭示了静态参数无序在亚百分位阶（~10^-3）下的稳定性敏感性及其缓解方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多量子比特架构的精确控制仍是超导量子处理器中的关键瓶颈，作者旨在通过周期性驱动方案在扩展超导线性链中实现高保真度量子操作与高效率的态转移。

Method: 采用Floquet理论将周期性驱动建模为delta-like脉冲序列，将量子控制问题映射到准能量共振条件；结合Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff展开与Floquet谱分解解析求解最优驱动参数，并借助协方差矩阵自适应进化策略（CMA-ES）进行精细化优化；在量子门合成中测试iSWAP门，在七位点链中采用有限宽度高斯脉冲周期序列激活多个双激发输运通道。

Result: 在三量子比特体系中成功实现了高保真度的iSWAP门的合成；在七位点链中实现了门时间为约170纳秒的超短门操作（t_gate），与IBM量子硬件等固定频率transmon器件上T1能量弛豫时间形成清晰的时间尺度分离；稳定性基准测试显示体系对亚百分位阶（约10^-3）的静态参数无序存在高度敏感性，其原因在于能谱拥挤效应。

Conclusion: 该框架将时间周期性控制理论（Floquet框架）与实际量子门工程相衔接，并指出通过闭环拓扑（closed-loop topologies）架构可缓解静态参数无序带来的稳定性约束。

Abstract: Precise control of multi-qubit architectures remains a critical bottleneck in superconducting quantum processors. In this work, we investigate the synthesis of high-fidelity quantum operations and state transfer protocols within an extended superconducting linear chain, scaling from three to seven sites. Using Floquet theory, we model the periodic drive as a train of delta-like pulses, mapping the quantum control problem onto quasi-energy resonance conditions. Combining the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff expansion with Floquet spectral decomposition, we analytically identify optimal driving parameters, refined via the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy (CMA-ES). In the three-qubit architecture, this enables high-fidelity synthesis of the iSWAP gate. Extending to a seven-site chain, we implement periodic trains of finite-width Gaussian pulses to activate distinct double-excitation transport channels with ultra-short gate durations t_gate (~170 ns). This achieves a clear scale separation from energy-relaxation times (T1) typical of fixed-frequency transmon devices with tunable couplers, such as IBM Quantum hardware. Finally, we benchmark stability under realistic imperfections, revealing a heightened sensitivity to static parameter disorder at the sub-percent level (eta ~ 10^-3) driven by spectral crowding, and discuss how closed-loop topologies could mitigate this constraint. This framework bridges time-periodic control theory and practical quantum gate engineering.

</details>


### [91] [Effective quasiparticle conserving Lindbladians in the thermodynamic limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02725)
*Lea Lenke,Kai Phillip Schmidt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将连续相似变换（continuous similarity transformations）框架推广至非厄米开放量子多体系统，提出了两种互补方法（pcst++和deepCST）来直接在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下构造守恒拟粒子（quasiparticle）数的有效Lindblad算子，并将它们应用于耗散横场Ising链（dissipative transverse-field Ising chain），揭示了Ising相互作用对自旋翻转激发衰减率的重整化效应。该方法为开放量子多体系统在热力学极限下推导有效的拟粒子图像提供了通用的微扰与非微扰工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子多体系统通常由Lindblad主方程描述，但在热力学极限下处理Lindblad算子的谱结构仍是一项重大挑战。现有方法难以直接对无穷系统的Lindbladian进行系统性的拟粒子图像下的对角化。

Method: 提出了两种方法：pcst++将微扰连续幺正变换推广至Lindblad算子，结合连锁团簇展开（linked-cluster expansion）获得无穷系统的高阶级数展开；deepCST则将增强的微扰连续幺正变换与非微扰截断方案结合，直接获得热力学极限下的非微扰有效Lindblad算子。两种方法共享同一拟粒子守恒生成元，并对每个自旋分裂为两个格点以实现Lindbladian的纯化（purification）。

Result: 将两种方法应用于低Ising相互作用区的耗散横场Ising链，在零到两个拟粒子的扇区中导出并分析了有效的拟粒子守恒Lindbladian；结果表明Ising相互作用会重整化基本自旋翻转激发的衰减率，并为相干相互作用与耗散之间的竞争提供了微观机制。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了微扰与非微扰的连续相似变换作为通用工具，可用于在热力学极限下推导开放量子多体系统的有效拟粒子图像，为理解耗散与相互作用协同作用下的量子多体行为提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Open quantum many-body systems are commonly described by Lindblad master equations, yet the treatment of Lindbladian operators in the thermodynamic limit remains a major challenge. We develop a framework for constructing effective quasiparticle-conserving Lindbladian operators directly in the thermodynamic limit. Our approach extends continuous similarity transformations to non-Hermitian open quantum systems and enables the systematic block diagonalization of Lindbladians with respect to the quasiparticle (qp) number. We formulate two complementary methods. The first, projective continuous similarity transformations (pcst++), generalizes perturbative continuous unitary transformations to Lindblad operators so that a linked-cluster expansion allows us to obtain high-order series expansions of the infinite system. The second, deepCST, extends directly evaluated enhanced perturbative continuous unitary transformations by combining the same qp-conserving generator with a perturbative truncation scheme that yields non-perturbative effective Lindbladian operators directly in thermodynamic limit. We apply pcst++ and deepCST to the dissipative transverse-field Ising chain with local dissipation. We focus on the low-Ising regime. We purify the Lindbladian by splitting each spin into two sites. A spin flip then corresponds to two qps. We derive and analyze the effective, qp-conserving Lindbladian in the sectors with zero to two qps. We show how the Ising interaction renormalizes the decay rates of elementary spin-flip excitations and provides the microscopic mechanism for a competition between coherent interactions and dissipation. Our work establishes perturbative and non-perturbative continuous similarity transformations as a versatile tool for deriving effective qp pictures of open quantum many-body systems in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>

### [92] [Memory as an Energy Landscape---Hopfield](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02195)
*Nima Dehghani*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文（章节）将Hopfield网络重新构建为一种记忆的物理理论，而非仅仅是一个早期神经网络算法。文章系统梳理了其动力学定义、能量函数、平均场检索理论和现代扩展，并对容量声明与有效性边界进行了批判性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 写作动机在于阐明Hopfield网络（Hopfield network）作为物理理论在记忆研究中的实质贡献，澄清其相对于早期结构（如阈值逻辑、Hebbian联想、关联记忆）的增量创新，并区分其中已被确立的结果、仍然有效的原理以及受到假设约束的局限。

Method: 本文采用理论重建方法：先孤立Hopfield合成所引入的核心新增内容——内容寻址记忆（content-addressable memory）的动力学定义、带Lyapunov函数的对称递归架构、模式在耦合中的Hebbian嵌入，以及盆地与鲁棒性的物理解释；随后完整推导二元与分级响应能量函数、信号-串扰分解、广泛负载下的平均场检索理论以及零温度检索旋节线（spinodal, α≈0.138）；最后追踪能量导向纲领在模拟优化网络、多项式稠密联想记忆、指数交互与现代连续Hopfield更新（含其退化为缩放点积注意力（scaled dot-product attention）的条件）中的延伸。文章并通过固定种子数值实验展示相关机理作为分析结果的佐证而非替代。

Result: 结果显示：Hopfield网络可被视为一种有效理论（effective theory），其对称性、局域性与点神经元假设划定了生物学适用范围；容量声明需绑定其无序系综、标度极限与成功准则，由此解释了不同数值存储极限之间表面冲突的消解。

Conclusion: 文章的结论性评估区分了已经确立的结果与仍然持存的原理、受假设约束的局限以及开放问题，主张将Hopfield网络视为一种具有物理理论结构的记忆模型，并强调数值实验只能展示机制图景，不能替代分析性结果。

Abstract: This chapter reconstructs the Hopfield network as a physical theory of memory rather than merely an early neural-network algorithm. It begins with the problem as it stood before 1982-threshold logic, Hebbian association, correlation memories, and recurrent binary networks-and isolates what Hopfield's synthesis added: a dynamical definition of content-addressable memory, a symmetric recurrent architecture with a Lyapunov function, a Hebbian embedding of patterns in its couplings, and a physical account of basins, robustness, and graceful degradation. The binary and graded-response energy functions are derived in full, together with the signal-crosstalk decomposition governing pattern stability, the mean-field theory of retrieval at extensive load, and the zero-temperature retrieval spinodal at (alpha 0.138) established by Amit, Gutfreund, and Sompolinsky. The energy-based program is then followed through analog optimization networks, polynomial dense associative memories, exponential interactions, and modern continuous Hopfield updates, including the precise conditions under which the update becomes scaled dot-product attention. Throughout, capacity claims are tied to their disorder ensemble, scaling limit, and success criterion, showing why numerically different storage limits need not conflict. A closing assessment distinguishes established results from surviving principles, assumption-bound limitations, and open problems, treating the Hopfield network as an effective theory whose symmetry, locality, and point-neuron assumptions delimit its biological reach. Fixed-seed numerical experiments expose the mechanisms discussed but do not substitute for analytical results.

</details>
