### [93] [Collective Cell Fluidity Controls Active Prestress Transmission in Cell-Extracellular-Matrix Tissues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02360)
*Liyang Wang,J. M. Schwarz,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个三维微力学模型，将可变形、可重排的细胞团簇与半柔性纤维无序网络通过动态力生成界面耦合，研究细胞产生的预应力（prestress）如何通过与细胞-基质界面的传输组织共同调控组织的宏观刚度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织是活性复合材料，多细胞集体与细胞外基质（extracellular matrix）在力学上相互重组，但细胞簇与纤维网络间耦合如何影响整体力学响应尚需定量理解。

Method: 构建三维微力学模型，将类固或类液的顶点模型（vertex model）球体细胞簇与半柔性纤维无序网络耦合，通过被动或收缩性连接子（linkers）在细胞边界重组时更新，并设计完整的、空腔的及被动连接子的对照实验以分离空腔形成、界面系留和主动加载效应。

Result: 在小应变下，被动系留提供适度增强，而主动收缩预应力显著刚化基质；类固细胞簇保持相干力传输，过量模量随活性、簇大小和数目跨条件下近似按|σ|^1.4标度；类液细胞簇界面更新增强，产生较弱且非单调的预应力-刚度耦合；簇数增加在预应力区域通过充分持久的界面连通时产生集体刚化；大应变下两类系统均趋近对应空腔网络响应。

Conclusion: 细胞生成的预应力只有在与其跨细胞-基质界面的传输组织和持久性协同作用时，才能控制宏观刚度。

Abstract: Tissues are active composites in which multicellular collectives and extracellular matrices mechanically reorganize one another. We develop a three-dimensional micromechanical model that couples deformable, rearranging cell clusters to a disordered network of semiflexible fibers through a dynamic, force-generating interface. Cell clusters are represented as solid-like or fluid-like vertex-model spheroids and coupled to the matrix by passive or contractile linkers renewed as the cluster boundary reorganizes. Matched intact, voided, and passive-linker controls separate cavity formation, interfacial tethering, and active loading. At small strain, passive tethering provides modest reinforcement, whereas active contraction prestresses and strongly stiffens the matrix. Solid-like clusters preserve coherent force transmission and exhibit an excess modulus scaling approximately as $|σ|^{1.4}$ across changes in activity, cluster size, and cluster number. Fluid-like clusters undergo greater interfacial renewal, producing weaker and nonmonotonic coupling between prestress and stiffness. Increasing cluster number produces collective stiffening when prestressed regions become connected through sufficiently persistent interfaces. At large strain, both solid-like and fluid-like systems approach the corresponding voided-network response as the residual fiber backbone becomes mechanically dominant. Thus, cell-generated prestress controls macroscopic stiffness only together with the organization and persistence of its transmission across the cell-matrix interface.

</details>


### [94] [Multivariate hyperdensity functional theory for inhomogeneous equilibrium fluids: From first principles to simulation-based machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02594)
*Florian Sammüller,Matthias Schmidt*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种超密度泛函理论（hyperdensity functional theory）的多变量推广，用于同时研究经典多体系统中多个超观测量（hyperobservables）的性质及其相互关系。该框架给出密度泛函（density functional）意义上的通用表征与预测方案，所有平衡平均值、方差与协方差均为普适泛函，且相关超涨落分布满足精确的超Ornstein-Zernike方程，可结合监督机器学习实践应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的超密度泛函理论仅针对单一超观测量，无法同时刻画多个超观测量之间的相互关联。为更好地描述空间非均匀经典多体系统中复杂多体现象的协同行为，需要发展一个多变量推广框架。

Method: 将精确的Mermin-Evans经典密度泛函映射应用到扩展系综中，发展多变量推广的超密度泛函理论。从扩展多变量系综的极小化原理推导多变量超Ornstein-Zernike方程，并利用监督机器学习（supervised machine learning）在模拟数据上训练神经网络以获取全部超密度泛函。

Result: 以受限硬棒流体（confined hard rod fluids）中的团簇化问题为例，选取总粒子数和最大团簇尺寸作为代表性超观测量加以验证。数值方法能够高效且成功地预测所有由所选超观测量诱导的统计量，通过与测试数据的对比验证了其准确性。

Conclusion: 多变量超密度泛函理论将第一性原理（first-principles）方法与机器学习紧密结合，能够系统地表征和预测一般多体现象中的统计关联，并具有数值可及性和实践可行性。

Abstract: Hyperdensity functional theory facilitates the investigation of the equilibrium behavior of a general order parameter or statistical mechanical observable in spatially inhomogeneous classical many-body systems. The approach is based on applying the exact Mermin-Evans classical density functional mapping to an extended ensemble. Here we present the multivariate generalization for investigating simultaneously the properties and interrelations of several different hyperobservables of choice. The resulting framework gives rise to a systematic characterization and prediction scheme for general many-body phenomena. All pertinent equilibrium averages, variances, and covariances constitute universal density functionals, as we demonstrate explicitly. Associated one-body hyperfluctuation profiles quantify the degree of correlation of the local density with first- and second-order combinations of hyperobservables. These multivariate hyperfluctuation profiles are accessible in many-body simulations and they satisfy exact hyper-Ornstein-Zernike equations, which we derive from the minimization principle in the extended multivariate ensemble. The formal structure of the theory integrates naturally with supervised machine learning, which renders all hyperdensity functionals accessible in practice via training of neural networks on simulation data. We demonstrate all salient techniques using the illustrative case of clustering in confined hard rod fluids, thereby choosing the total number of particles and the largest cluster size as the representative hyperobservables of interest. Our numerical methodology enables the efficient and successful prediction of all statistical quantities induced by the chosen hyperobservables, which we verify via comparison to test data and which we attribute to the tight interplay of first-principles and machine-learning concepts that our general approach combines.

</details>


### [95] [The Universal Role of Fragility on the Yielding Transition of Active Glass under Oscillatory Shear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02609)
*Arnab Mandal,Roni Chatterjee,Smarajit Karmakar*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过分子动力学模拟，揭示了活性掺杂（Run-and-Tumble 粒子）能系统性降低无定形玻璃的动力学脆性（kinetic fragility），从而从脆性向韧性转变其屈服响应，并确立动力学脆性为统一调控被动与活性玻璃屈服行为的核心参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屈服转变（yielding transition）标志着无定形固体不可逆塑性变形的开端，但其微观控制因素（尤其是脆性-韧性响应之间的转变）尚不清楚。尽管已有研究确认动力学脆性是调控被动玻璃屈服的关键参数，该联系在活性玻璃（active glasses）中是否成立仍是开放问题。

Method: 采用分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟，对掺杂了 Run-and-Tumble（RTP）活性粒子的 Kob-Andersen 玻璃形成体系施加振荡剪切（oscillatory shear），系统研究活性强度对屈服行为和力学响应的影响。

Result: 活性会系统性降低动力学脆性并改变力学响应：屈服点 γ_c 随活性单调下降，且与 Arrhenius 活化能垒呈幂律关系；增加活性抑制屈服应变对热历史的依赖，使响应由脆性变为更韧，伴随更平滑的应力松弛和更小的应力不连续性。接近屈服时达到稳态的时间尺度仍保持临界幂律发散，表明活性不改变转变的根本临界特性；活性玻璃还发展出更宽、更弥散的剪切带（shear band）。

Conclusion: 结果表明动力学脆性是被动与活性玻璃中统一表征屈服行为的参数，同时证明活性提供了一条有效调控无定形材料力学响应的途径。

Abstract: The yielding transition marks the onset of irreversible plastic deformation in amorphous solids and plays a central role in determining the mechanical stability and failure of metallic glasses, colloidal suspensions, and biological assemblies. Despite extensive research, the microscopic factors governing the nature of yielding, particularly the transition between brittle and ductile mechanical responses, remain poorly understood. Recent studies have identified kinetic fragility as a key parameter governing yielding in passive glasses; whether this connection persists in active glasses remains open. Here, using molecular dynamics simulations of a Kob-Andersen glass former doped with Run-and-Tumble (RTP) active particles under oscillatory shear, we show that activity systematically reduces kinetic fragility and consequently alters the mechanical response. The common yield point $γ_c$ decreases monotonically with activity and exhibits a power-law dependence on the Arrhenius activation barrier. Increasing activity suppresses the dependence of the yield strain on thermal history and transforms the response from brittle-like to increasingly ductile, with smoother stress relaxation and reduced stress discontinuities. The timescale to reach steady state near yielding retains a critical power-law divergence, indicating that activity does not alter the underlying critical character of the transition. Active glasses also develop broader, more diffuse shear bands. Our results establish kinetic fragility as a unifying parameter governing yielding in both passive and active glasses and demonstrate that activity offers a powerful route to tune the mechanical response of amorphous materials.

</details>


### [96] [Pure FENE Bond Potential for Soft Matter and Biological Simulations: Theory, HOOMD-blue Implementation, and Applications to Polymer, Colloidal, and Membrane Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02627)
*Anirban Polley*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文在HOOMD-blue中引入了一种独立式FENE键合势（standalone FENE bond potential），将有限键长可延伸性与非键合相互作用分离，实现了对键弹性和集体结构组织的独立控制，并在聚合物链、胶体网络和生物膜模型中验证了其效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Kremer–Grest框架中FENE键合与WCA短程排斥相互作用耦合在一起，限制了独立控制键合弹性与非键合相互作用的能力，需要一种模块化的键合势实现分离。

Method: 在HOOMD-blue仿真软件中开发独立式FENE键合势，其有限键延伸性不依赖所选择的非键合相互作用类型，可灵活与WCA、Lennard–Jones或其他对相互作用结合；并在粗粒化聚合物链、胶体网络和网格基生物膜模型中测试其性能。

Result: 无短程斥力稳定化的纯FENE键合会导致结构显著收缩，而添加WCA排斥后能抑制这种塌缩并保持有限的空间延伸结构，表明两种贡献的分离在不同体系中均表现出预期效果。

Conclusion: 该独立式FENE公式提供了分子连接性与非键合相互作用各自独立调控的模块化框架，赋予有限的键延伸性与空间排斥相互作用独立控制键局部力学和结构组织的能力。

Abstract: The finitely extensible nonlinear elastic (FENE) potential is widely used as a bonded interaction in coarse-grained simulations of polymers, soft matter, colloids, and biological systems. In the classical Kremer--Grest framework, FENE bonding is combined with a short-range Weeks--Chandler--Andersen (WCA) interaction to provide finite bond extensibility together with excluded-volume repulsion. Although this combination is highly successful, it intrinsically couples bonded elasticity to the nonbonded interaction, limiting the ability to independently control these two contributions. Here, we introduce a standalone FENE bond potential in HOOMD-blue in which finite bond extensibility is implemented independently of the choice of nonbonded interaction. This formulation allows the same FENE bond potential to be combined with WCA, Lennard--Jones, or other pair interactions without modifying the bonded interaction itself. We demonstrate the utility of the standalone formulation in coarse-grained polymer chains, colloidal networks, and mesh-based biological membrane models. Across these systems, Pure FENE bonding without short-range excluded-volume stabilization produces pronounced structural contraction, whereas the addition of WCA repulsion suppresses this collapse and preserves finite, spatially extended structures. These results demonstrate that separating finite bond extensibility from steric interactions provides independent control over local bond mechanics and collective structural organization. The standalone FENE formulation therefore provides a modular framework for coarse-grained simulations in which molecular connectivity and nonbonded interactions represent distinct physical mechanisms.

</details>


### [97] [XPCS-Echo and broad relaxation measurements using a bunch-mode data acquisition scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02645)
*William Chèvremont,Thomas Gibaud,Yuriy Chushkin,Theyencheri Narayanan*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于束团模式（bunch-mode）和非周期斐波那契序列（Fibonacci sequence）的多散斑X射线光子关联谱（XPCS）数据采集方案，以高效测量大范围延时上的强度-强度自关联函数$g_2(q, τ)$，用于研究软粘弹性材料在振荡剪切下的非仿射变形与屈服行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多散斑XPCS中，对大量回波进行高时间分辨测量对计算硬件和样品稳定性提出严重约束；传统全帧采集方式面临数据量巨大和样品辐射损伤的问题，需要更高效的数据采集策略。

Method: 实施束团模式数据采集方案，即在回波峰中心区域仅采集最高时间分辨率的帧或束团；同时将束团按非周期斐波那契序列排布，从而以数量级更少的帧数实现对$g_2(q, τ)$在长时间跨度上的测量；并提供了用于定量描述完整$g_2(q, τ)$的解析表达式。

Result: 利用缓慢弛豫的模型胶体悬浮液（model colloidal suspensions）展示了所提出的XPCS采集方案的性能，验证了该方案能以极少的帧数有效重构长时间跨度的$g_2(q, τ)$。

Conclusion: 束团模式结合斐波那契序列的采集方案能够大幅减少数据采集量和计算负担，同时保持回波探测和非仿射变形研究的精度，为软粘弹性材料的振荡剪切研究提供了可行且高效的技术路径。

Abstract: Echoes observed in the intensity-intensity autocorrelation functions [$g_2(q, τ)$] is a powerful method for probing nonaffine deformations and yielding behavior of soft viscoelastic materials subjected to an oscillatory shear. Multispeckle X-ray photon correlation spectroscopy (XPCS) measurements of large number of echoes with high time resolution impose severe constraints in terms of the computational hardware and potential degradation of the sample. These issues are alleviated by implementing a bunch-mode data acquisition scheme in which the highest resolution frames or bunches centered at the echo peaks. In addition, by placing the bunches in an aperiodic Fibonacci sequence, $g_2(q, τ)$ over a long time span can be measured with orders of magnitude lesser number of frames. The performance of these XPCS acquisition schemes is demonstrated using slowly relaxing model colloidal suspensions. Furthermore, an analytical expression is provided for the quantitative description of full $g_2(q, τ)$.

</details>


### [98] [Liquid-liquid phase transitions in dipolar liquids. Insights into Supercooled Water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02666)
*Maria Grazia Izzo*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文通过粗粒化理论证明，偶极液体中平动与偶极两个自由度之间存在耦合，铁电转变可触发液-液转变，反之顺电相的液-液一级转变也可诱导铁电有序；低密度液体第一配位壳层空间各向异性增强，表明超冷水的液-液转变涉及第一壳层内结构变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶极液体（dipolar liquid）中平动与偶极两个耦合自由度可以处于不同的热力学状态，且某一分量的状态可能影响另一分量。通过粗粒化方法消去一个分量以获得另一分量的有效相互作用，可揭示铁电序在偶极液体中引发液-液转变的微观机制。

Method: 构建一个粗粒化理论（coarse-grained theory），其中最近邻粒子间距通过依赖密度的机械柔度（mechanical compliance）与偶极相互作用耦联；同时结合超冷TIP4P/Ice水的数值模拟，分析对关联函数（pair correlation functions）第一配位壳层结构对偶极作用的敏感性。

Result: 理论预测铁电转变可触发液-液转变，顺电相（paraelectric phase）中的一级液-液转变也可诱导铁电有序。各向异性的偶极依赖软核相互作用等效于Jagla型各向同性双尺度相互作用，但其双尺度模式依赖密度与偶极构型。模拟表明低密度液体中第一配位壳层的空间各向异性增强且有效排除体积增大。

Conclusion: 超冷水液-液转变不仅涉及间隙区域重组和四面体有序度变化，还包含第一配位壳层内的结构变化，提示偶极相互作用在超冷水液-液相变中扮演重要角色。

Abstract: Dipolar liquids combine two coupled degrees of freedom, translational and dipolar. Intriguingly, these two sectors can reside in distinct thermodynamic states, with the state of one sector possibly favoring a specific state of the other. The onset of ferroelectric order in dipolar liquids is an example of this mutual stabilization. Since this coupling is often encoded in interaction potentials, projecting out one sector to derive an effective interaction for the other can uncover the microscopic mechanism underlying this phenomenon. A coarse-grained theory in which the distance between nearest-neighbor particles depends on their dipolar interaction through a density-dependent mechanical compliance shows that a ferroelectric transition can trigger a liquid-liquid transition. It also predicts a first-order liquid-liquid transition in the paraelectric phase that can in turn induce ferroelectric ordering. The anisotropic dipolar-dependent soft-core interaction is shown to lead to an effective Jagla-like isotropic two-length-scale interaction, known to support liquid-liquid phase transitions. Here, however, the two-length-scale pattern depends on density and dipolar configuration, becoming sensitive to dipolar order. Analysis of supercooled TIP4P/Ice water numerical simulations shows that first-coordination-shell features of pair correlation functions are sensitive to dipolar interaction, supporting the coarse-grained theory foundations. Comparison of low- and high-density liquid water reveals enhanced spatial anisotropy of the first coordination shell in the low-density liquid, with increased effective excluded volume, showing that the liquid-liquid transition involves structural changes within the first shell, beyond reorganization of the interstitial region and tetrahedral order. These findings point to a role for dipolar interactions in supercooled water liquid-liquid phase transition.

</details>


### [99] [Response of a Model Glass to Athermal Quasistatic Pinching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02692)
*Takumi Nagasawa,Kirsten Martens,Jean-Louis Barrat,Misaki Ozawa*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本论文通过无热准静态模拟（athermal quasistatic simulation），数值研究了非晶固体（amorphous solid）对局域力偶（force dipole，即"夹捏"）的力学响应，揭示了弹性到塑性转变过程中响应由玻璃稳定性（glass stability）与施加伸长量共同调控的规律，并提出夹捏可作为研究非晶固体的一种最小局域探针（minimal local probe）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非晶固体在局域扰动下的力学响应是揭示其弹性与塑性行为的关键，但此前缺乏对局域力偶施加下弹性-塑性转变的系统研究。作者旨在通过夹捏这一最简局域探针，阐明玻璃稳定性如何调控响应从可被连续弹性理论描述的弹性态过渡到集体塑性重排。

Method: 采用无热准静态模拟，对具有不同稳定性程度的模型玻璃施加局域力偶（夹捏），以施加伸长量（imposed extension）作为控制参数，即两夹捏粒子间静止长度相对其初始间距的增加量，由此连续调节系统从弹性到塑性区域。通过提取塑性变形主轴（principal axis of plastic deformation）分析相应的位移场与塑性活性。

Result: 小伸长量下响应为弹性，位移场与线性弹性理论的偶极强迫一致，呈长程幂律衰减；平均响应呈各向异性类四极（quadrupolar-like）图案，与解析预测定量吻合，且连续描述在粒子尺度距离内仍有效。大伸长量下响应变为塑性：退火充分的玻璃呈现局域塑性事件，而未充分退火的系统表现出非局域化的级联重排。

Conclusion: 夹捏提供了一种研究非晶固体最小局域探针，其响应由玻璃稳定性与施加伸长量共同决定，揭示了弹性、基本重排与集体塑性涌现之间的相互作用。

Abstract: We numerically investigate the mechanical response of amorphous solids to localized force dipoles, referred to as pinching, using athermal quasistatic simulations of model glasses with varying degrees of stability. We employ a control parameter, the imposed extension, corresponding to the increase in rest length between the two pinched particles relative to their initial separation. Increasing this extension allows us to continuously tune the system from the elastic to the plastic regime. For small extensions, the response remains elastic. In this regime, the displacement field induced by pinching is well described by linear elasticity and exhibits a long-range power-law decay consistent with dipolar forcing. Averaged responses display anisotropic, quadrupolar-like patterns, with quantitative agreement between simulations and analytical predictions. This continuum description remains valid down to particle-scale distances. As the imposed extension increases, the response becomes plastic. Pinching can then trigger either localized or system-spanning rearrangements, depending on glass stability. Well-annealed glasses exhibit localized plastic events, whereas poorly annealed systems display delocalized cascades. We introduce a method to extract the principal axis of plastic deformation and analyze the associated displacement fields and plastic activity. Overall, our results demonstrate that pinching provides a minimal local probe of amorphous solids. The resulting response, governed by both glass stability and imposed extension, offers insight into the interplay between elasticity, elementary rearrangements, and the emergence of collective plasticity.

</details>


### [100] [Relating solute interactions to interfacial properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02762)
*Varun Mandalaparthy,Benjamin M. Curlee,William G. Noid*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种稀溶液理论（dilute solution theory, DST），用于描述稀释共溶质（cosolute）对液-汽共存界面自由能的影响。该理论基于巨正则扰动理论并借助吉布斯系综消除体相贡献，通过分子动力学模拟验证其在较高浓度下的准确性，并揭示了溶质-溶质吸引作用可将弱内禀表面活性剂转变为弱有效排斥剂（depletant）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体界面在商业产品、现代技术、生物过程和环境现象中普遍存在且至关重要，且其性质对液相体相组成高度敏感，因此需要一种普适理论来定量描述稀释共溶质对液-汽界面性质的影响。

Method: 作者发展了一种稀溶液理论，采用巨正则扰动理论（grand canonical perturbation theory）将界面自由能严格关联到液体溶液浓度，并利用相应的吉布斯系综（Gibbs ensemble）处理液-汽共存及消除体相贡献；将展开系数用微观配分函数表达，并对溶质-溶质相互作用作最低阶处理，区分溶质的内禀界面偏好与有效界面偏好。理论通过Lennard-Jones球体的二元和三元混合物的分子动力学模拟进行验证。

Result: 分子动力学模拟表明，该稀溶液理论能够准确描述体系在直至较高浓度下的界面性质。此外，模拟揭示了吸引性溶质-溶质相互作用可将弱内禀表面活性剂（surfactant）转化为弱有效排斥剂（depletant）的重要现象。

Conclusion: 本研究建立并验证了一种适用于液-汽界面的稀溶液理论，其在较宽浓度范围内表现准确，并阐明了溶质-溶质相互作用在决定溶质有效界面行为中的关键角色，为理解稀释共溶质调控液体界面性质提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Liquid interfaces are both ubiquitous and also critically important for many commercial products, modern technologies, biological processes, and environmental phenomena. The properties of these interfaces can depend quite sensitively upon the composition of the bulk liquid phase. In this work, we develop a dilute solution theory (DST) for the influence of dilute cosolutes upon the interface between coexisting liquid and vapor phases. We employ a grand canonical perturbation theory to rigorously relate the interfacial free energy to the concentration of the liquid solution. We leverage a corresponding Gibbs ensemble to treat liquid-vapor coexistence and to eliminate the contribution of the bulk phases from this free energy. We express the coefficients of the resulting expansion in terms of microscopic partition functions. By treating solute-solute interactions to lowest order, we distinguish between the intrinsic and effective interfacial preferences of solutes. While the former reflects only solute-solvent interactions, the latter depends upon the solution composition and reflects the influence of solute-solute interactions. We assess this DST with molecular dynamics simulations of binary and ternary mixtures of Lennard-Jones spheres. These simulations demonstrate that DST accurately models the interfacial properties of these systems up to relatively high concentrations. Moreover, the simulations illustrate the impact of attractive solute-solute interactions in converting weak intrinsic surfactants into weak effective depletants.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Extended Body Propagators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01829)
*Rory O'Dwyer*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，Ansoldi等人、Erbin等人与Stanford等人提出的三种看似不同的延展体（extended-body）传播子，均可视为Polyakov路径积分（Polyakov path integral）的实现；其中Ansoldi传播子对应于一个特定概率密度函数的特征函数，该密度在环境维度D下由$f(A)=C A (\alpha^2+A^2)^{-D/2}$给出。文章进一步证明该面积测度（area measure）恰为三角形面积的分布，并论证管状世界面（tubular worldsheet）的测度可约化为此三角形的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三种在不同物理设置中独立出现的延展体传播子看似互不相关，但理论上都应统一于Polyakov路径积分的框架之下。作者希望揭示它们之间的数学联系，并寻找一种自然的面积测度，以便将此前一维Polyakov积分的路径空间测度推广到二维单纯复形（simplicial complex）上。

Method: 作者采用解析与概率论方法，将Ansoldi传播子识别为某个随机变量特征函数，该随机变量的概率密度在环境维度D下为$f(A)=C A (\alpha^2+A^2)^{-D/2}$，尺度参数为$\alpha=\sqrt{b/2}$。随后将Erbin等人的壳上两点振幅与JT引力（JT gravity）与该密度的D=4情形进行对比，并建立面积测度与三角形面积律之间的等价关系，最后对任意两条边界曲线间的管状世界面测度展开（部分严格的）约化论证。

Result: Ansoldi传播子可唯一地对应到上述幂律形式概率密度的特征函数，其指数仅由维度D决定；该密度在D=4时与Erbin等人的壳上两点振幅及JT引力在文中所明确的意义下一致。面积测度被证明等价于三角形面积定律，其中两条边来自伴生手稿中一维Polyakov积分的对应测度。作者经文献综述认为该观察及所构造的面积测度具有新颖性。

Conclusion: 该面积测度是此前非延展体路径空间测度向二维单纯复形自然推广的候选对象，并为统一不同延展体传播子框架提供了新的数学工具。作者部分严格地论证了管状世界面测度可归结为三角形测度的约化结果，为进一步研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Three seemingly distinct extended-body propagators devised by Ansoldi et al., Erbin et al., and Stanford et al.\ respectively arise in completely different settings, though each can be considered a realization of the Polyakov path integral. This paper proves that the Ansoldi propagator corresponds to the characteristic function of the random variable with the probability density function $f(A)=C\,A\,(α^{2}+A^{2})^{-D/2}\mathbf{1}_{A>0}$ in ambient dimension $D$, of scale $α=\sqrt{b/2}$. The exponent is fixed by the dimension and by nothing else in the Ansoldi/geometric comparison; the on-shell two-point amplitude of Erbin et al. and JT gravity agree with its $D=4$ member in the senses made precise below. After an extensive literature review, the author believes that this observation and constructed area measure is novel. The area measure will, however, emerge as the natural candidate for the extension of the earlier non-extended body path space measures to two dimensional simplicial complexes. We further show that the area measure of this paper is exactly the law of the area of a triangle, two of whose sides are drawn from the measure obtained in the companion manuscripts for the one-dimensional Polyakov integral. We close with an argument, rigorous in part, which reduces the measure of tubular worldsheets between two arbitrary boundary curves to that of this triangle.

</details>


### [102] [Applying the convolution theorem to the two-dimensional magnetic inverse problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01862)
*Michael T. M. Woodley*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统整理并阐明了利用卷积定理（convolution theorem）求解二维磁学反演问题（magnetic inverse problem）的关键数学细节，即从磁场数据重建电流密度。文中重点给出了Biot-Savart定律中卷积核（convolution kernel）傅里叶变换的闭式解，并在此基础上通过卷积定理，将傅里叶变换后的电流密度表示为傅里叶变换后磁场数据及其他变量的函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维磁学反演问题的数学求解涉及复杂的卷积运算及其傅里叶变换处理，然而其中关键的数学细节在文献中往往散落且缺乏系统整理，需要一篇论文对该方法进行全面收集与澄清，以为后续将该方法应用于实际数据打下清晰、可用的数学基础。

Method: 论文对卷积定理在二维磁学反演问题中的应用方法进行了数学归纳与阐明。具体而言，首先求解Biot-Savart定律中涉及卷积核的傅里叶变换并以闭式（closed-form）表达；随后借助卷积定理，将电流密度的傅里叶变换表示为磁场数据傅里叶变换及其他相关变量的函数，从而完成反演问题的频域求解。

Result: 论文给出了Biot-Savart卷积核傅里叶变换的闭式解，并据此通过卷积定理建立了傅里叶变换后的电流密度与傅里叶变换后的磁场数据之间的函数关系，从而提供了完整的二维磁反演频域求解框架。

Conclusion: 通过对关键数学步骤的系统整理与澄清，论文为利用卷积定理求解二维磁学反演问题提供了严谨且完整的数学方法基础，可支撑从磁场测量数据出发的电流密度重建实践。

Abstract: A collection and clarification is given of the key mathematical details of the method of applying the convolution theorem as part of the solution to the two-dimensional magnetic inverse problem: reconstructing current density from magnetic field data. Particular emphasis is given to obtaining a closed-form solution for the Fourier transform of the convolution kernel that features in the Biot-Savart law. Having done this, the Fourier-transformed current density is obtained, via the convolution theorem, as a function of Fourier-transformed magnetic field data and other variables.

</details>


### [103] [Fractional oscillators with fractional damping in the presence of external forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01883)
*Fernando Olivar-Romero,Oscar Rosas-Ortiz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在外力作用下含有显式分数阶阻尼项的分数阶振子的动力学行为。对应的三参数Caputo型分数阶微分方程可求得精确解，其中两个分数阶参数描述固有耗散机制，第三个参数刻画外驱作用中的阻尼特征。研究发现外力类型决定了系统的长期演化行为：恒力下系统到达稳定位置，正弦力下则呈现持续振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶动力学在描述粘弹性材料等复杂系统时具有广泛适用性，而带有分数阶阻尼项的振子对外部力的响应规律此前尚未被系统阐明。该研究旨在揭示分数阶阻尼振子在不同外驱条件下的解析解及其长期行为特征。

Method: 本文建立了一个含三个分数阶参数（fractional parameter）的分数阶微分方程（在Caputo意义下），其中两个参数对应于产生连续能量耗散的内在耗散机制，第三个参数表征振子受外力驱动时面对的阻尼项。通过拉普拉斯变换（Laplace transform）的方法求解外力的时域形式对解的影响，并选取无外力、恒力、正弦力及阶梯力等典型外力量为具体范例进行分析。

Result: 外力存在时该分数阶动力学规律与粘弹性材料的牛顿-斯科特-布莱尔模型（Newton-Scott-Blair model）相关联；外力的拉普拉斯变换决定了解的形式。在恒驱动力下系统经历瞬态后到达固定位置；在正弦驱动力下系统呈现持续的振荡行为。

Conclusion: 结果表明外部驱动力决定了分数阶阻尼振子的长期行为：恒力导致系统趋于稳定平衡位置，而周期力则维持持续振荡，说明分数阶阻尼不影响系统对外力的稳态响应形态，而只改变其瞬态与耗散特性。

Abstract: We investigate how a fractional oscillator reacts to external forces when the dynamic law includes a damping term that is explicitly fractional. The corresponding three-parametric fractional differential equation (in the Caputo sense) admits exact solution. Two of the fractional parameters are associated with the intrinsic dissipation mechanism that produces continuous dissipation of energy. The third fractional parameter characterizes the damping term against which the oscillator is driven by the external force. When external time-dependent forces are included, the fractional dynamic law is associated with the Newton-Scott-Blair model of viscoelastic materials. The Laplace transform of the external force defines the profile of the solution; specific examples include the absence of external forces as well as constant, sinusoidal and stepped external forces. With a constant driving force, the system reaches a fixed position after a transient period. With a sinusoidal driving force, the system exhibits persistent oscillatory behavior. These results suggest that the external driving force determines the long-term behavior of the fractionally damped oscillator.

</details>


### [104] [On the Convergence of Metadynamics with Gaussian Hills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01934)
*Andrey Badanin,Olga Rogacheva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对一维集体变量（collective variable）下的元动力学（metadynamics）方法，将其演化问题归结为复制子型微分方程（replicator-type differential equation）并分析其长期行为。研究发现：周期集体变量下元动力学收敛可证；但当集体变量定义在有限区间上时，平稳解仅在弱意义下作为有限原子测度（finite atomic measure）存在，缺乏清晰的准稳态（quasi-stationary state），导致元动力学失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元动力学是分子建模中广泛使用的增强采样（enhanced-sampling）方法类别，但其理论基础——尤其是在一维集体变量配以高斯丘陵（Gaussian hills）时的收敛性——尚未得到充分刻画，需要结合现有收敛结果来探究其应用边界。

Method: 作者将元动力学的演化问题约化为复制子型微分方程，并利用其长期行为分析来研究收敛性。分析分为两种情形：周期性集体变量的收敛证明，以及有限区间定义下方程的弱收敛分析。

Result: 周期集体变量情形下，元动力学的收敛性得到证明。然而，当集体变量定义在有限区间时，平稳解仅以有限原子测度的形式在弱意义下存在；微分方程解弱收敛于此测度，但因缺乏清晰准稳态，元动力学在该情形下无效。

Conclusion: 元动力学的有效性依赖于准稳态的存在。在有限区间框架中，只有当区间外的自由能（free energy）在比σ足够大的区域上保持近似恒定时，才能捕捉到准静态的瞬态解（quasi-stationary transient solution）。

Abstract: Metadynamics is a class of enhanced-sampling methods that is widely used in molecular modeling. Here, we consider metadynamics with a one-dimensional collective variable and explore the limitations of using Gaussian hills in light of existing convergence results. We reduce the corresponding evolution problem to a replicator-type differential equation and analyze its long-time behavior. For a periodic collective variable, we prove the convergence of metadynamics. However, the situation is fundamentally different when the collective variable is defined on a finite interval. In this case, the stationary solution only exists in the weak sense as a finite atomic measure. The solution to the differential equation weakly converges to it. Consequently, metadynamics is ineffective due to the absence of a clear quasi-stationary state. A quasi-stationary, transient solution can only be captured in the "INTERVAL" framework if the free energy outside the finite interval remains nearly constant across sufficiently large regions compared to $σ$.

</details>


### [105] [Superintegrability of stratified symplectic spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01953)
*Zhuo Chen,Kai Jiang,Nicolai Reshetikhin,Husileng Xiao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文定义了分层辛空间（stratified symplectic space）上哈密顿系统的超可积性（superintegrability），并证明了 $SU(3)$ 的自旋 Calogero-Moser-Sutherland (sCMS) 系统是超可积的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在建立分层辛空间上超可积性的一般性定义，并将其应用于具体物理系统中验证，以推广超可积性理论。

Method: 通过紧李群的余切丛（cotangent bundle）的哈密顿约化（Hamiltonian reduction）构造分层辛空间作为相空间，并基于此定义超可积性；随后将该框架应用于 sCMS 系统。

Result: 证明了 $SU(3)$ 的自旋 Calogero-Moser-Sutherland (sCMS) 系统在该定义下具有超可积性。

Conclusion: 超可积性的概念可被推广至分层辛空间上的哈密顿系统，且自旋 Calogero-Moser-Sutherland 系统是该理论框架的适用实例。

Abstract: We define the notion of superintegrability of a Hamiltonian system on a stratified symplectic space. We focus on spin Calogero-Moser-Sutherland (sCMS) systems, where the phase space is a stratified symplectic space obtained by the Hamiltonian reduction of the cotangent bundle over a compact Lie group, and demonstrate that the sCMS systems for $SU(3)$ are superintegrable.

</details>


### [106] [Heat transfer problem of a dense gas described by the Enskog equation with a modification of the Enskog factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02276)
*Shigeru Takata,Soma Sakata,Masanari Hattori*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究平行平板之间稠密气体（dense gas）的传热问题，定量比较原始恩斯可格方程（Enskog equation）与一种带有修正恩斯可格因子的变体方程的差异。这种修正版本的主要优势在于具备原始版本所缺乏的H定理。研究发现，该修正对密度、温度和热流矢量的影响十分有限，但在某些严苛参数条件下对应力张量的影响则较为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始恩斯可格方程缺乏H定理，而本论文前作提出了一种带有修正恩斯可格因子的新版本方程，已证明其满足H定理；本文旨在通过稠密气体传热这一具体问题，定量评估该修正对方程预测的宏观物理量所产生的影响程度。

Method: 采用两平行平板之间稠密气体的热传导作为测试场景，对原始恩斯可格方程与修正恩斯可格因子变体方程进行比较求解，通过逐一对比密度、温度、热流矢量和应力张量等宏观量，定量刻画修正带来的影响。

Result: 结果表明，修正因子对密度、温度和热流矢量的影响相当有限；但在某些严苛的参数设置下，对应力张量的影响是清晰可见的。

Conclusion: 修正恩斯可格因子虽然在理论完备性上（H定理的成立）具有优势，但其对多数宏观热传递量的实际影响较小，仅在应力张量等特定量上于极端条件下体现明显偏差，这一结论可为该修正方程在工程与理论应用中的适用性提供参考。

Abstract: Heat transfer in a dense gas between two parallel plates is studied for assessing the quantitative difference of two variants of the Enskog equation, i.e., the original Enskog equation and the Enskog equation with a modified Enskog factor recently proposed in Phys. Rev. E \textbf{111}, 065108 (2025). The main advantage of the latter is that the H theorem, which is lacking in the former, has been established. The influence of the modification is assessed by comparing the macroscopic quantities in details. It is found that the influence of this modification is quite limited in the density, temperature, and heat flow vector. However, the influence on the stress tensor is visible in some severe setting of parameters.

</details>


### [107] [Precise spectral asymptotics, exponential localization, and spectral gap estimates for the three-boson lattice Schrödinger operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02488)
*Abdikhurayra Toshturdiev,Abdumalik Eshniyozov,Janikul Abdullaev,Mikhail Dolgopolov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维整数格点上的三玻色子薛定谔算子（Schrödinger operator）与成对接触相互作用，在强耦合极限下精确刻画了束缚态能量渐近行为、基态波函数的指数局域化上界，以及在准动量π处奇偶子空间的对称性破缺与束缚态结构变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解光学晶格中少玻色子系统的量子模拟需要精确掌握晶格上多体束缚态的谱性质，而格点离散色散（lattice dispersion）的有界性给连续介质之外的解析求解带来挑战，需要发展专门方法。

Method: 采用具有不变子空间分解（invariant subspace decomposition）的Birman–Schwinger算子方法，结合Krein–Rutman定理保证基态的唯一性与严格正性；对零准动量使用渐近展开提取束缚态能级，对π准动量分析奇偶子空间对称性；利用离散Agmon比较方法与Paley–Wiener定理建立波函数指数局域化上界。

Result: 在零总准动量强耦合极限下，基态能量为−3μ+6+O(1/μ)，第一激发态能量为−μ+C+O(1/μ)，其中C≈3.96458由涉及格点格林函数（Green's function）的超越方程确定，谱隙为2μ+O(1)；基态波函数衰变率上界至多为ln(3μ)+O(1/μ)；在准动量π处，奇子空间仅有虚能级，偶子空间仅有唯一束缚态，能量为−2μ+6+8/μ+O(1/μ²)，对应谱隙为μ−2−8/μ+O(1/μ²)。

Conclusion: 零准动量处两个束缚态到π准动量处一个束缚态的约化保持了总谱流（spectral flow），该现象具有晶格特异性，对光学晶格中的少玻色子量子模拟具有直接意义。

Abstract: We study the three-boson Schrodinger operator on the two-dimensional integer lattice with pairwise contact interactions. First, we obtain precise asymptotics of the two bound states below the essential spectrum at zero total quasimomentum in the strong-coupling limit mu to infinity: the ground state energy is -3 mu + 6 + O(1/mu), and the first excited state energy is - mu + C + O(1/mu), where C = 4 - delta approximately 3.96458, with delta determined by the transcendental equation b0(delta) = 1/(1+delta) involving the lattice Green's function. The associated spectral gap is 2 mu + O(1). Second, using the discrete Agmon comparison method and the Paley-Wiener theorem, we establish exponential localization of the ground-state wavefunction with a logarithmic upper bound on the decay rate alpha(mu) at most ln(3 mu) + O(1/mu), reflecting the bounded nature of the lattice dispersion; the sharp asymptotic rate is conjectured. Third, at quasimomentum pi, the parity symmetry is preserved, but the odd quadratic form becomes positive definite with a unique eigenvalue of order 1/mu that never reaches the Birman-Schwinger threshold, so the odd subspace yields only a virtual level. The even subspace supports exactly one bound state with energy -2 mu + 6 + 8/mu + O(1/mu^2), and the spectral gap to the essential spectrum is mu - 2 - 8/mu + O(1/mu^2). The reduction from two bound states at zero to one at pi preserves the total spectral flow and is a lattice-specific phenomenon. Our approach uses an invariant subspace decomposition of the Birman-Schwinger operator and the Krein-Rutman theorem to guarantee uniqueness and strict positivity of the ground state. These results have direct implications for quantum simulation of few-boson systems in optical lattices.

</details>


### [108] [Bimaterial Eshelby's inclusion problem for polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02539)
*Chunlin Wu,Huiming Yin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了双材料域（bimaterial domain）中任意取向多面体夹杂在均匀本征应变（eigenstrain）下的闭式Eshelby张量，通过将体积积分约化为表面积分和初等线积分，补齐了Boussinesq位移势（Boussinesq's displacement potential）对应的缺失域积分，给出了完整的弹性和热弹性双材料Eshelby张量公式，并验证了其正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双材料夹杂解主要限于特殊夹杂形状或仅为膨胀型（dilatational）本征应变，原因在于双材料Green函数中除Kelvin解里的调和与双调和势外还包含两个Boussinesq位移势，导致通用闭式解难以获得。为解决该局限，本文旨在推导任意取向多面体夹杂的一般化闭式Eshelby张量。

Method: 本文将两个Boussinesq位移势对应的体积域积分约化为表面积分与基本线积分，从而得到可直接累加的子项表达式；基于此构造完整的双材料弹性和热弹性Eshelby张量，并辅以奇异性和边界行为分析。

Result: 推导结果与平行于双材料界面的球形、立方体夹杂的解析解以及倾斜立方体的有限元结果一致；奇异性分析表明，当夹杂与界面分离时界面相关贡献保持正则，而当夹杂的边或顶点接触界面时会出现附加对数奇异性（logarithmic singularity），但主奇异阶数不提高。

Conclusion: 本文所提供的闭式公式填补了双材料多面体夹杂在一般均匀本征应变下完整Eshelby张量的空白，在弹性和热弹性分析中可直接使用并适用于任意取向与任意界面位置（包括夹杂与界面相切情形）。

Abstract: This paper presents the closed-form Eshelby's tensor for an arbitrarily oriented polyhedral inclusion in a bimaterial domain under general uniform eigenstrain. Existing bimaterial solutions are mainly restricted to special inclusion shapes or dilatational eigenstrains, because the bimaterial Green's function contains two Boussinesq's displacement potentials in addition to the harmonic and biharmonic potentials in Kelvin's solution. This paper derives the missing domain integrals of the two Boussinesq's potentials by reducing the volume integrals to surface and elementary line integrals. The formulae provide the complete elastic and thermoelastic bimaterial Eshelby's tensors, which are verified against analytical solutions for spherical and cuboidal inclusions parallel to the bimaterial interface, and finite element results of an inclined cuboid. Singularity analysis demonstrates that the interface-related contribution remains regular when the inclusion is separated from the bimaterial interface, while additional logarithmic singularities arise when an edge or vertex touches the interface without increasing the dominant singularity order.

</details>


### [109] [Ruelle--Pollicott Theory for Metastable Systems: A Unified Framework for Tipping Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02614)
*Mickaël D. Chekroun,Valerio Lucarini*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文从Ruelle–Pollicott（RP）谱理论出发，发展了一种针对随机、多稳态和亚稳态系统临界转变（tipping point）预警的一般理论。研究表明预警信号并非仅由RP谱隙闭合决定，而是由RP谱块、可观测量与扰动方向三者共同决定；残差增长可在谱隙完全不变时产生经典预警指标，并可被归一化指标𝒩(f)>1所认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的临界减速（critical slowing down）预警范式仅在简单平衡分岔附近有效，并非随机、多稳态或亚稳态系统的一般理论。作者旨在从Kolmogorov生成元的RP谱出发，建立更一般的临界转变预警理论，以解释谱隙不闭合时预警指标仍可能升高的现象。

Method: 解析求解hypoelliptic Itô扩散的Kolmogorov生成元的Ruelle–Pollicott（RP）谱，将不变统计量对谱块的敏感性分解为谱分母与耦合可观测量及扰动方向的残差（residue）因子。进一步在亚稳态系统中引入被杀死问题（killed problem），得到Doob Q-过程与逃逸时钟（escape clock）等互补谱对象，并定义认证残差驱动的指标𝒩(f)。

Result: 在随机非正规系统中，RP谱完全冻结但经典预警指标仍比谱隙真实闭合时更显著，RP分解将其归因于残差增长。𝒩(f)在可逆动力学下满足𝒩(f)≤1，因而𝒩(f)>1可认证残差驱动的放大。在一维折叠中，井内恢复与井间转变分别按(ε_c−ε)^{1/2}和(ε_c−ε)^{3/2}标度，区分了分岔诱导与噪声诱导的临界转变。Doob漂移还与最优Girsanov采样相联系。

Conclusion: 早期预警信号本质上是RP谱块、可观测量与扰动方向构成的三元组的性质，而非单一谱隙的函数。谱隙闭合既不充分也不必要，残差增长可单独驱动预警；该框架为区分分岔诱导与噪声诱导的临界现象提供了定量判据。

Abstract: Tipping points---abrupt, potentially irreversible reorganizations of a system's statistical state---are commonly anticipated through critical slowing down: recovery slows, autocorrelation and variance rise, and spectra redden. This paradigm is powerful near simple equilibrium bifurcations but is not a general theory for stochastic, multistable, or metastable systems. We develop such a theory from the Ruelle--Pollicott (RP) spectrum of Kolmogorov generators of hypoelliptic Itô diffusions. Resolving the sensitivity of invariant statistics on RP spectral blocks shows that each contribution factorizes into a spectral denominator and a residue coupling the block to both the observable and perturbation direction. Small denominators permit large responses; residues produce them. Thus a closing RP gap is not sufficient for an early warning, while growing residues can generate the classical signature with no gap closure. An early-warning signal is therefore a property of a triple: RP block, observable, and perturbation direction. We demonstrate this on a stochastic non-normal system whose RP spectrum is exactly frozen, yet classical indicators become more alarming than during genuine gap closure. The RP decomposition attributes this to residue growth and yields an index $\mathcal{N}(f)$ satisfying $\mathcal{N}(f)\leq 1$ for reversible dynamics; hence $\mathcal{N}(f)>1$ certifies residue-driven amplification. For metastable systems, one killed problem yields two complementary spectral objects: the Doob $Q$-process isolates in-well recovery, while escape clocks and committor-weighted destination probabilities govern interwell transitions. In a one-dimensional fold, these scale as $(ε_c-ε)^{1/2}$ and $(ε_c-ε)^{3/2}$, separating bifurcation-induced from noise-induced tipping. Doob drift also connects to optimal Girsanov sampling.

</details>


### [110] [Asymptotic long-range order for the XY-model on random geometric graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02618)
*Margherita Disertori,Max Mihailescu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了定义在随机几何图（random geometric graph）上的经典XY模型，证明当逆温度β以适当速率趋于无穷时，该模型在固定随机环境下表现出长程有序（long-range order），即两点函数存在非零下界；该结果以淬冷（quenched）意义成立，即在高概率下长程有序随n增大趋于保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于将经典XY模型的研究从规则或格子图上扩展到随机几何图这一随机环境上，考察该模型在具有欧氏几何结构的随机图上是否仍能展现长程有序，从而推动无序系统中经典自旋模型相变行为的理解。

Method: 方法上要求参数ε随n→∞以足够慢的速率趋于零，使得图G_{n,ε}保持区域Ω的几何信息；随后令逆温度β以依赖于n和ε的速率趋于无穷，通过证明随机环境具有足够正则性（以高概率成立），进而应用凸性论证（convexity argument）和Brascamp–Lieb不等式证明两点函数的下界。

Result: 结果表明，在β→∞且退化速率恰当的条件下，XY模型在G_{n,ε}上存在非零下界的两点函数，即长程有序成立；该结论是淬冷的，意味着长程有序发生的概率随n→∞趋于一。

Conclusion: 该工作将长程有序结果推广至随机几何图上的XY模型，确立了在固定随机环境（淬冷）意义下的相变行为，为后续研究更一般随机几何系统上的经典自旋模型提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the classical $XY$-model on random geometric graphs $\mathcal{G}_{n, \varepsilon}$, which are obtained by sampling $n \in \mathbb{N}$ independent points in a finite domain $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^d$, $d \geq 2$, and connecting two points by and edge if their distance is of order $\varepsilon > 0$. We refer to $\mathcal{G}_{n, \varepsilon}$ as the random environment. Letting $\varepsilon \to 0$ as $n \to \infty$ at a sufficiently slow rate, these graphs capture the geometry of $Ω$.
  Denoting the inverse temperature by $β$, we show that in the limit $β\to \infty$ at a rate depending on $n$ and $\varepsilon$, the $XY$-model on $\mathcal{G}_{n, \varepsilon}$ exhibits long range order in the sense that we prove a lower bound away from zero on the two-point function. Our result is quenched in the random environment: long-range order holds with large probability, converging to one as $n \to \infty$. To prove the statement, we show that with high probability the environment is sufficiently regular to apply a convexity argument and the Brascamp--Lieb inequality.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Holographic algebras at null infinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01706)
*Chang-Han Chen,Geoff Penington,Gautam Satishchandran,Elisa Tabor*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了渐近平直时空（asymptotically flat spacetimes）中与零无穷远（null infinity）切割相关的可观测量代数。作者构造了有限半径、时间涂抹化的Bondi质量算符，将其加入物质与引力子可观测量，发现无论是否存在黑洞，切割附近都产生满足非平凡嵌套关系的Type III₁ von Neumann代数；在闵可夫斯基时空和黑洞时空中分别重建了相应的空间区域，并在极限与正则化情况下获得Type I∞和Type II∞代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bondi质量与未来零无穷远的尖锐切割上并非定义良好的量子算符，其涨落即使在延迟时间涂抹后仍然发散。因此需要构造一种有限半径、时间涂抹化的Bondi质量版本，并研究渐近平直时空中与切割相关的可观测代数结构。

Method: 作者引入有限半径、时间涂抹化的Bondi质量算符，将其与切割附近任意小渐近邻域中的物质和引力子可观测量合并在同一代数中。分别分析含有和不含有黑洞的时空，研究所得von Neumann代数的类型与嵌套关系；在黑洞情况下构造Type II∞正则化并计算其重整化von Neumann熵；在附录中证明Schwarzschild时空中在无穷远处渐近到闵可夫斯基真空的量子场的两个技术性结果（分裂性质与Hartle-Hawking权重的构造）。

Result: 在无黑洞与含黑洞两种情形下，均得到满足非平凡嵌套关系的Type III₁ von Neumann代数。在闵可夫斯基时空中，所得代数重建了与切割相关的类空楔形区域；在稳态黑洞时空中，重建了切割与黑洞分岔面之间的区域，提供了纠缠楔（entanglement wedge）的渐近平直类比。当切割移至过去无穷远极限时，无黑洞时空获得Type I∞代数，黑洞时空获得Type II∞代数。对有限延迟时间切割处的代数，构造了Type II∞正则化，其重整化von Neumann熵与广义熵一致。

Conclusion: 渐近平直时空中与零无穷远切割相关的可观测量代数展现了丰富的类型结构：有限切割处为Type III₁代数，极限与正则化情形下分别呈现Type I∞和Type II∞结构；黑洞时空中的代数重建提供了一种超出AdS/CFT框架的纠缠楔类比，且其熵满足广义熵的Bekenstein-Hawking关系。

Abstract: We construct an algebra of observables associated to a cut of null infinity in asymptotically flat spacetimes. The Bondi mass associated with a sharp cut of future null infinity is not a well-defined quantum operator: its fluctuations diverge even after smearing in retarded time. We instead introduce a finite-radius, time-smeared version of the Bondi mass and adjoin this operator to the matter and graviton observables in an arbitrarily small asymptotic neighborhood of the cut. We separately analyze spacetimes with and without black holes and find, in both cases, Type III$_1$ von Neumann algebras that satisfy non-trivial nesting relations. In Minkowski spacetime, the resulting algebra reconstructs the spacelike wedge associated with the cut. In a stationary black hole spacetime, it reconstructs the region bounded by the cut and the black hole bifurcation surface, providing an asymptotically flat analogue of an entanglement wedge. In the limit as the cut is moved to past infinity, we recover a Type I$_{\infty}$ algebra for spacetimes without black holes and a Type II$_{\infty}$ algebra for black hole spacetimes. For algebras at finite cuts of retarded time, we construct a Type II$_\infty$ regularization of the black hole algebra whose renormalized von Neumann entropy agrees with the generalized entropy. In appendices, we prove two technical results about quantum fields, in a Schwarzschild spacetime, that asymptote to the Minkowski vacuum at infinity: a split property for unbounded, spacelike separated regions and the construction of a faithful, normal, and semifinite Hartle-Hawking weight whose modular flow is Schwarzschild time evolution.

</details>


### [112] [On-shell recursion and soft theorems for worldline quantum field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01714)
*Nathan Castet,Vincent F. He*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在世界线量子场论（worldline quantum field theory, WQFT）中推导了一组外壳递归关系（on-shell recursion relations），用于计算与经典引力观测相关的振幅。通过推导并利用存在世界线时的新软引力子定理（soft-graviton theorem），克服了全直线复动量形变下振幅在大z极限下衰减不足的问题，得到了d维递归公式，可将任意n引力子有理振幅表示为低点振幅的组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WQFT是研究经典引力观测量的有效引力理论，但其中振幅在全直线复动量形变下的衰减性较差，无法直接应用标准的外壳递归方法，因此需要新的技术途径来系统构造该理论的有理振幅。

Method: 作者推导并利用了一个含世界线的新的软引力子定理，该定理推广了领头阶（Weinberg）和次领头阶软定理，额外包含世界线软发射的贡献。基于此定理，推导出d维递归公式，将任意n引力子有理振幅表达为WQFT与纯引力中低点振幅的函数。借助软世界线定理，可从全部引力子振幅获得任意数量的外部世界线涨落振幅，使递归关系足以构造该理论的任意有理振幅。

Result: 作者验证了递归关系的正确性，通过显式计算了两引力子和三引力子振幅进行了检验。

Conclusion: 研究建立了WQFT中有理振幅的递归构造方案，为计算经典引力观测量提供了系统的技术工具。

Abstract: We derive a set of on-shell recursion relations for amplitudes in worldline quantum field theory (WQFT), an effective theory of gravity coupled to a massive worldline which is relevant for computing classical gravitational observables. We overcome the poor large-$z$ falloff of amplitudes under an all-line complex momentum deformation by deriving and exploiting a new soft-graviton theorem in the presence of a worldline. This theorem generalizes the leading (Weinberg) and subleading soft theorems with additional contributions due to soft emission from the worldline. Our main result is a $d$-dimensional recursion formula, expressing any $n$-graviton rational amplitude in terms of lower-point amplitudes from WQFT and pure gravity. Using the soft-worldline theorem, we can obtain amplitudes with any number of external worldline fluctuations from the corresponding all-graviton amplitudes, making the recursion sufficient to construct any rational amplitude of the theory. We verify our results by explicitly computing two-graviton and three-graviton amplitudes.

</details>


### [113] [Truncated Polyakov bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01732)
*Kaushik Kangshabanik,Apratim Kaviraj,Shailesh Lal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在Polyakov自举（Polyakov bootstrap, PB）框架中建立了一种截断数值方法，通过梯度下降优化求解PB求和规则，从而能够迭代地从广义自由场（generalized free field, GFF）的微扰解出发，求解任意谱变形的crossing方程。研究表明，即使不施加幺正性（unitarity）条件，只要与GFF存在光滑连接，一般的crossing解也能被唯一确定。该方法通过单关联函数与混合关联函数的多个示例得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Polyakov自举是一种研究共形场论（CFT）谱的数值方法，但现有的求解策略通常依赖幺正性假设。作者希望探索一个更一般的问题：在没有正性（positivity）条件下，crossing方程的解是否仍然可以被唯一地确定，并为此建立一套不依赖幺正性的数值求解方案。

Method: 作者首先在PB框架中引入截断数值方法，然后利用梯度下降（gradient descent）优化算法对PB求和规则进行数值求解。具体流程为：从广义自由场的微扰解出发，以迭代方式求解任意谱变形下的crossing方程，并在单关联函数（single correlator）和混合关联函数（mixed correlator）两种设定下通过多个示例对该方法进行验证。

Result: 计算结果表明，对于幺正变形（unitary deformations），所得解与极值CFT谱（extremal CFT spectra）一致；更重要的发现是，对于一般性的crossing解——即使不满足幺正性（即不含正性条件）——只要能通过光滑路径连接到GFF，该解就是唯一的。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种有效的截断Polyakov自举数值方法，展示了在广义自由场附近，crossing解的唯一性可以不依赖于幺正性假设而成立。这一结果为在更广泛的非幺正CFT谱空间中探索解的确定性与结构提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: We set up a truncated numerical approach in the Polyakov bootstrap (PB) framework. We employ a gradient descent optimization to solve PB sum rules numerically, and hence solve crossing for arbitrary deformations of the generalized free field (GFF) spectrum in an iterative way starting from a perturbative solution. For unitary deformations the solutions coincide with extremal CFT spectra. But more interestingly, our findings indicate that a general crossing solution, not necessarily unitary (i.e. without positivity), can be uniquely identified by a smooth connection to GFF. The truncated Polyakov bootstrap approach is established through a number of examples in both single and mixed correlator settings.

</details>


### [114] [Pre-Strings Lectures on Holographic Correlators and Analytic Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01986)
*Xinan Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文是Pre-Strings 2026学校五讲课程的扩展讲义，系统介绍了利用bootstrap策略计算全息关联函数（holographic correlators）的进展，并以4维N=4超杨-米尔斯理论为例给出了教学性导引与综合阐述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年中，bootstrap策略已深刻改变了全息关联函数的计算方法，揭示了AdS中正在兴起的散射振幅纲领。作者旨在为这一快速发展的领域提供系统的教学性介绍与综合，帮助读者掌握核心方法论及其多种扩展方向。

Method: 首先回顾共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）基础与AdS微扰论要点；随后以4维N=4超杨-米尔斯（super Yang-Mills）理论中的四点函数为规范示例，详细演示在经典IIB型超引力（Type IIB supergravity）对偶区域中的bootstrap计算策略；最后讨论包括更高点函数、量子修正与弦论修正等多类扩展，以及将bootstrap方法推广至含共形缺陷（conformal defects）的全息CFT。

Result: 文中展示了bootstrap策略在规范示例中的完整计算流程，并概述了若干扩展场景——更高阶关联函数、量子级别的修正、弦论级别的修正，以及不同构型的共形缺陷全息CFT——在这些场景中的应用现状与分析深度各有差异。

Conclusion: bootstrap策略已成为计算全息关联函数的系统性框架，不仅能处理经典超引力区域的四点函数，还可推广至更广义的算符结构、更精细的修正层次以及带缺陷的全息CFT，显示出AdS中散射振幅纲领的雏形与前景。

Abstract: Over the past decade, the bootstrap strategy has transformed the computation of holographic correlators and revealed structures suggesting an emerging scattering amplitude program in AdS. These notes, which are an extended version of the five lectures delivered at the Pre-Strings 2026 School, give a pedagogical introduction and synthesis of these developments. We start with a quick reminder of the essentials of CFT and a brief review of AdS perturbation theory. We then demonstrate in detail the bootstrap strategy for computing holographic correlators in the canonical example 4d $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory, at the level of four-point functions and in the regime dual to classical Type IIB supergravity. Several extensions, including higher-point functions, quantum corrections and stringy corrections, are discussed at varying levels of detail. We also explain how the bootstrap strategy can be extended to holographic CFTs with conformal defects, covering a diverse array of different setups.

</details>


### [115] [Unfolded Krylov complexity: universal chaotic dynamics without false positives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02228)
*Johanna Erdmenger,Kyoung-Bum Huh,Hyun-Sik Jeong,Juan F. Pedraza*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出在对谱进行展平（unfolding）处理后再构建克雷洛夫（Krylov）动力学，以解决量子混沌诊断中Krylov态复杂度可能产生的误判问题。展平通过移除非普遍的平滑态密度并保留微观谱关联，能够抑制可积体系中的虚假峰，同时保留混沌体系的普遍谱特征，使展平后的Krylov复杂度成为更可靠的量子多体扰动量诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诊断量子混沌的核心挑战在于区分真正的多体扰动量（many-body scrambling）与谱的动力学效应。Krylov态复杂度（Krylov state complexity，又称spread complexity）虽已被广泛用作混沌的诊断指标，但已有研究表明该判据可能产生假阳性——鞍点主导的可积体系即使缺乏随机矩阵能级关联，也可能出现显著峰值。作者旨在解决这一歧义，提升Krylov复杂度作为量子混沌探针的可靠性。

Method: 作者结合数值与解析两方面的证据，提出在构建后续Krylov动力学之前先对谱进行展平（unfolding）处理。展平操作通过移除非普遍的平滑态密度而保留微观谱关联，从而抑制可积体系中的虚假峰。解析层面，作者将Lanczos迭代表述为正交多项式形式，得到具有稳健近对角结构的精确复杂度核（complexity kernel），其精细特征反映底层谱关联。针对对数模型，展平可精确执行，将谱映射到均匀晶格并得到解析的spread复杂度，从而消除假阳性峰。

Result: 研究结果表明，展平后的Krylov复杂度在可积系统中抑制了虚假峰的出现，同时在混沌系统中保留了普遍的谱特征。对对数模型的精确处理证实了展平方法能够彻底移除假阳性峰。这些结果确立了展平Krylov复杂度作为更可靠的量子多体扰动量探测方案的可行性。

Conclusion: 展平操作解决了Krylov复杂度在混沌诊断中的歧义问题，使其能够更准确地反映真正源于谱微观关联的多体扰动量特征，远离由谱平滑部分导致的运动学效应干扰。

Abstract: A central challenge in diagnosing quantum chaos is to distinguish genuine many-body scrambling from kinematic effects of the spectrum. Krylov state complexity, or spread complexity, has emerged as a powerful diagnostic, with its characteristic growth, peak, and relaxation often taken as signatures of chaos. However, previous work has shown that this criterion can give false positives: saddle-dominated integrable systems may display prominent peaks even without random-matrix level correlations. We argue, based on complementary numerical and analytical evidence, that this ambiguity can be resolved by unfolding the spectrum prior to constructing the ensuing Krylov dynamics. By removing the non-universal smooth density of states while retaining microscopic spectral correlations, unfolding suppresses spurious peaks in integrable systems while preserving the universal spectral signatures of chaotic systems. Analytically, the formulation of the Lanczos iteration in terms of orthogonal polynomials yields an exact complexity kernel with a robust near-diagonal structure whose fine-grained features reflect the underlying spectral correlations. Moreover, for the logarithmic model, unfolding can be performed exactly, mapping the spectrum to a uniform lattice and yielding an analytic spread complexity that removes the false-positive peak. These findings establish unfolded Krylov complexity as a more reliable probe of genuine many-body scrambling.

</details>


### [116] [Residues on Permanent Pinches: Finite Integrals and Leading Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02300)
*Dimitri Corradini,Cristian Vergu,Shun-Qing Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从“永久收缩”（permanent pinch）的视角研究红外（IR）发散，证明通过消去永久收缩可构造有限线性组合的积分，并发展出一种无需引入正则化（regulator）即可计算与方案无关（scheme-independent）的领头红外发散的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统处理红外发散的方式通常依赖引入正则化，但该论文希望找到不借助正则化直接计算领头红外发散的方法，从而提供更本质且与方案无关的理解途径。

Method: 作者从永久收缩的几何结构出发，展示其消去过程允许构建有限线性组合的积分；随后通过沿去奇异化（desingularization）路径对留数形式（residue forms）进行积分来计算与方案无关的领头红外发散。该方法适用于无质量与混合有质量-无质量积分、非平面（non-planar）任意分子结构的积分，以及具有高次传播子幂次的积分。

Result: 作者利用该分析确定了若干盒积分（box integrals）的领头奇异项，并研究了更高圈有限积分的例子，特别地为非平面两圈五点拓扑提供了一组有限的非消失积分的基函数。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何方法为红外发散的领头奇异结构和有限积分基的构造提供了统一、无需正则化的计算框架，拓展了现有红外分析工具的适用范围。

Abstract: We study infrared (IR) divergences from the point of view of permanent pinches. We show how canceling permanent pinches allows us to form finite linear combinations of integrals. We further show how to compute scheme-independent leading IR divergences with- out introducing a regulator, by integrating certain residue forms along desingularizations of permanent pinch varieties. Our methods work for massless and mixed massive-massless inte- grals, for non-planar integrals with arbitrary numerators as well as for integrals with higher (integer) powers of propagators. We use this analysis to determine leading singular terms in some examples of box integrals. We further study examples of finite integrals at higher loops, and in particular provide a basis for finite non-evanescent integrals in a non-planar two-loop five-point topology.

</details>


### [117] [QFT on flat, compact, orientable manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02330)
*Sebestyen Nagy,Daniel Nogradi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文围绕有限温度下旋转时空可由特定的平坦（flat）、紧致（compact）、可定向（orientable）四维流形（4-manifolds）描述这一近期观测，系统列举并分类了所有此类流形。总计存在26个平坦、紧致、可定向的四维流形，其中23个为自旋流形（spin manifolds），因此可在其上定义费米子与Dirac算子；其中7个适用于有限温度下发生旋转的有限体积周期盒子（periodic boxes）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期有观测指出有限温度下旋转时空可由不同于 $\mathbb T^4$ 的平坦紧致可定向流形来描述，但此前缺乏对这类流形的完整系统处理。本文的动机在于系统地目录化所有此类四维流形并考察其性质，以支撑有限体积旋转系统的物理研究。

Method: 本文采用数学分类方法，逐一枚举所有平坦、紧致、可定向的四维流形，并检验其是否具备自旋结构（spin structure），以判断能否定义费米子与Dirac算子；同时甄别其中适用于有限温度旋转有限周期盒子的流形子集。

Result: 共识别出26个平坦、紧致、可定向的四维流形，其中23个为自旋流形，可在其上定义费米子与Dirac算子。在此集合中，有7个流形适合描述有限体积周期盒子在有限温度下的旋转情形。

Conclusion: 该分类结果表明，除通常的 $\mathbb T^4$ 外，存在丰富的平坦紧致可定向四维流形可用于旋转时空的格点或有限体积描述，且多数支持自旋结构，为费米子在旋转系中的研究提供了可行的几何框架。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent observation that rotating space-times at finite temperature can be described by particular flat, compact, orientable manifolds, which are different from $\mathbb T^4$, we catalogue all such 4-manifolds and their properties. There are in total 26 flat, compact, orientable 4-manifolds, 23 of them spin-manifolds, so fermions and a Dirac operator can also be defined on these. Seven of them are appropriate for finite volume periodic boxes undergoing rotation at finite temperature.

</details>


### [118] [Surprises of Klein paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02370)
*T. R. Ahmadov,E. T. Akhmedov,V. I. Lapushkin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了1+1维二次量子化的狄拉克费米子（Dirac fermions）在背景电场中的行为。作者发现，当两个平行电容器分别承载相反方向的电场，且势能差满足特定条件（0<V<2m）时，即使每个电容器单独都会产生施温格效应（Schwinger effect）粒子对并形成反向电流，系统在 t→+∞ 时电流算符的极限期望值实际上为零。只有 V>2m 时才出现非零电流。作者还将该理论扩展到存在无穷墙和深势阱的情形进行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于对施温格效应的常规物理理解，两个方向相反的平行电容器各自产生粒子对和方向相反、大小不等的电流，总电流不应为零。本文旨在检验这一直观认知是否正确，并探讨该观察与施温格效应及超临界核（supercritical nuclei）衰变的联系。

Method: 作者考虑1+1维、质量为m的二次量子化狄拉克费米子在背景电场中的情形，构造两个平行电容器模型，势能差分别为V0>2m和V+V0（0<V<2m）。在瞬时开启上述势能形式的条件下，通过分析电流算符在 t→+∞ 时的极限期望值来评估总电流。

Result: 当0<V<2m时，即使每个电容单独都会产生施温格效应粒子对，总电流的极限期望值在 t→+∞ 时实际为零；仅当V>2m时才获得非零电流。对于包含无穷墙（infinite wall）和深势阱 V0>2m 的情形，在高斯近似（Gaussian approximation）下电流的极限期望值同样为零。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，对施温格效应中反向电流不能抵消的朴素预期过于简单化。电流能否非零取决于参数V是否超过2m的阈值，该观察与施温格效应及超临界核的衰变存在密切关系。

Abstract: We consider second quantized $1+1$ dimensional Dirac fermions with mass $m$ in background electric fields. This theory mimics the following situations. We consider two parallel capacitors with opposite directions of the electric field $E$ in them. The potential energy difference in one of them is equal to $V_0 > 2m$ and in the other to $V+V_0$, with $0<V<2m$. According to general physical understanding of the Schwinger effect, each capacitor separately will create pairs and there will be oppositely directed currents, which will not be equal to each other. The total current will not vanish. In this paper we show that this is too naive an observation: the limiting expectation value of the current operator, as $t\to +\infty$, is actually zero, if the above form of the potential with $0<V<2m$ is instantaneously turned on. The non-vanishing current is obtained only if $V>2m$. We also consider the same theory in the presence of the infinite wall and a deep well $V_0>2m$. We show that in such a potential the limiting expectation value of the current is zero in the Gaussian approximation. We discuss the relation of these observations to the Schwinger effet and to the decay of supercritical nuclei.

</details>


### [119] [Localization and Abelianization of Strings on Group Manifolds: The Simply Connected Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02425)
*Yongchao Lü*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本研究对紧致连通单连通简单李群上环面维兹-祖米诺-维顿（WZW）模型配分函数的局域化公式进行了考察，发现原始Murthy-Witten局域化处理中缺失了一个因子，并证明其与哈密顿形式得到的结果一致。该缺失因子的起源可追溯至Wess-Zumino振幅的阿贝尔化，其约化为与极大环面相关的Narain格点CFT中的平坦Kalb-Ramond B-场和乐，从而建立了局域化结果与相应阿贝尔理论的格点描述之间的直接联系。此外，作者分析了环面配分函数的点粒子极限，在该极限下WZW模型退化为群流形上的量子力学，并重现了Frenkel的热核迹公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在修正WZW模型环面配分函数局域化方法中缺失的因子，并建立局域化结果与格点共形场论描述之间的一致性联系。

Method: 采用局域化计算与哈密顿形式方法的对比分析，通过追踪Wess-Zumino振幅的阿贝尔化过程定位缺失因子的来源，并利用Narain格点CFT中的平坦Kalb-Ramond B-场和乐对其进行表征；最后通过点粒子极限约化到群流形上的量子力学来验证结果。

Result: 识别出原始局域化处理中缺失的关键因子，证实该因子与哈密顿形式化结果一致；将其起源归结为Wess-Zumino振幅约化为极大环面上Narain格点CFT中的flat B-场和乐；在点粒子极限下成功重现了Frenkel热核迹公式。

Conclusion: 局域化方法与哈密顿形式之间存在此前未被识别的缺失因子，该因子的阿贝尔化起源揭示了局域化结果与阿贝尔理论格点描述的直接关系，且点粒子极限的一致性验证进一步确认了修正后公式的正确性。

Abstract: We study the localization formula for the partition function of the Wess--Zumino--Witten (WZW) model on the torus for compact, connected, and simply connected simple Lie groups. We identify a missing factor in the original localization treatment of Murthy and Witten and show that it agrees with the result obtained from the Hamiltonian formulation. We trace its origin to the abelianization of the Wess--Zumino amplitude, which reduces to a flat Kalb--Ramond B--field holonomy in a Narain lattice CFT associated with the maximal torus. This establishes a direct relation between the localization result and the lattice description of the corresponding abelian theory. We further analyze the point--particle limit of the torus partition function, in which the WZW model reduces to quantum mechanics on the group manifold, and reproduce Frenkel's heat--kernel trace formula.

</details>


### [120] [An Infinite Family of Non-Rational VOAs from Strongly Coupled 4d Higgsless SCFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02437)
*Hongliang Jiang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一族具有平凡Higgs分支的强耦合四维$\mathcal N=2$超共形场论（SCFT），即$(A_2,D_{3m+1})$ Argyres-Douglas理论，提出其对应的顶点算子代数（VOA）为双代数（doublet algebra）$\mathcal A(4m+2)$，并通过多种非平凡检验加以验证。该理论还通过对角规范（diagonal gauging）实现，其Schur指标与$\mathcal N=4$ $SU(2)$超杨-米尔斯理论存在关联。结果揭示了无Higgs分支SCFT、对角规范与非理性顶点算子代数之间的系统联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合超共形场论的数学结构（尤其是其对应的顶点算子代数与非理性代数性质）尚未被充分理解，需要为具有平凡Higgs分支的SCFT族寻找精确的代数描述。

Method: 提出$(A_2,D_{3m+1})$ Argyres-Douglas理论对应的VOA为双代数$\mathcal A(4m+2)$；利用双对角规范构造实现该理论并用之计算Schur指标（Schur index）；通过与共形反常$a$、$c$及超特征（supercharacter）的精确比较进行验证；并推广到两参数族$(A_{2s},D_{(2s+1)m+1})$。

Result: 从VOA精确重现了四维共形反常$a$和$c$，并分析证明了Schur指标与VOA超特征的严格相等性；发现$(A_2,D_{3m+1})$的Schur指标与$\mathcal N=4$ $SU(2)$超杨-米尔斯理论一致（相差整体前因子与适当的逃逸度同一化）；子族$(A_{2s},D_{2s+2})$中四维共形反常满足$a=c$。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了无Higgs分支SCFT、对角规范与具有有限强生成集但非理性的顶点算子代数之间的系统性联系，为理解和分类平凡Higgs分支的SCFT族提供了新的数学框架。

Abstract: We study an infinite family of strongly coupled four-dimensional $\mathcal N=2$ superconformal field theories (SCFTs) distinguished by a trivial Higgs branch, known as the $(A_2,D_{3m+1})$ Argyre-Douglas theories. We propose that their associated vertex operator algebras (VOAs) are the doublet algebras $\mathcal A(4m+2)$, an infinite family of non-rational vertex operator superalgebras with a remarkably simple strong generating set of only three fields. We provide several highly nontrivial checks of this proposal. In particular, we reproduce the four-dimensional conformal anomalies $a$ and $c$ from the VOA and analytically prove the exact equality between the Schur index of the SCFT and the supercharacter of the VOA. A key ingredient is a diagonal-gauging realization of the $(A_2,D_{3m+1})$ theories in terms of two simpler building blocks, which makes the Schur-index computation tractable. Remarkably, we find that the resulting Schur index of the $(A_2,D_{3m+1})$ theory coincides with that of $\mathcal N=4$ $SU(2)$ Super-Yang-Mills theory, up to an overall prefactor and an appropriate identification of fugacities. We also discuss a generalization to the two-parameter family $(A_{2s},D_{(2s+1)m+1})$, whose members likewise have trivial Higgs branches and admit diagonal-gauging realizations. A particularly interesting subfamily is $(A_{2s},D_{2s+2})$, for which the four-dimensional conformal anomalies coincide, $a=c$. Our results reveal a systematic connection between Higgsless SCFTs, diagonal gauging, and strongly finite but non-rational vertex operator algebras.

</details>


### [121] [Localization and Abelianization of Strings on Group Manifolds: The Non-simply Connected Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02472)
*Yongchao Lü*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了目标流形为非单连通紧致连通单李群流形上的Wess–Zumino–Witten（WZW）模型配分函数。基于Felder–Gawędzki–Kupiainen（FGK）模不变配分函数，作者推导出局域化公式，揭示了由目标群非平凡拓扑引起的多种拓扑效应，包括FGK余循环控制的Wess–Zumino振幅以及由相对Rochlin不变量捕捉的Pfaffian线丛整体反常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于WZW模型配分函数的研究多聚焦于单连通目标群情形，而对于目标李群流形非单连通的情况，模不变配分函数中拓扑贡献的系统性描述仍不清晰。作者旨在为这类更一般情形提供一种统一的分层描述，厘清Wess–Zumino振幅和费米子Pfaffian中各项拓扑效应的来源与结构。

Method: 作者从FGK构造的模不变配分函数出发，推导出一个局域化公式；该公式也可通过对相应超对称WZW模型进行超对称局域化（supersymmetric localization）直接获得。推导中引入了FGK余循环来描述Wess–Zumino振幅，并通过Pfaffian线丛的完整群及其相对Rochlin不变量来刻画整体反常。

Result: 结果表明，对于目标群为非单连通李群流形的WZW模型，其配分函数的拓扑贡献由两类效应共同决定：一是由FGK余循环控制的Wess–Zumino振幅，二是由相对Rochlin不变量捕捉的Pfaffian线丛整体反常（global anomaly）。这些结果统一描述了非单连通目标群情形下半经典局域化中的全部拓扑贡献。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了非单连通目标流形上WZW模型配分函数的统一拓扑框架，将FGK余循环与相对Rochlin不变量两个数学结构在物理局域化描述中联系起来。该框架为超对称局域化方法在更广拓扑背景下的应用以及相关拓扑不变量物理解释提供了依据。

Abstract: We study the partition functions of Wess--Zumino--Witten (WZW) models with compact connected simple Lie group manifolds that are not simply connected. Starting from the modular-invariant partition function of Felder--Gawędzki--Kupiainen (FGK), we derive a localization formula, which can also be obtained directly by supersymmetric localization of the corresponding supersymmetric WZW model. Our results reveal a variety of topological effects associated with the nontrivial topology of the target group. In particular, the Wess--Zumino amplitude is governed by FGK cocycles, while the fermion Pfaffians exhibit global anomalies associated with the holonomies of Pfaffian line bundles, captured by relative Rochlin invariants. Together, these results provide a unified description of the topological contributions to the semiclassical localization of WZW models with non-simply connected target groups.

</details>


### [122] [Universal fingerprint of topological defect cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02521)
*Zi-Qiang Zhao,Nayun Jia,Zhang-Yu Nie,Hua-Bi Zeng,Jing-Fei Zhang,Xin Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文发现拓扑缺陷中在k>1/ξ区域存在一个普适标度律，即形状因子S_f ∝ k^{-(d+p+2)}，并从数学上证明该指数源于缺陷核心处普遍存在的V形尖点，与具体系统和动力学无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑缺陷在物理学中广泛存在，从凝聚态物理到早期宇宙均有出现。尽管已存在许多关于拓扑缺陷之间关联的普适标度律（如Porod标度），但拓扑缺陷核心的特征指纹此前基本未被探索。

Method: 作者通过解析证明，缺陷核心处普遍的V形尖点（V-shaped cusp）结构产生了形状因子的普适指数d+p+2，其中d为空间维度，p为缺陷余维数（codimension）。随后在四类典型系统中进行数值模拟验证：弱耦合区的时间依赖Ginzburg-Landau方程和Gross-Pitaevskii方程、强耦合区的规范/引力对偶（gauge/gravity duality）模型，以及宇宙学Friedmann-Robertson-Walker背景下的Klein-Gordon方程。

Result: 数值模拟在四种截然不同的物理框架（涵盖弱耦合与强耦合、凝聚态与宇宙学）中均精确验证了该普适标度律，表明该标度律不依赖于具体的底层系统与动力学机制。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究从超导体到宇宙学相变等各种系统中的拓扑缺陷提供了一种全新的探测手段。

Abstract: Topological defects are ubiquitous in physics, arising from condensed matter physics to the early universe. Although there exist many universal scaling laws for correlations between topological defects, such as Porod scaling, the fingerprint of topological defect cores has remained largely unexplored. Here, we discover a universal scaling law in the region $k>1/ξ$, where $ξ$ is the healing length of the topological defects, taking the scaling of the form factor $S_f \propto k^{-(d+p+2)}$, where $d$ is the spatial dimension and $p$ is the defect codimension. We analytically prove that this exponent originates from a universal V-shaped cusp at the defect core and is independent of the underlying system and dynamics. Numerical simulations verify this scaling law in four typical frameworks: the time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau and Gross-Pitaevskii equations in the weak-coupling regime, the gauge/gravity duality model in the strong-coupling regime, and the Klein-Gordon equation in the Friedmann-Robertson-Walker background in cosmology. Our work provides a new probe for studying topological defects in systems ranging from superconductors to cosmological phase transitions.

</details>


### [123] [Scale-separated AdS$_2$ flux vacua from type II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02814)
*George Tringas,Timm Wrase*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文首次构造了具有参数化尺度分离（parametric scale separation）的 AdS₂ 通量真空（flux vacua）。通过将 II 型弦理论在 G₂ 结构轨形乘以圆的紧化上，配合涂抹的 O1/O5 或 O4/O8 平面以及 NSNS 和 RR 通量，作者推导出二维膨胀-引力有效理论，并发现无损的有界通量可在 IIB 和 IIA 中提供参数化控制，使弦耦合变弱、内部半径变大、Kaluza–Klein 尺度与 AdS₂ 曲率尺度分离，同时识别出保持二维 N=(1,1) 超对称的参数分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于回答紧化内部尺度能否从外部曲率尺度参数化解耦的问题，并探索通过全息方式探测这类真空的可能性。

Method: 作者将 II 型弦理论在 G₂ 结构轨形乘以圆上紧化，引入涂抹的时空填充 O1/O5 平面或 O4/O8 平面，并加入 NSNS 和 RR 通量；推导二维膨胀-引力有效理论，分析十维 Killing-自旋方程，并借助 T-对偶讨论其他紧化构型。

Result: 在 IIB 中，无界 F₅ 和 H₇ 通量提供参数化控制；在 IIA 中，无界 F₄、F₈ 和 H₇ 通量提供参数化控制。对于大通量量子数，弦耦合参数化变弱，所有内部体半径在弦单位中参数化变大，Kaluza–Klein 尺度与 AdS₂ 曲率尺度分离。识别出的真空分支保持 N=(1,1) 超对称。

Conclusion: 结果表明这些几何上尺度分离的 AdS₂ 真空与扩展超对称理论中尺度分离的 no-go 定理兼容；T-对偶还产生其他类型 IIB/IIA 在 G₂ 结构空间乘以圆以及 SU(3) 结构空间乘以二维环面上的紧化。

Abstract: Motivated by the question of whether the internal scales of a compactification can be parametrically decoupled from the external curvature scale, and by the possibility of probing such vacua holographically, we construct the first AdS$_2$ flux vacua with parametric scale separation. We compactify type II string theory on a $G_2$ structure orbifold times a circle in the presence of smeared spacetime-filling O1/O5-planes or O4/O8-planes, together with NSNS and RR fluxes. We derive the two-dimensional dilaton-gravity effective theory and exhibit families of vacua in which unbounded $F_5$ and $H_7$ fluxes provide parametric control in type IIB, while unbounded $F_4$, $F_8$, and $H_7$ fluxes provide parametric control in type IIA. For large flux quanta, the string coupling becomes parametrically weak, all internal bulk radii become parametrically large in string units, and the Kaluza--Klein scale separates from the $\mathrm{AdS}_2$ curvature scale. We analyze the ten-dimensional Killing-spinor equations and identify parametric branches preserving $\mathcal N=(1,1)$ supersymmetry in two dimensions. We explain how our geometrically scale-separated AdS$_2$ vacua are compatible with a recent no-go theorem for scale separation in theories with extended supersymmetry. Finally, we discuss various T-dualities leading to other type IIB and type IIA compactifications on $G_2$ structure spaces times a circle and $SU(3)$ structure spaces times a two-torus.

</details>


### [124] [Zero-damped modes of near-extremal Reissner--Nordström black holes from exact WKB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02816)
*Prisco Lo Chiatto,Sebastian Schenk,Nils Wagner,Felix Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明精确WKB（exact WKB）方法为分析近极端Reissner–Nordström黑洞的近零阻尼模（ZDMs）谱提供了一个极为强大的框架：作者完整构建了径向本征值问题对应的Stokes几何，建立了精确量子化条件，并通过Voros符号的解析计算获得了较以往研究更高阶的谱精度，且该方法可系统化改进。此项研究为对其它系统的ZDM谱进行精确WKB分析提供了概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近极端黑洞（near-extremal black holes）晚时段的铃宕（ringdown）动力学预期由近零衰减模（zero-damped modes, ZDMs）主导，这类模的衰减率相对于普通拟正规模（quasinormal modes）会出现参数化的抑制，因此需要精确且系统可改进的方法来解析其谱结构。

Method: 作者将精确WKB方法应用于近极端RN黑洞背景上无质量中性标量场的径向本征值问题，完整给出该问题的Stokes几何，并由此建立精确量子化条件（exact quantization condition, EQC）。对EQC中出现的Voros符号进行了解析计算，实现了对ZDM谱的高阶精度解析。

Result: 作者提出的Voros符号解析计算在ZDM谱上达到了相比以往研究更高阶的精度，且该方案在构造上是系统可改进的（systematically improvable）。

Conclusion: 该工作作为概念验证（proof of concept），表明精确WKB方法可推广用于其他系统ZDM谱的解析研究。

Abstract: The late-time ringdown dynamics of near-extremal black holes (BHs) are expected to be dominated by zero-damped modes (ZDMs), whose decay rates are parametrically suppressed relative to those of ordinary quasinormal modes. In this paper, we demonstrate that exact WKB methods provide an exceptionally powerful framework for analyzing the ZDM spectrum of near-extremal Reissner--Nordström (RN) BHs. Focusing on massless, neutral scalar modes propagating on an RN background, we present the full Stokes geometry derived from the radial eigenvalue problem and establish the corresponding exact quantization condition (EQC). Our analytic computation of the Voros symbols entering the EQC achieves higher-order accuracy for the ZDM spectrum compared to previous studies and is systematically improvable. Ergo, this work serves as a proof of concept for investigations of ZDM spectra of other systems using exact WKB methods.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Asymmetric optical transmission from broken interface-orientation symmetry in self-shadowed grating metallizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01755)
*Can Yesilyurt*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种无需多层叠加（overlay-free）的单步方法实现互易光学结构中有限角度照明的非对称透射：通过在传统衍射光栅上倾斜镀银，利用弹道自遮蔽（ballistic self-shadowing）效应生长出单侧百叶窗状银轮廓，从实验和理论两方面证明该结构可实现基底侧优先的宽带非对称透射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互易光学结构通常被认为是对称的，但研究者发现当两个照明方向与可利用衍射通道的耦合方式不同时，可实现照明方向的非对称透射；本文旨在通过简单的单步制造工艺实现这一效应，而无需复杂的多层堆叠结构。

Method: 在传统衍射光栅表面进行倾斜角度的银金属化沉积，利用弹道自遮蔽效应在一步内生长出单侧百叶窗状银剖面；通过电子显微镜与银元素图谱验证银的单侧重新分布，并结合全波麦克斯韦（full-wave Maxwell）计算对透射行为进行理论预测。

Result: 理论计算预测宽带基底侧优先透射：在衍射区由横磁（TM）偏振承载（对比度最高达2.1），在几何光学区则偏振无关且光谱平坦（比值1.5–1.9）；被拒绝的功率主要重定向为镜面反射，沉积角度控制效应幅度与符号，最佳角度接近45°。实验中，45°镀膜的1μm周期样品在双输出分析器投影下得到暗校正基底侧优先比值最高达1.8；30μm周期样品比值处于计算预测的几何区范围内（1.5–1.8）。

Conclusion: 倾斜金属化单步制备的百叶窗状银结构在互易光学框架内实现了可持续的非对称透射，实验结果与理论预测一致，表明该方法可作为实现宽带方向选择性光学器件的简便途径。

Abstract: Reciprocal optical structures can transmit finite-angle illumination asymmetrically when the two illumination directions couple differently to the available diffraction channels. We realize this in a single overlay-free step: oblique metallization of a conventional diffraction grating, where ballistic self-shadowing grows a one-sided, louver-like silver profile. Electron microscopy and Ag mapping show the one-sided silver redistribution on oblique-coated films and its absence on a normal-incidence control. Full-wave Maxwell calculations on the ballistic-growth geometry predict broadband substrate-favored transmission, carried by the transverse-magnetic (TM) polarization in the diffraction regime (contrast up to 2.1) and polarization-independent and spectrally flat (1.5-1.9) deep in the geometric regime; the rejected power is redirected predominantly into specular reflection, and the deposition angle selects the magnitude and sign of the effect (optimum near $45^\circ$). Calibrated three-color sample-reversal measurements yield dark-corrected substrate-favored ratios that persist in both output-analyzer projections on a $45^\circ$-coated 1-$μ$m-period film (up to 1.8) and lie within the computed geometric-regime band on a 30-$μ$m-period film (1.5-1.8, with reference-normalized absolute transmittances).

</details>


### [126] [Multidimensional Light Detection with Symmetry-engineered Heterojunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02161)
*Yucai Lin,Yaoqiang Zhou,Ruijuan Tian,Faisal Ahmed,Andreas C. Liapis,Youqiang Huang,Yawei Dai,Yuwei Chen,Weiwei Cai,Zongyin Yang,Weida Hu,Tawfique Hasan,Luojun Du,Zhipei Sun*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究通过在范德瓦尔斯异质结（van der Waals heterojunction）中打破旋转和反演对称性，实现了面积仅10微米×10微米的超紧凑多维光电探测器，可同时进行宽带偏振分辨与光谱分辨的光检测，在全斯托克斯偏振重构中总体均方根误差低于0.05，并能分辨间隔0.4纳米的谱峰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型化多维光检测（涵盖全斯托克斯偏振测量与光谱测量）在超紧凑平台上具有潜在应用价值，但现有方案仅能独立实现偏振或光谱检测，同时实现高维光检测仍是阻碍其走向完全集成的突出挑战。

Method: 通过对称性工程打破旋转对称性和反演对称性，在范德瓦尔斯异质结中引入非平凡的量子几何与拓扑特征，设计并制备超紧凑多维光电探测器，以电学可调谐的方式实现偏振与光谱的联合检测。

Result: 该器件有效面积仅10微米×10微米，可重构全斯托克斯偏振且总体均方根误差低于0.05，并可分辨间隔0.4纳米的谱峰；相比此前仅能检测单一模态的范德瓦尔斯材料器件，实现了双模态同时检测，这在单像素探测器中前所未有。

Conclusion: 该工作将高保真偏振测量与亚纳米级光谱分析统一于单个电学可调谐结中，消除了级联探测架构的需求，为集成光子学、量子信息处理和精密成像中的多维探测器阵列奠定了基础。

Abstract: Miniaturised multidimensional light detection, encompassing full-Stokes polarimetry and spectroscopy in ultracompact footprints, is attracting growing interest for its potential to capture a comprehensive set of light properties in portable platforms. Although significant progress has been made in miniaturised schemes for independent polarisation and spectral detection, achieving simultaneous high-dimensional light detection remains an outstanding challenge that limits their development toward full integration. We overcome this limitation by breaking the rotational and inversion symmetries in a symmetry-engineered van der Waals heterojunction to realise an ultracompact multidimensional photodetector. The dual symmetry breaking gives rise to non-trivial quantum geometric and topological features, enabling simultaneous broadband polarisation- and spectrum-resolved light detection, in contrast to previous van der Waals material-based devices, which could detect only one of these modalities. Our device, with an effective area of only 10 micron x 10 micron, reconstructs full-Stokes polarisation with overall root-mean-square errors below 0.05 and resolves spectral peaks separated by 0.4 nm, capabilities not previously achieved in single-pixel detectors. By unifying high-fidelity polarimetry and sub-nanometre spectroscopy in a single electrically tunable junction, our work eliminates the need for cascaded detection architectures and establishes a foundation for multidimensional detector arrays for integrated photonics, quantum information processing, and precision imaging.

</details>


### [127] [Exceptional Topological Signatures of Non-Hermitian Photonic Hopf-Link Braids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02589)
*Samit Kumar Gupta*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个广义Hatano-Nelson模型，该模型包含高阶非互易跳跃、非阿贝尔规范场和交错增益-损耗过程，展示了Hopf-link辫子（Hopf-link braids）的例外拓扑结构，并经历了由例外点（exceptional point, EP）介导的拓扑相变，拓展了非厄米拓扑物理的拓扑景观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米物理赋予非阿贝尔系统以非交换编织模式为特征的例外拓扑；不同且相互竞争的非厄米来源之间相互作用可能引发新颖的拓扑效应，因此作者希望探究多重非互易通道和增益-损耗过程共同作用下的复杂拓扑结构及其相变行为。

Method: 作者构建了一个包含高阶非互易跳跃项、非阿贝尔规范场以及交错增益-损耗过程的广义Hatano-Nelson模型；利用双正交本征向量追踪技术研究例外相边界结构，并通过最大Petermann因子在相位角参数平面上绘制例外边界形态演化的相图。

Result: 混合多个非互易通道可将系统驱动进入高度复杂、嵌套的复能量Hopf-link辫子结构，并将拓扑景观扩展至更高阶辫子区域；增益-损耗非厄米性可驱动拓扑交叉，使扩展的例外轮廓收窄为孤立区域；交错增益-损耗与多重非互易通道的竞争可实现EP介导的拓扑相变。

Conclusion: 对Hopf-link辫子的EP介导拓扑相变及其丰富例外相位形态的演示，可能为鲁棒、容错的通信信道和量子计算平台提供新的物理见解和潜在应用前景。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian physics endows the non-Abelian systems with exceptional topology characterized by non-commutative braid patterns. Interplay of distinct competing sources of non-Hermiticity may induce novel topological effects. Here, we provide a generalized Hatano-Nelson model with higher-order nonreciprocal hoppings, non-Abelian gauge fields, and staggered gain-loss processes showing the exceptional topological structure of the Hopf-link braids that undergoes a EP-mediated topological phase transition. We demonstrate that mixing multiple nonreciprocal channels drives the system into highly intricate, nested complex energy Hopf-link braids and expands the topological landscape up to higher-order braiding sectors. Furthermore, utilizing biorthogonal eigenvector tracking, we map the structural evolution of the exceptional phase boundaries via the maximum Petermann factor in the phase angle parameter planes. We show that the gain-loss non-Hermiticity drives a topological crossover where the extended exceptional contours constrict into isolated regimes. The demonstration of EP-mediated topological phase transition of Hopf-link braids and the associated rich exceptional phase portraits may offer new physical insights with promising applications in robust, fault-tolerant communication channels and quantum computing platforms.

</details>

### [128] [Robin counting functions on cuboids in the critical scaling regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02297)
*Matthias Baur,Simon Larson*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了长方块（cuboid）上Robiin拉普拉斯算子的特征值计数函数在临界情态下的渐近行为，获得了双项渐近展开式，并证明了该情态下第二项在Dirichlet和Neumann渐近之间连续插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Robin拉普拉斯算子的谱计数函数在Robin参数与谱截断耦合时的渐近性质，特别是临界情态下的精细结构。

Method: 将Robin参数设置为与√λ成比例的临界情态，推导计数函数的双项渐近展开，并与Dirichlet和Neumann边界情形进行对比插值分析。

Result: 在临界情态下得到双项渐近展开，其第二项非平凡地依赖比例常数，并在Dirichlet与Neumann对应的第二项之间连续插值；在临界情态之外恢复标准Dirichlet或Neumann渐近。

Conclusion: 建立了临界情态下Robin计数函数的刻画，并证明了当Robin参数与√λ之比超过维度相关阈值时，计数函数满足一个Pólya型不等式。

Abstract: We consider eigenvalue counting functions of Robin Laplace operators on cuboids where the Robin parameter and the spectral cut-off $λ$ are coupled. Our main focus is the critical regime, in which the Robin parameter is proportional to $\sqrtλ$. We obtain a two-term asymptotic expansion for the counting functions in this coupled setting. In the critical regime, the second term in the asymptotic expansion depends non-trivially on the proportionality constant and interpolates continuously between the corresponding second terms for the Dirichlet and Neumann Laplacians. Outside the critical regime, one recovers the corresponding Dirichlet or Neumann asymptotics. We also establish a Pólya-type inequality for the counting function whenever the ratio of the Robin parameter and $\sqrtλ$ exceeds a dimension-dependent threshold.

</details>
