### [129] [Membranes and Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02409)
*Alessandro Giacchetto,Rahul Pandharipande,Yannik Schuler*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文针对Calabi-Yau五流形的等变Gromov-Witten（Gromov–Witten）不变量提出两类猜想：正度数部分与M2-膜相联系，给出模解释并转化为五重Hodge积分的闭式公式；常数映射部分与超引力指标相联系，其生成级数连接到点的Donaldson-Thomas理论。作者在等变参数的两种极限下证明了这些猜想，并顺带获得若干新的三重Hodge积分闭式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Calabi-Yau五流形的等变Gromov-Witten不变量在物理上预期与M2-膜相关，但缺乏精确的数学表述；常数映射部分缺乏对应的解释框架。本文将第一类预期精确化，并联合两类图景推导可验证的具体公式。

Method: 作者对支撑在相交于n重点（n-fold point）的多条光滑曲线上的M2-膜提出模解释（modular interpretation）；基于此与超引力指标猜想推导Gromov-Witten不变量的猜想性公式，并将之转化为带点与不带点的五重Hodge积分（quintuple Hodge integrals）闭式表达式。同时引入K理论的Gromov-Witten/Pairs对应（Gromov–Witten/Pairs correspondence），并利用等变参数的极限情形进行验证。

Result: 论文证明这些猜想蕴含局部曲线在度数为一时满足K理论的Gromov-Witten/Pairs对应；常数映射的生成级数与点的Donaldson-Thomas理论建立联系；在等变参数的两个极限下猜想成立；推导出若干新的三重Hodge积分（triple Hodge integrals）闭式公式。

Conclusion: 本文在Calabi-Yau五流形上统一了正度数与常数映射两部分Gromov-Witten不变量的物理解释，为M2-膜模空间的模结构与超引力指标提供了数学上的猜想框架，并在特定极限中给出了证明支持。

Abstract: In positive degree, equivariant Gromov-Witten invariants of Calabi-Yau fivefolds are expected to admit an interpretation in terms of M2-branes, while we conjecture that constant maps are governed by the corresponding supergravity index. We make the first expectation precise by proposing a modular interpretation of M2-branes supported on several smooth curves meeting at an $n$-fold point. Together, the two pictures yield conjectural formulas for the Gromov-Witten invariants, which we translate into closed formulas for pointed and unpointed quintuple Hodge integrals. We show that these conjectures imply a K-theoretic Gromov-Witten/Pairs correspondence for local curves in degree one and link the generating series of constant maps to Donaldson-Thomas theory of points. We also prove the conjectures in two limits of the equivariant parameters. Along the way, we obtain new closed formulas for certain triple Hodge integrals.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII实验利用质心能量在4.128至4.226 GeV间的e+e–对撞数据（对应7.33 fb⁻¹积分亮度），首次搜索重子衰变D_s*+→p n̄。未观测到显著信号，并在90%置信水平下设定其分支比上限为1.3×10⁻⁴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在首次寻找D_s*+介子的重子衰变通道D_s*+→p n̄，以填充该衰变模式研究中的空白，并检验标准模型对相关衰变率的理论预言。

Method: 研究者使用BESIII实验在质心能量4.128–4.226 GeV范围内采集的e⁺e⁻对撞数据（积分亮度7.33 fb⁻¹），对该衰变道进行搜索分析。

Result: 实验未观察到显著信号，因此在90%置信水平（confidence level）下将D_s*+→p n̄的分支比（branching fraction）上限设定为1.3×10⁻⁴。

Conclusion: 该研究完成了对D_s*+→p n̄衰变道的首次实验搜索，给出的分支比上限为1.3×10⁻⁴，为该衰变模式的已有认识提供了实验约束。

Abstract: The first search for the baryonic decay $ D_{s}^{*+} \to \ p \bar{n} $ is performed using $e^+e^-$ collision data taken at center-of-mass energies between 4.128 and 4.226 GeV, collected by the BESIII experiment and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 7.33 fb$^{-1}$. No significant signal is observed, and an upper limit on the branching fraction is set to be $1.3\times 10^{-4}$ at the $90\%$ confidence level.

</details>

Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器采集的$2.712 \times 10^{9}$个$ψ(3686)$事例样本，首次以$11.5σ$的统计显著性观测到$ψ(3686) \to p K^- K_S^0 \bar{Ξ}^0 + c.c.$衰变，其分支比（branching fraction）测得为$(2.84\pm 0.40\pm 0.25) \times 10^{-6}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在扩展关于稀有多奇异数（multi-strange）$ψ(3686)$衰变的实验信息，并为未来相关衰变模式的研究提供实验参考。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器采集的$2.712 \pm 0.014 \times 10^{9}$个$ψ(3686)$事例样本，对$ψ(3686)\to p K^- K_S^0 \bar{Ξ}^0+c.c.$衰变进行信号搜寻与分支比测量。

Result: 该衰变首次被观测到，统计显著性为$11.5σ$；测得分支比为$(2.84\pm 0.40\pm 0.25) \times 10^{-6}$，其中第一项不确定度为统计误差，第二项为系统误差。

Conclusion: 这一测量扩展了稀有多奇异数$ψ(3686)$衰变的实验数据，并为未来相关衰变模式的深入研究提供了重要的实验参照。

Abstract: Using a sample of $(2.712 \pm 0.014) \times 10^{9}$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector, the decay of $ψ(3686)\to p K^- K_S^0 \bar Ξ^0+c.c.$ is observed for the first time with a statistical significance of $11.5σ$. The branching fraction of this decay is measured to be $(2.84\pm 0.40\pm 0.25) \times 10^{-6}$, where the first and second uncertainties are statistical and systematic, respectively. This measurement extends the experimental information on rare multi-strange $ψ(3686)$ decays and provides an experimental reference for future studies of related decay modes.

</details>

Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于LHCb探测器在2015–2018年收集的质心能量13 TeV的质子-质子对撞数据，通过B_s⁰介子衰变至D_s⁻π⁺态的干涉效应检验CPT和洛伦兹不变性（Lorentz invariance）等基本对称性。在标准模型扩展（Standard Model Extension, SME）框架下，对洛伦兹破缺参数Δa_μ的灵敏度达到O(10⁻¹⁴) GeV量级，并在无洛伦兹不变性破缺假设下测得B_s⁰系统的CPT破缺参数z，给出迄今最精确的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基本对称性（如CPT和洛伦兹不变性）的检验可为超越标准量子场论框架的新物理提供灵敏探针。现有模型中这些对称性被视为普适的，但可能的破缺需要高精度实验加以检验。

Method: 利用LHCb探测器在2015–2018年收集的质子-质子对撞数据（质心能量13 TeV，积分亮度6 fb⁻¹），通过B_s⁰介子衰变到D_s⁻π⁺态中的干涉效应来研究可能的对称性破缺。结果在标准模型扩展（Standard Model Extension, SME）框架下进行诠释。

Result: 对洛伦兹破缺参数Δa_μ的灵敏度达到O(10⁻¹⁴) GeV量级。在假设无洛伦兹不变性破缺的条件下，测得CPT破缺参数z的实部和虚部分别为Re(z)=0.018±0.016±0.003和Im(z)=0.003±0.004±0.002（第一项为统计不确定度，第二项为系统不确定度）。这些结果为B_s⁰系统中CPT破缺提供了迄今最精确的约束。

Conclusion: 根据摘要内容，本文未报告发现CPT或洛伦兹不变性破缺的显著证据，而是给出了对相关破缺参数最精确的实验约束，深化了在介子衰变系统中对这些基本对称性的实验检验。

Abstract: Tests of fundamental symmetries, such as $C\!PT$ and Lorentz invariance, provide sensitive probes of physics beyond the standard quantum-field-theory framework. Possible violations of these symmetries are investigated through interference effects in decays of $B_s^0$ mesons to $D_s^-π^{+}$ states. The analysis uses proton-proton collision data collected with the LHCb detector in 2015--2018 at a centre-of-mass energy of $13~\mathrm{TeV}$, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $6~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$. The results are interpreted within the Standard Model Extension framework, yielding sensitivities to the Lorentz-violating parameter $Δa_μ$ at the ${\cal O}(10^{-14})\,\mathrm{GeV}$ level. Without assuming Lorentz-invariance violation, the $C\!PT$-violating parameter $z$ in the $B_s^0$ system is measured to be $\mathcal{R}e(z) = 0.018 \pm 0.016 \pm 0.003$ and $\mathcal{I}m(z) = 0.003 \pm 0.004 \pm 0.002$, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second systematic. These results represent the most precise constraints on $C\!PT$ violation in the $B_s^0$ system.

</details>

Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器采集的约(10087±44)×10⁶个J/ψ事件，利用超子（hyperon）衰变产生的Λ和反Λ超子与束流管材料靶发生非弹性散射，首次测量了Λ+p→Σ⁰+p与反Λ+p→反Σ⁰+p过程，并获得了反超子非弹性散射的首个证据（3.1σ显著性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超子-核子与反超子-核子相互作用对理解强相互作用的低能行为至关重要，但此前缺乏直接实验数据，尤其是反超子参与的非弹性散射过程尚无实验观测。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器收集的J/ψ→Λ反Λ衰变中产生的超子作为有效束流（effective beam），以束流管材料作为靶。一方面测量Λ+⁹Be→Σ⁰+p+⁸Li及对应的反超子过程，并在表面主导（surface-dominated）散射假设下提取质子靶截面；另一方面使用束流管油（beam-pipe oil）中的氢成分进行独立的直接测量。

Result: 在超子动量1.074 GeV/c下，Λ+⁹Be与反Λ+⁹Be非弹性散射截面分别为(10.1±1.4_stat±0.7_syst) mb和(1.7±0.6_stat±0.4_syst) mb；直接测量得到Λ+p→Σ⁰+p截面为(3.2±1.1_stat±0.5_syst) mb，反Λ+p→反Σ⁰+p截面为(1.5±0.5_stat±0.1_syst) mb；合并后分别为(4.7±0.7) mb和(1.1±0.3) mb。反Λ+p→反Σ⁰+p信号具有3.1σ显著性，构成反超子非弹性散射的首个证据。

Conclusion: Λp与反Λp非弹性散射截面的显著差异为超子-核子与反超子-核子相互作用提供了新的实验约束，表明反超子-核子相互作用强度明显弱于相应的超子-核子过程。

Abstract: Using a sample of $(10087\pm44)\times10^{6}$ $J/ψ$ events collected with the BESIII detector, we investigate the inelastic scattering processes $Λ+p\toΣ^{0}+p$ and $\overlineΛ+p\to\overlineΣ^{0}+p$, exploiting hyperons from $J/ψ\toΛ\overlineΛ$ decays as an effective beam and the beam-pipe materials as targets. The processes $Λ+{}^{9}\mathrm{Be}\toΣ^{0}+p+{}^{8}\mathrm{Li}$ and $\overlineΛ+{}^{9}\mathrm{Be}\to\overlineΣ^{0}+p+{}^{8}\mathrm{Li}$ are measured at a hyperon momentum of $1.074~\mathrm{GeV}/c$, with cross sections of $(10.1\pm1.4_{\rm stat}\pm0.7_{\rm syst})$ mb and $(1.7\pm0.6_{\rm stat}\pm0.4_{\rm syst})$ mb, respectively. Under the assumption of surface-dominated hyperon-nucleus scattering, these measurements are used to extract the corresponding proton-target cross sections. Independently, direct measurements using the hydrogen component of the beam-pipe oil yield $(3.2\pm1.1_{\rm stat}\pm0.5_{\rm syst})$ mb for $Λ+p\toΣ^{0}+p$ and $(1.5\pm0.5_{\rm stat}\pm0.1_{\rm syst})$ mb for $\overlineΛ+p\to\overlineΣ^{0}+p$, consistent with the indirect determinations. The combined cross sections are $(4.7\pm0.7)$ mb and $(1.1\pm0.3)$ mb, respectively. The $\overlineΛ+p\to\overlineΣ^{0}+p$ signal constitutes the first evidence for anti-hyperon inelastic scattering with baryonic final states, with a significance of $3.1σ$. The pronounced difference between the $Λp$ and $\overlineΛp$ inelastic scattering cross sections provides new experimental constraints on hyperon-nucleon and anti-hyperon-nucleon interactions.

</details>

Zhang,T. Zhang,Y. Zhou*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验以2.84吨·年的曝光量搜索暗物质粒子与氙核的相互作用，扩展核反冲能量窗口至约270 keV以覆盖有效场论和非弹性暗物质模型。观测到1个与核反冲特征一致的事件，对应能量为248±23(统计)±23(系统)keV，与纯背景假设的全局显著性为2.6σ（最大局部显著性3.4σ）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自旋无关（spin independent）暗物质相互作用模型预期的高能反冲占比有限，而有效场论（effective field theory）和非弹性暗物质模型可能在更高能量区间产生可探测信号，因此需要通过扩展核反冲能量窗口来覆盖这些新物理模型。

Method: 采用LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验装置，累计2.84吨·年的氙靶曝光量，将核反冲能量搜索窗口扩展至约270 keV。构建包含已知稀有背景拓扑的背景模型，利用轮廓似然比检验（profile likelihood ratio test）进行统计推断，并考虑look-elsewhere效应以校准全局显著性。

Result: 观测到1个与核反冲特征一致的事件，能量为248±23(统计)±23(系统)keV，位于已知背景预期较低的区间。该事件与背景只假设的张力在考虑look-elsewhere效应后的全局显著性为2.6σ，在所测模型中的最大局部显著性为3.4σ。

Conclusion: 此次搜索在扩展能量窗口下对有效场论和非弹性暗物质模型提供了新的约束，观测到的事件虽未达到发现标准，但与背景预期存在一定的偏离（2.6σ全局显著性），值得进一步积累曝光量以确认该信号是否具有物理意义。

Abstract: We report on a search for dark matter particles interacting with xenon nuclei in an exposure of 2.84 tonne-years with the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment. An extended nuclear recoil energy window up to approximately 270 keV enables searches for effective field theory and inelastic models of dark matter where high-energy recoils account for a larger fraction of the predicted recoil spectrum compared to the nominal spin independent interaction. We observe one event with characteristics consistent with a nuclear recoil of $248\pm23\,\mathrm{(stat)}\pm23\,\mathrm{(sys)}$ keV, in a region where the known background expectation is low. A profile likelihood ratio test finds tension with the background-only hypothesis at a global significance of 2.6$σ$ when accounting for look-elsewhere effects, with a maximum local significance of 3.4$σ$ across the models tested. We describe the analysis and event of interest, the background model used in the statistical inference, and detail several of the rare background topologies considered.

</details>


### [135] [Effective Sub-Quantum Readout for Non-Monochromatic Axion Signals in High-$Q$ Haloscopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02828)
*Junu Jeong,Max Silva-Feaver*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文证明，当轴子信号通过约瑟夫森参数放大器与半泵频腔共振耦合时，信号可对称占据信号带和闲置带，其非相干叠加使信号功率加倍而真空噪声不变，从而将标准量子极限的有效噪声减半至每频率槽0.5量子（最优匹配滤波后为1/√2量子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波腔晕子探测器（haloscope）搜寻波动暗物质时受标准量子极限（Standard Quantum Limit）限制，即相位保持线性放大对窄带信号至少引入一个量子的总系统噪声。研究人员希望突破这一极限以提升轴子（axion）信号探测灵敏度。

Method: 将约瑟夫森参数放大器（Josephson Parametric Amplifier, JPA）工作于腔共振频率恰好位于半泵频率（half-pump frequency）的条件，使非单色轴子信号对称地同时占据信号带（signal band）和闲置带（idler band）；随后通过零差读出（homodyne readout）的正交分量（quadrature）分析，获取两个镜像谱分量的非相干叠加（incoherent sum）结果。

Result: 镜像谱分量的非相干叠加使测量的信号功率加倍，而真空噪声保持不变。该运行方式将有效噪声极限降至每频率槽0.5量子，经最优匹配滤波（matched filtering）后整体有效极限为1/√2量子。

Conclusion: 相比标准量子极限，该方案在非单色轴子信号与高Q腔耦合的情形下实现了有效噪声极限的减半，为提高微波腔晕子探测器的轴子搜寻灵敏度提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The search for wave-like dark matter using microwave cavity haloscopes is constrained by the Standard Quantum Limit, which dictates that phase-preserving linear amplification results in a minimum of one quantum of total system noise for a narrow-band signal. We demonstrate that this limit is effectively halved for a non-monochromatic axion signal coupled to a high-$Q$ cavity. By operating a Josephson Parametric Amplifier such that the cavity resonance is centered exactly at the half-pump frequency, the axion signal symmetrically populates both the signal and idler bands. Through quadrature analysis of the homodyne readout, we show that the incoherent sum of these mirrored spectral components doubles the measured signal power while the vacuum noise remains constant. This operation yields an effective noise limit of 0.5 quanta per frequency bin, translating to an overall effective limit of $1/\sqrt{2}$ quanta after optimal matched filtering.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [A Hilbert Module Approach to Classical and Quantum Reference Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01894)
*Lucus Brady*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文利用希尔伯特模（Hilbert modules）与C*-动力系统的等变对应（equivariant correspondences），为C*-代数量子参考系建立了经典与量子两种模型。经典模型作为量子模型的交换基线（commutative baseline），量子模型则编码了量子参考系的希尔伯特空间操作化表述（Hilbert space operational formulation）。文中还提供了两种模型的多个显式示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子参考系（quantum reference frames）在量子力学与量子信息中日益重要，但目前缺乏一个统一的代数框架来系统刻画经典与量子参考系之间的关系。作者旨在通过C*-代数（C*-algebra）的语言，为参考系理论提供一个数学严谨且普适的建模方案。

Method: 借助希尔伯特模和C*-动力系统（C*-dynamical systems）的等变对应构造参考系模型，并区分经典参考系的交换模型与量子参考系的非交换模型，同时通过对比展示经典模型作为量子模型的退化（交换）基线。

Result: 成功构建了C*-代数框架下的经典与量子参考系模型，证明了经典模型作为量子模型的交换基线；量子模型能够涵盖希尔伯特空间操作化表述，并给出了若干显式示例支撑该框架的自洽性与适用性。

Conclusion: 该C*-代数参考系模型为经典与量子参考系提供了统一的数学基础，既保留了经典极限下的交换结构，又能够自然容纳量子效应，为后续参考系理论与量子信息交叉研究提供了新的代数工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a model for classical and quantum reference frames for $C^*$-algebras by utilizing Hilbert modules and equivariant correspondences of $C^*$-dynamical systems. We show the classical model provides a commutative baseline for the quantum model, and we discuss how the quantum model encodes the Hilbert space operational formulation of quantum reference frames. We also include various explicit examples for both classical and quantum reference frames.

</details>


### [137] [Noncommutative majorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02799)
*Matthew Kennedy,Paul Skoufranis*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文提出了非交换优序（noncommutative majorization）理论，将经典的Hardy-Littlewood-Pólya优序理论推广到不一定对易的自伴矩阵元组，并利用凸性理论刻画了该序关系，进而给出了量子信道（quantum channel）插值问题的新充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典优序（majorization）理论在矩阵和算子理论中有广泛应用，但传统理论仅适用于对易矩阵或单一迹（trace）情形。本文旨在将经典优序推广到非对易的自伴矩阵元组情形，为量子信息等领域的信道插值问题提供更一般的数学框架。

Method: 本文通过非交换凸函数（convex noncommutative functions）来定义并刻画非交换优序，借助Davidson与第一作者近期发展的非交换凸性理论（noncommutative convexity theory）和非交换Choquet理论（noncommutative Choquet theory）作为主要技术工具。

Result: 获得了两个有限矩阵集合之间存在迹保持完全正映射（trace-preserving completely positive map，即量子信道）的新的充要条件。通过反例表明该映射并非总能选为混合酉（mixed unitary）映射，即使是在局域情形亦然。此外，结论可推广至超越迹情形，刻画了保持任意忠实态（faithful state）的幺正完全正映射乃至一般幺正完全正映射的存在性。

Conclusion: 非交换优序理论为量子信道存在性问题提供了统一的数学刻画，其适用性不仅限于迹情形，还涵盖更一般的忠实态保持情形。文中还指出验证非交换优序的计算难度问题，暗示该方向的数值或算法挑战仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: We introduce a theory of noncommutative majorization that extends the classical majorization theory introduced by Hardy, Littlewood and Pólya to tuples of self-adjoint matrices that do not necessarily commute. We define and characterize a noncommutative majorization order that extends the classical majorization order. The definition is in terms of convex noncommutative functions, and we utilize the noncommutative convexity theory and noncommutative Choquet theory recently introduced by Davidson and the first author. As an application, we obtain a new necessary and sufficient condition for the existence of a trace-preserving completely positive map, i.e. a quantum channel, that interpolates between two finite sets of matrices. We give examples demonstrating that it is not always possible for this map to be chosen mixed unitary, even locally. We also address the computational difficulty of verifying the noncommutative majorization order. Our results further apply beyond the tracial case, and we obtain more general results characterizing the existence of unital completely positive maps that preserve an arbitrary faithful state or even unital completely positive map.

</details>

### [138] [A greedy nearest-neighbor approach to quantify site revisitation: comparing two sympatric raven species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01858)
*Bar Ashkenazi,Miguel de Guinea,Michael Assaf,Ran Nathan*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于贪心最近邻聚类（greedy nearest-neighbor clustering）与局部重分配和多边形站点构建的方法，从原始追踪数据中提取每只个体带时间戳的站点访问序列，并将其应用于死海地区两种同域渡鸦的九年GPS数据，通过非马尔可夫个体移动模型（IMM）量化递归模式，揭示了两物种在站点发现与优先回访之间的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动生态学（movement ecology）的核心挑战在于，由于采样频率的异质性和站点边界不确定，难以从原始追踪数据中描述具有生态意义的居住站点，因此需要开发能够稳健提取站点访问序列的方法。

Method: 采用带局部重分配的贪心最近邻聚类方法，结合基于多边形（polygon）的站点构建来生成个体时间戳站点访问序列；随后利用非马尔可夫个体移动模型（IMM）量化新站点发现（novel-site discovery，β）与优先回访（preferential return，α）之间的平衡。

Result: 推断出的动态行为与IMM预测一致，且揭示了明确的种间差异：棕颈渡鸦（Brown-necked raven）以更低的β值持续以更高的速率发现新站点，而扇尾渡鸦（Fan-tailed raven）对排名靠前站点的访问更为集中。熵分析进一步区分了物种：棕颈渡鸦表现出更高的站点熵和更高的站点间转移条件熵。

Conclusion: 该框架为描述种间移动策略差异提供了稳健工具，可用于从GPS数据推断记忆特征，并提示两种密切相关的同域物种采取了截然不同的空间利用策略。

Abstract: A central challenge in movement ecology is to describe ecologically meaningful residence sites from raw tracking data due to heterogeneous sampling frequency and uncertain site boundaries. Here, we develop a greedy nearest-neighbor clustering approach with local reassignment and polygon-based site construction that generates timestamped sequences of site visits for each tracked individual, and apply it to nine years of GPS data from two sympatric raven species in the Dead Sea region---the Fan-tailed raven (\emph{Corvus rhipidurus}) and the Brown-necked raven (\emph{C. ruficollis}). Using the resulting visitation sequences, we quantify recursion patterns using the non-Markovian individual mobility model (IMM), which captures the balance between novel-site discovery $β$ and preferential return $α$. The inferred dynamics are consistent with IMM predictions and reveal clear interspecific differences: Brown-necked ravens continue to discover new sites at a higher rate (lower $β$), whereas Fan-tailed ravens show a steeper concentration of visits among top-ranked sites. Entropy analyses further separate the species, with Brown-necked ravens exhibiting higher site entropy and higher conditional entropy of site-to-site transitions. Together, these results provide a robust framework for describing interspecific differences in movement strategies that can be used to infer memory from GPS data and suggest distinct space-use strategies in two closely related sympatric species.

</details>

### [139] [Trypanosome motility in blood and particle suspensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02201)
*Florian A. Overberg,Marie Kater,Timothy Krüger,Gerhard Gompper,Markus Engstler,Dmitry A. Fedosov*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过数值模拟与体外实验结合，发现锥虫在浓缩红细胞悬浮液中的游动速度可比在纯液体中提高高达50%，该增强源于悬浮颗粒增大鞭毛摆动垂直与平行阻力系数之间的各向异性，从而提升推进力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血液锥虫锥体虫（Trypanosoma brucei）在血液——一种致密的红细胞悬浮液——中运动，但其在这种复杂拥挤条件下游动的物理机制由于直接实验观察困难而长期未能阐明。

Method: 结合数值模拟（numerical simulations）和体外实验（in vitro experiments），分别考察锥虫在浓缩红细胞悬浮液及球形胶体颗粒悬浮液中的游动行为，并通过控制胶体颗粒尺寸进行对照研究。

Result: 模拟显示在接近血液的红细胞体积分数下锥虫游动速度提升达50%；悬浮颗粒显著增强了鞭毛摆动过程中垂直与平行阻力系数的各向异性（anisotropy），从而增强推进，该效应在颗粒尺寸接近或小于鞭毛摆动特征波长时最为显著。微粒悬浮液实验证实随颗粒浓度增加锥虫推进增强，与模拟定性一致。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一个普遍物理机制：浓缩颗粒悬浮液可通过增强各向异性摩擦来提升鞭毛驱动运动，提示血液中拥挤的颗粒环境可能反而促进锥虫推进，这一发现可能广泛适用于复杂悬浮液中的其他鞭毛微游泳者（flagellated microswimmers）。

Abstract: Motile microorganisms often navigate crowded and structurally complex environments, where surrounding obstacles can strongly influence locomotion. The bloodstream form of the flagellate parasite Trypanosoma brucei circulates in blood, a dense suspension of red blood cells (RBCs), yet the physical mechanisms governing its locomotion under such conditions remain poorly understood because direct experimental observations are challenging. Here, we combine numerical simulations with in vitro experiments to investigate trypanosome motility in concentrated RBC suspensions and in suspensions of spherical colloidal particles. Simulations reveal that the parasite swimming speed increases by up to $50\%$ at RBC volume fractions comparable to those in blood. To identify the origin of this enhancement, we perform controlled studies in colloidal suspensions with particles of different sizes. We find that suspended particles substantially increase the anisotropy between the perpendicular and parallel friction coefficients acting on the beating flagellum, thereby enhancing propulsion. This effect is most pronounced when the suspended particles are comparable to or smaller than the characteristic wavelength of the flagellar beat. Experiments with microparticle suspensions confirm an increase in trypanosome propulsion with increasing particle concentration, in qualitative agreement with the simulations. Our results uncover a general physical mechanism by which concentrated particle suspensions can enhance flagellar-beat-driven locomotion and suggest that the densely crowded, particulate environment of blood may facilitate trypanosome propulsion. These findings provide new insight into trypanosome motility in the bloodstream and may apply broadly to other flagellated microswimmers in complex suspensions.

</details>


### [140] [A viscoelastic theory for ultrasound-induced intracellular streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02696)
*Niels Gieseler,Falko Ziebert,Ulrich S. Schwarz*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个半解析模型，用于预测超声诱导的细胞内微流（intracellular streaming）的流动模式与能量分布。细胞被建模为浸没在具有不同黏弹性性质流体中的黏弹性液滴，利用Oldroyd-B本构关系结合声流体力学的摄动展开进行求解。研究发现一系列与能量模式分配相关的流动反转现象，并探讨了结果在细胞感知与信号传导方面的潜在生物学意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声波越来越多地被用于操控生物细胞，但其多种物理效应（如辐射力、声流、加热和空化）需要定量比较相对强弱的理论框架。当前缺乏能够预测超声诱导细胞内微流模式与能量分布的理论模型，因此有必要建立一个可量化的分析框架。

Method: 将细胞建模为浸没在具有不同黏弹性性质的流体中的黏弹性液滴，利用Oldroyd-B本构关系描述动量方程。采用声流体力学的摄动展开（perturbation expansion）方法求解，随后通过分波展开（partial wave expansion）和显式积分求解各阶方程。模型覆盖了与合成聚合物和蛋白质溶液相关的参数范围。

Result: 研究发现一系列流动反转（flow reversals）现象，并与不同模式间的能量分布相关联。密度和可压缩性对比会移动这些转换点并引入新的转换，表明材料参数对流动模式有显著影响。

Conclusion: 该数学模型的结论对细胞感知与信号传导具有潜在的生物学相关性，为理解超声操控细胞的多物理机制提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Ultrasound is increasingly used to control biological cells, but its multiple physical effects, including radiation force, streaming, heating, and cavitation, require theoretical frameworks that can quantify their relative strengths. Here we develop a semi-analytical model to predict flow patterns and energy distributions of ultrasound-induced intracellular streaming. Cells are modeled as viscoelastic droplets immersed in a fluid with different viscoelastic properties. For this geometry, the momentum equation with an Oldroyd-B constitutive law is solved using a perturbation expansion commonly applied in acoustofluidics. The resulting equations for the different orders of the expansion are solved using partial wave expansion and explicit integration. We investigate our model for a range of parameters which is relevant both for synthetic polymer and for protein solutions. We find a series of flow reversals that we can connect to the distribution of energy into the different modes. We also consider density and compressibility contrasts, which shift these transitions and introduce new ones. Finally, we discuss the potential biological relevance of our mathematical results for cellular sensing and signaling.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [When Literature Data Mislead Artificial Intelligence in Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01621)
*Qian Wang,Ying Li,Ryuhei Sato,Hidemi Kato,Shin-ichi Orimo,Hao Li,Eric Jianfeng Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本论文以固态电解质（solid electrolyte）电导率数据为例，系统考察了从原始文献到数据集的数值追溯过程，发现文本与图表不一致、轴标注模糊、单位不统一和测量背景缺失等系统性问题会以结构化标签噪声（structured label noise）的形式在数据库构建和机器学习复用中传播，甚至可造成100倍级的电导率误差。该研究将数据准确性重新定义为人工智能驱动科学发现的基础设施要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能日益将科学文献作为构建数据库、训练预测模型和引导发现的数据源，但文献衍生数据集往往默认所报实验数值具有内部一致性和可直接复用性，这一隐含假设缺乏系统检验。

Method: 以固态电解质电导率数据作为材料科学代表性案例，将数值从原始来源文章追踪至策划数据集（curated datasets），通过人工审计识别文本-图表不匹配、轴标注歧义、单位不一致及测量背景缺失等误差类型，并利用跨数据库示例展示歧义报告的影响规模。

Result: 研究发现这些数据不一致在数值上往往看似合理，常规预处理难以察觉，但会作为结构化标签噪声在数据库构建和机器学习复用中系统性传播；跨数据库案例分析表明，模糊的报告方式可导致100倍的电导率错误。

Conclusion: 本分析将数据准确性重新定义为人工智能驱动发现的基础设施要求，并提出建立可追溯报告（traceable reporting）、策划与验证实践以保障科学数据的可复用性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) increasingly treats scientific literature as a data source for building databases, training predictive models, and guiding discovery. Yet literature-derived datasets often assume that reported experimental values are internally consistent and directly reusable. Here, we analyze this assumption using solid electrolyte (SE) conductivity data as a representative materials-science case. By tracing values from source articles to curated datasets, we identify recurrent text-figure mismatches, ambiguous axis annotations, unit inconsistencies, and missing measurement context. These discrepancies are often numerically plausible and therefore difficult to detect through routine preprocessing, but they can propagate as structured label noise during database construction and machine-learning reuse. A cross-database example shows how ambiguous reporting can create a 100-fold conductivity error. Our analysis reframes data accuracy as an infrastructure requirement for artificial-intelligence-driven discovery and motivates traceable reporting, curation, and validation practices for reusable scientific data.
  Keywords: AI for science; Data reliability; Scientific databases; Structured label noise; Literature-derived data; Materials informatics; Solid electrolytes

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Curvature-driven spin transport in rank-two string-Carroll hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01634)
*Nikko John Leo Lobos,Reggie Pantig*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了固定无挠背景上探针物质的典范应力-自旋Ward系统的秩二弦-Carroll极限，保留了秩一Carroll自旋流体动力学之外的部分：耦合的Riemann-自旋力、第二纵向动量投影以及由二维纵向核允许的纵向boost双矢。文章系统性地在近地平线参数λ=ε²中展开，并证明了在特定调优扇区存在自洽的流体动力学框架，推导出显式的闭式参数解，并验证了闭环面Killing通量在正则伪规范改进下的不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越秩一Carroll自旋流体动力学，理解强引力（near-horizon）物理中自旋与曲率耦合的结构，并避免非线性自旋反馈对展开结果的污染。

Method: 先从独立自旋振幅ς中提取线性项，再在近地平线参数λ=ε²中展开，将诱导应力与曲率源的限制条件应用于一般压热关系；对仿射常数声速族导出参数化解，并研究正则伪规范改进下的Killing通量。

Result: 在光滑非极值静态球形外视界的正则分支上，纵向和横向自旋振幅服从相同稀释律，曲率力首次出现在O(ςλ²)阶；一般的正压流体将定态系统约化为一个热力学求积和一个局部求逆；对仿射族p=αε+Π获得了闭式参数解；Reissner-Nordström中响应在|Q|/M=2√2/3处消失，Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton族中响应零点为R₋/R₊=(1+a²)/2。

Conclusion: 秩二弦-Carroll极限允许自旋-曲率耦合与纵向boost扇区，其响应在特定平衡半径处消失；Frenkel极限消除该响应和纵向boost扇区，简化了结构。

Abstract: We derive the rank-two string-Carroll limit of the canonical stress--spin Ward system for probe matter on a fixed torsion-free background. Beyond rank-one Carroll spin hydrodynamics, this retains the coupled Riemann--spin force, a second longitudinal momentum projection, and the longitudinal boost bivector allowed only by a two-dimensional longitudinal kernel. We first extract the term linear in an independent spin amplitude $ς$ and then expand in the near-horizon parameter $λ=ε^2$, avoiding contamination by the omitted $\mathcal{O}(ς^2)$ thermodynamic spin feedback. For finite mixed stress and $S^{λμν}=\mathcal{O}(ςε^p)$, $p=0$ is the unique sector in which the induced stress and curvature source balance at the first generic Ward order. On the regular branch of a smooth nonextremal static spherical outer horizon, locally normalized longitudinal and transverse spin amplitudes obey the same dilution law, while the curvature force first appears at $\mathcal{O}(ςλ^2)$. For a general barotrope, the stationary system reduces to one thermodynamic quadrature and one local inverse. For the affine constant-sound-speed family $p=α\mathcal E+Π$, $0\leqα<1$, we obtain an explicit closed-form parametric solution, leading profiles for both spin channels, and fixed-radius, linear-spin corrections to enthalpy, pressure, and rapidity away from the sonic point. The response is governed by $\mathcal R^{(L)}_h=(f_2+3f_1ψ_1)/2$ and the longitudinal spin flux. We prove that the stationary closed-sphere Killing flux is invariant under regular pseudo-gauge improvements. In Reissner--Nordström, the response vanishes at the radial-tidal inversion $|Q|/M=2\sqrt{2}/3$; in the Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton family, its zero is $R_-/R_+=(1+a^2)/2$ for $0\leq a<1$. The Frenkel limit removes this response and the longitudinal boost sector.

</details>


### [143] [A Unified Treatment of the Self-Force Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01644)
*Beka Modrekiladze,Ira Z. Rothstein,Jordan Wilson-Gerow*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于世界线有效场论（worldline effective field theory）引入了一种形式体系，可系统地计算浸没在具有非零应力能量环境中的致密天体所受到的引力自力（gravitational self-force）。通过闭合时间路径积分（closed time path integral）构建普适有效作用量，并证明领头阶运动方程可统一用环境应力—能量张量的推迟两点函数表示，同时揭示Chandrasekhar的"库仑对数"应被理解为紫外发散重整化产生的重整化群对数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密天体在具有非零应力能量（stress-energy）的环境中运动时会受到引力自力的影响，但此前缺乏一种系统且完全相对论性的计算框架。本文旨在建立一套基于世界线有效场论的形式体系，使得在任何相关展开参数阶上都能计算致密天体因环境相互作用而产生的运动方程。

Method: 作者采用闭合时间路径积分构造了一个普适的有效作用量（effective action），可计算致密天体与环境相互作用导致的运动方程至任意阶展开参数。由此推导出领头阶运动方程，其可普适地用环境中应力—能量张量的推迟两点函数（retarded two-point function）表示。整个推导以完全相对论性的方式进行，涵盖耗散力（dissipative force）与保守力（conservative force）两类效应。

Result: 作者将该框架分别应用于尘埃（dust）、无黏流体（inviscid fluid）和相干场（coherent field）三种环境，计算了其耗散力，结果与文献中先前推导的结论一致。此外，论文揭示Chandrasekhar著名的"库仑对数"（Coulomb Log）应解释为由于紫外发散重整化in-in作用量耗散部分而产生的重整化群（renormalization group）对数，并证明在牛顿极限（Newtonian limit）下对一类通用环境和轨迹而言，该对数的值是普适的。

Conclusion: 该库仑对数是耗散作用量中重整化群流的领头对数，这一尚未被探索的RG流结构为未来研究提供了新方向。本文建立的形式体系具有高度普适性，为在不同环境中系统计算致密天体自力的耗散与保守效应提供了统一的理论工具。

Abstract: This paper introduces formalism, based on worldline effective field theory, which allows one to systematically calculate the gravitational self-force on a compact object immersed in an environment with non-vanishing stress energy. Using the closed time path integral we present a universal effective action that can be used to calculate the equations of motion of a compact object due to its interaction with the environment to any order in the relevant expansion parameters, the relative importance of which depends upon the choice of environment. We show that the leading order equations of motion can be universally written in terms of the retarded two-point function of the stress-energy tensor for the environment. The resulting action can be used to calculate both dissipative (dynamical friction) and conservative forces in a completely relativistic fashion. We demonstrate the utility of the result by calculating the dissipative force due to dust, an inviscid fluid, and a coherent field. Our results agree with those previously derived in the literature. We furthermore show that the famous ``Coulomb Log" found by Chandrasekhar should be interpreted as a renormalization group log due to a UV divergence that renormalizes the dissipative part of the in-in action. We then prove that this log is universal in the Newtonian limit in that its value is universal, for a generic class of environments and trajectories. This log is the leading log in an RG flow in the dissipative action that has yet to be explored.

</details>


### [144] [Parameter Space, Realistic Matter, and Universal Relations in Bose--Einstein Condensate Dark Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01646)
*M. Ilyas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过联立求解TOV、Hartle偶极和Postnikov–Hinderer方程，系统研究了慢转动玻色–爱因斯坦凝聚（BEC）暗物质恒星的性质，发现该状态方程类别中2 M⊙最大质量约束与GW170817潮汐约束无法同时满足，但I–Love普适关系在多种模型间保持良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暗物质对致密星观测性质的影响，并检验BEC暗物质状态方程在质量、潮汐形变和普适关系（universal relation）方面的可观测预言能力。

Method: 联立求解Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff方程、Hartle偶极（dipole）展开和Postnikov–Hinderer方程，采用含Lee–Huang–Yang修正（强度参数ζ）的多方状态方程；连续扫描ζ及玻色子参数(m, a_s)，构建十二种模型集合并将求解器推广至核物质、夸克物质及双流体情形。

Result: ζ从0到1.5的平均场到修正态过渡平滑；2 M⊙质量上限与Λ₁.₄≲800的潮汐约束在本类状态方程中无法同时实现；I–Love普适关系在BEC模型中偏差仅0.13%；求解器对SLy、APR4核物质的最大质量复现误差在1%以内；混合星的最大质量对中心暗物质分数非单调；跨核物质、混合和BEC模型的I–Love序列汇聚于单一曲线（约5%偏差），而自束缚夸克星偏离高达90%。

Conclusion: BEC暗物质恒星在慢转动条件下可由统一求解器可靠描述，其状态方程类无法同时满足现有质量与潮汐观测约束；I–Love普适关系表现出跨模型稳健性，可据此区分暗物质恒星与自束缚夸克星。

Abstract: We study slowly rotating Bose--Einstein condensate (BEC) dark stars by solving the Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff, Hartle dipole, and Postnikov--Hinderer equations together for a polytropic equation of state with a Lee--Huang--Yang correction of strength $ζ$~\citep{Panotopoulos2026}. A continuous scan of $ζ$ from 0 to 1.5 shows the mean-field-to-corrected transition is smooth, with no hidden structure at intermediate values. Scanning the underlying boson parameters $(m,a_s)$ more broadly, we find that a $2\,M_\odot$ maximum-mass bound and a GW170817-like tidal bound $Λ_{1.4}\lesssim800$ cannot be satisfied simultaneously anywhere in this equation-of-state class. The $I$-Love universal relation holds to $0.13\%$ across twelve $(m,a_s,ζ)$ models, and the $ζ=0$ and $ζ=1$ sequences sit on opposite sides of the master curve by a consistent, non-random offset. Applied without modification to realistic nuclear matter (SLy, APR4), the same solver reproduces published maximum masses to within $1\%$; applied to self-bound MIT-bag quark matter it gives the expected mass--radius shape; and once extended to a two-fluid baryon-plus-dark-matter formalism, it shows that the maximum mass of a hybrid star is not a monotonic function of the central dark-matter fraction. Pooling $I$-Love sequences across nuclear, hybrid, and BEC dark-star models, we find they collapse onto a single curve to within about $5\%$, while self-bound quark stars sit far off it, departing by up to $90\%$.

</details>


### [145] [Late-time dark energy dynamics in $f(Q)$ gravity: A data-driven analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01650)
*Kshetrimayum Govind Singh,Kangujam Priyokumar Singh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在对称平移的f(Q)引力框架下，采用幂律形式f(Q)=βQ^{m+1}描述晚期暗能量动力学，并利用宇宙计时器（CC）、DESI DR2 BAO以及三个独立Ia型超新星样本（Pantheon+、DES-SN5YR、Union3）联合约束模型参数。结果显示该模型给出互相一致的哈勃常数（H0在67–68 km/s/Mpc之间），在多数数据集组合下优于ΛCDM（由χ²min和AIC判据支持），并预测类精质（quintessence-like）行为与从减速到加速的转变红移0.680≤zt≤0.745。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晚期宇宙观测日益精确和互补，为检验修改引力理论是否符合观测到的宇宙膨胀历史提供了新机遇。DESI DR2 BAO与宇宙计时器和独立Ia型超新星数据的结合为宇宙加速提供数据驱动的检验，从而激发对晚期暗能量动力学在f(Q)引力中的探索。

Method: 采用对称平移f(Q)引力（symmetric teleparallel f(Q) gravity）中幂律形式f(Q)=βQ^{m+1}刻画暗能量，通过有效状态方程（EoS）参数描述宇宙演化；利用CC、DESI DR2 BAO以及Pantheon+、DES-SN5YR和Union3三套超新星编译数据对模型进行贝叶斯约束，并使用AIC/BIC进行模型比较。

Result: 模型给出的哈勃常数分别为H0=67.60^{+1.60}_{-1.66}、67.08^{+1.66}_{-1.61}和68.16^{+1.46}_{-1.43} km/s/Mpc，相互一致；该f(Q)模型在三套数据集组合中均给出更低的χ²min并被AIC支持，而BIC在两套组合中偏好ΛCDM，在Union3组合中无显著偏好。重建的EoS显示类精质行为，减速度参数为负，减速-加速转变发生在0.680≤zt≤0.745。

Conclusion: 高阶宇宙学参数、Statefinder和Om(z)判据均一致支持类精质行为，且Statefinder轨迹在远未来趋近ΛCDM不动点。总体而言，幂律f(Q)模型为晚期宇宙加速提供了可行且具有竞争力的描述。

Abstract: The increasing precision and complementarity of late-time cosmological observations provide new opportunities to test modified gravity against the observed expansion history of the universe. The combination of DESI DR2 BAO measurements with cosmic chronometers (CC) and independent Type Ia supernova compilations provides a data-driven test of cosmic acceleration. Motivated by this progress, we investigate late-time dark energy dynamics in symmetric teleparallel $f(Q)$ gravity by adopting the power-law form $f(Q)=βQ^{m+1}$ and characterizing cosmic evolution through the effective equation of state (EoS) parameter. The model is constrained using CC, DESI DR2 BAO and three independent Type Ia supernova compilations, namely Pantheon+, DES-SN5YR and Union3. The inferred Hubble constant values are mutually consistent, yielding $H_0=67.60^{+1.60}_{-1.66}$, $67.08^{+1.66}_{-1.61}$ and $68.16^{+1.46}_{-1.43}\,\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$, respectively. Statistically, the proposed $f(Q)$ model yields lower $χ^2_{\min}$ values and is favored by the AIC for all dataset combinations, whereas the BIC favors $Λ$CDM for the CC + DESI DR2 + Pantheon+ and CC + DESI DR2 + DES-SN5YR combinations and indicates no meaningful preference between the two models for the CC + DESI DR2 + Union3 dataset combinations. The reconstructed EoS indicates quintessence-like behavior, while the negative deceleration parameter confirms the current accelerated expansion. The transition from deceleration to acceleration occurs within $0.680\leq z_t\leq0.745$. Higher-order cosmographic parameters and the Statefinder and $Om(z)$ diagnostics consistently support this quintessence-like behavior, with the Statefinder trajectories approaching the $Λ$CDM fixed point in the far future. Overall, the power-law $f(Q)$ model provides a viable and competitive description of late-time cosmic acceleration.

</details>


### [146] [Dark-Sector Effects on the Phase Structure of Nonlinear Magnetic AdS Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01695)
*Carlos E. Romero-Figueroa,J. R. Villanueva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了完美流体暗物质（PFDM）与暗能量场对非线性磁荷反德西特（AdS）黑洞相结构的影响。发现小黑洞-大黑洞相变由非线性磁扇区驱动，PFDM参数增大会抑制该相变并阻止规则黑洞核心形成；暗能量场的效果取决于其状态方程参数——在精质（quintessence）区增强一级相变，在幽灵（phantom）区仅呈现旋节线（spinodal）行为；几何热力学（GTD）曲率奇点复现了相结构，且临界行为遵循与黑洞、宇宙视界和真实流体一致的普适幂律标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在解释宇宙加速膨胀的背景下，暗物质与暗能量与黑洞热力学之间的关系尚待阐明。此前研究已探索了多种暗物质/暗能量模型对AdS黑洞相变的影响，但完美流体暗物质与暗能量场共同作用下非线性磁荷AdS黑洞的相结构仍缺乏系统分析，另外其临界普适性也需进一步验证。

Method: 作者在非线性磁荷（非线性磁场驱动器）背景下构造带PFDM与暗能量场（含精质与幽灵两种状态方程参数区域）的AdS黑洞热力学模型，通过吉布斯自由能（Gibbs free energy）分析相变结构，并利用几何热力学（GTD）建立的均衡流形及其曲率奇点来检视相结构与临界行为。

Result: 小大黑洞相变由非线性磁荷扇区驱动，暗物质/暗能量不加额外相变；增大PFDM参数抑制相变并使系统趋向单相区，且阻止规则黑洞核心形成。精质型暗能量促使一级相变加强（吉布斯构造区域和潜热增加、临界区域缩小），幽灵型则只显示旋节线（spinodal）行为而无相共处（phase coexistence）；GTD曲率奇点正确复现相结构，临界附近幂律标度与黑洞、宇宙视界和真实流体结果一致。

Conclusion: PFDM通过压缩相共存区而抑制SBH/LBH相变，暗能量场则以方程状态参数决定是否产生或强化一级相变；GTD从几何角度验证了该相结构，且其临界幂律与黑洞、视界和普通流体一致，暗示这些系统共享“普适热力学行为（universal thermodynamic behavior）”。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) and a dark-energy field on the phase structure of a nonlinear magnetically charged Anti--de Sitter (AdS) black hole. We find that the small--large black hole (SBH/LBH) phase transition is driven by the nonlinear magnetic sector, while the dark sector does not generate additional transitions. In particular, increasing the magnitude of the PFDM parameter $λ$ suppresses this phase transition, eventually driving the system toward a single-phase regime. Moreover, a nonzero $λ$ prevents the formation of a regular black hole core. The effect of the dark-energy field depends on its equation-of-state parameter: in the quintessence regime, increasing its intensity strengthens the first-order phase transition, increasing the Gibbs construction area and latent heat while reducing the critical regime; in the phantom regime, the system exhibits spinodal behavior without phase coexistence. Finally, geometrothermodynamics (GTD) shows that the curvature singularities of the thermodynamic equilibrium manifold reproduce the phase structure of the black hole, while the associated power-law scaling near criticality is consistent with previous results for black holes, cosmological horizons, and real fluids, suggesting a universal thermodynamic behavior across these systems.

</details>


### [147] [Geodesics and thermodynamics of a $κ$-deformed anti-de Sitter black hole surrounded by a quintessence field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01704)
*Faizuddin Ahmed,Ahmad Al-Badawi,Izzet Sakalli,Erdem Sucu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究在Kiselev quintessence场中κ变形Schwarzschild-anti-de Sitter黑洞的测地线结构与热力学性质。由于质量相关的时移形变(lapse deformation)，该黑洞无需电荷或自旋即产生内Cauchy视界，但中心奇异性仍无法解决，且Kretschmann标量以r^{-10}更强发散。标准Bekenstein-Hawking面积律违反热力学第一定律，通过修正熵或重标定质量可恢复第一定律并获得自洽的Smarr关系。光学方面，变形参数增大会缩小光子球与临界碰撞参数。闭合形式的Joule-Thomson系数表明κ形变触发了反转曲线(inversion curve)，且该反转现象可与van der Waals临界性完全解耦。此外，形变将峰值Hawking辐射抑制约四分之一，并增加通量稀疏性，其效应几乎完全由温度下降驱动而非阴影变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在探索κ形变(κ-deformation)对Schwarzschild-anti-de Sitter黑洞在quintessence暗能量背景下的几何与热力学影响，特别关注质量相关形变能否在不引入电荷或自旋的情况下产生内视界，以及形变如何影响热力学律、光学性质和Joule-Thomson效应等可观测性质。

Method: 作者分析了κ变形SAdS黑洞的测地线结构与热力学；检验Kretschmann标量的奇异性行为；通过修正熵或重标定质量的方式恢复热力学第一定律并推导Smarr关系；计算光子球和临界碰撞参数随变形参数的变化；推导闭合形式的Joule-Thomson系数以分析反转曲线；并考察Hawking辐射峰值与通量稀疏性对形变的响应。

Result: 研究发现：质量相关的时移形变在无电荷或自旋条件下产生内Cauchy视界，但奇异性未消除且Kretschmann标量以r^{-10}发散；修正熵或重标定质量可恢复第一定律及Smarr关系；变形参数增大导致光子球和碰撞参数收缩；κ形变触发Joule-Thomson反转曲线（在标准中性SAdS中不存在），但未产生van der Waals临界点，表明两者可解耦；峰值Hawking辐射被抑制约四倍，通量更稀疏，该效应主要源于温度下降而非shadow改变。

Conclusion: κ形变提供了在无电荷/自旋条件下生成内视界的途径，但也使奇异性更发散。该形变同时引入了标准中性SAdS所不具备的Joule-Thomson反转特征，并且Joule-Thomson反转与van der Waals临界性可在理论上明确分离，突出了κ形变框架的独特热力学与光学指纹。

Abstract: We investigate the geodesic structure and thermodynamics of a $κ$-deformed Schwarzschild-anti-de Sitter black hole in a Kiselev quintessence field, where dynamics are governed by a mass-dependent lapse deformation. This term produces an inner Cauchy horizon without requiring charge or spin, yet it fails to resolve the central singularity; instead, the Kretschmann scalar diverges more strongly as $r^{-10}$. Because the deformation amplitude couples to the mass, the standard Bekenstein-Hawking area law violates the first law of thermodynamics. We resolve this by either deriving a modified entropy or rescaling the mass, both of which restore the first law and yield a consistent Smarr relation. Optically, an increasing deformation parameter shrinks both the photon sphere and the critical impact parameter. We also derive a closed-form Joule-Thomson coefficient, revealing that the $κ$-deformation triggers an inversion curve a feature absent in standard neutral SAdS. Notably, this occurs without generating a van der Waals critical point, demonstrating that Joule-Thomson inversion and van der Waals criticality can be cleanly decoupled. Finally, the deformation suppresses peak Hawking emission by a factor of roughly four and increases flux sparsity, an effect driven almost entirely by a drop in temperature rather than changes to the black hole shadow.

</details>


### [148] [One-cycle radiation-reaction Magnusian for eccentric binaries through relative 1PN order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01713)
*Andrea Placidi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了非旋转致密双星在平面束缚偏心轨道上的单周期首阶Magnus积分器（first Magnusian），涉及首阶后牛顿（1PN）相对论修正和线性级辐射反应，并构造了对应的首阶近星点回归映射（periastron return map）。结果表明辐射反应规范参数的依赖在径向周期内抵消，且部分指数化映射（partially exponentiated map）在多个观测指标上优于严格首阶映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非旋转致密双星在束缚偏心轨道上的动力学演化，现有方法往往需要在偏心率上做展开或依赖特定的辐射反应规范选择。作者旨在构建不依赖偏心率展开、且在径向周期内消除规范参数依赖的首阶Magnus积分器及相应的回归映射，以提高长期演化的数值精度和规范不变性。

Method: 将守恒的1PN动力学及其Jacobi输运（Jacobi transport）与2.5PN和3.5PN辐射反应力相结合，在0<e<1范围内以封闭形式推导单周期首阶Magnusian的全部四个分量。将所得相空间更新投影到受扰的近星点回归截面（periastron return section）上，得到回归时间和拱点相位（apsidal phase）的修正；再将严格首阶映射与基于同一Magnusian的部分指数化映射进行比较。

Result: 所有八个Iyer–Will辐射反应规范参数对结果的依赖仅表现为周期余边界（periodic coboundaries），在径向周期内完全抵消。推导结果再现了平均能量与角动量损失、圆轨道极限以及1PN径向轨道要素的长期演化。在固定近星点指标的40个径向周期演化中，严格映射给出更小的残差；而部分指数化在累积近星点计时残差上约降低一个数量级，并在等质量单周期网格的每个采样点上改善了径向作用量和能量的更新精度。

Conclusion: 两种映射的高下取决于所观测的物理量：严格首阶映射在近星点指数对应的轨道分布上更优，而部分指数化映射在长期计时精度以及径向作用量、能量更新方面表现更佳。该结果为偏心双星系统的1PN级高效长期积分方案提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We derive the one-cycle first Magnusian for nonspinning compact binaries on planar, bound eccentric orbits through relative first post-Newtonian (1PN) order and linear order in radiation reaction, and construct the associated first-order periastron return map. Combining the conservative 1PN dynamics and its Jacobi transport with the 2.5PN and 3.5PN radiation-reaction forces, we obtain all four components in closed form for \(0<e<1\), without expanding in eccentricity. The dependence on all eight Iyer--Will radiation-reaction gauge parameters reduces to periodic coboundaries and therefore cancels over a radial cycle. The result reproduces the averaged energy and angular-momentum losses, their circular limit, and the secular evolution of the 1PN radial orbital elements. The two remaining components encode the dissipative angle-sector information. Projecting the resulting phase-space update onto the perturbed periastron return section yields the corrections to the return time and apsidal phase. We then compare the strict first-order map with a partially exponentiated map built from the same Magnusian. The comparison is observable dependent: at fixed periastron index, the strict map gives smaller residuals for a representative evolution over 40 radial cycles, whereas partial exponentiation reduces the accumulated periastron-timing residual by about an order of magnitude. Across a representative one-cycle grid at equal masses, partial exponentiation improves the radial-action and energy updates at every sampled point.

</details>


### [149] [How Much Geometry Does Horizon Entropy Determine? A Discourse on Entropy, Gravity, and Observable Quantities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01720)
*Yen Chin Ong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了广义熵变引力常数（GEVAG）框架与熵-引力对应（EGC）方法在广义黑洞视界熵公式 S=f(A)/4G 下的异同，并扩展近视界版本的 GEVAG，进而探讨其对贝肯斯坦界（Bekenstein bound）以及实验室所测引力常数 G 的本质含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于雅各布森（Jacobson）对爱因斯坦场方程的热力学推导，广义视界熵形式 S=f(A)/4G 被引入时空热力学与几何，催生了 GEVAG 和 EGC 两种修正方案。需要厘清两者在视界一致性关系之外的全局因果结构、渐近几何及多中心构型上的根本差异，并澄清近视界范围内两种方案与 f(R) 重构方法之间的关系。

Method: 通过理论分析比较 GEVAG（将 f'(A) 引入视界处有效引力耦合 G_eff）与 EGC（将视界关系推广为径向变化的体耦合 G_eff(r)）两种方案的逻辑结构与物理预言；并对 GEVAG 进行近视界扩展以恢复热力学质量与 ADM 质量之间的等价性；在此基础上考察各方案对贝肯斯坦界的约束意义。

Result: GEVAG 与 EGC 共享相同的视界一致性关系，但二者的全局因果结构和渐近几何可显著不同；EGC 的径向规定对多中心构型引入额外歧义性。近视界 GEVAG 恢复了仅视界表述所缺失的两种质量等价性。近视界 GEVAG 中的 G_eff 与 EGC 及基于 Wald 熵的 f(R)-重构方法之间存在通过贝肯斯坦界建立的意外联系。

Conclusion: 不同方案对视界熵与引力耦合的修正路径在近视界区域趋于相互关联，但全局层面存在本质差异；这最终引发一个核心未决问题——实验室实际测量的引力常数 G 本质上究竟代表什么。

Abstract: Building on Jacobson's thermodynamics derivation of Einstein field equations, the "generalized entropy and varying-G" (GEVAG) framework was proposed to consistently incorporate generalized horizon entropy $S=f(A)/4G$ into both the thermodynamics and the geometry of spacetime. This leads to an effective gravitational coupling $G_\text{eff}=G/f'(A)$. A related "entropy-gravity correspondence" (EGC) instead is equivalent to promoting this horizon relation to a radially varying bulk coupling $G_\text{eff}(r)$. We clarify the similarities and fundamental distinctions between these approaches. Although they share the same horizon consistency relation, their global causal structures and asymptotic geometries can differ substantially. In addition, the radial prescription of EGC introduces an additional ambiguity for multi-center configurations. We further examine a near-horizon extension of GEVAG, which restores the equivalence between thermodynamic and ADM masses that is absent in the horizon-only formulation. We then discuss the implications of these different prescriptions for the Bekenstein bound. The Bekenstein bound provides a surprising link between $G_\text{eff}$ in near horizon GEVAG, EGC, and the one obtained in the recent $f(R)$-reconstruction approach of generalized entropy geometry via Wald entropy. Finally, we raise the most important question: what exactly is the $G$ measured in laboratories?

</details>


### [150] [Exploring the construction of field equations for regular black holes with dynamical regularization scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01733)
*Raúl Carballo-Rubio,Jacopo Mazza*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次探讨将正则化尺度（regularization scale）视为动力学场（dynamical field）的场方程构建问题，用于描述正则黑洞（regular black holes）核心的演化。研究表明，静态正则黑洞解的存在性问题可归结为关于标量场与度规耦合函数的线性偏微分方程（linear partial differential equation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则黑洞动力学是新兴研究领域，已有工作通过在爱因斯坦场方程中引入作为调节器（regulator）的新物理尺度来正则化球对称黑洞解，但尚未有人考虑该正则化尺度本身作为动力学场的情形。

Method: 构造描述球对称引力与标量场耦合的一般场方程族，并检验常数标量场的扇区（sector with a constant scalar field）中是否存在仅由标量场提供物理尺度而不引入额外物理尺度的静态正则黑洞解。该问题被证明可约化为关于标量与度规自由度之间耦合函数的线性偏微分方程。

Result: 证明这类场方程的存在性问题可以简化为求解一个关于标量—度规耦合函数的线性偏微分方程，从而提供了一条构建替代动力学解的途径。

Conclusion: 所提出的场方程可能为描述正则黑洞核心在物质反作用（backreaction）下的演化提供有效描述，并为构造具有理论和现象学意义的替代动力学解开辟新方向。

Abstract: The study of the dynamics of regular black holes is an emergent area of research. Recent works have discussed how to deform the Einstein field equations to regularize spherically symmetric black hole solutions due to the introduction of a new physics scale acting as regulator. In this exploratory paper, we consider for the first time the problem of constructing field equations in which the regularization scale is a dynamical field. Such field equations may provide an effective description of the evolution of the cores of regular black holes due to the backreaction of matter, and a novel avenue for constructing alternative dynamical solutions of theoretical and phenomenological interest. We discuss a general family of field equations describing spherically symmetric gravity coupled to a scalar field, and study whether the sector with a constant scalar field contains solutions describing static regular black holes with no additional physics scales aside from the ones provided by the scalar field. We show that this question can be reduced to solving a linear partial differential equation for the coupling functions between the scalar and the metric degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [151] [Exploration of Zero-Complexity Compact Stars in Higher Dimensions under the Finch-Skea Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01803)
*Shyam Das,Megandhren Govender,Kevin Reddy,Bikram Keshari Parida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在$(n+2)$维爱因斯坦引力中，结合Finch–Skea几何与推广的复杂度形式，构造了满足零复杂度条件的新一类精确致密星内部解，并系统分析了其物理性质和维度效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期Herrera引力复杂度概念被推广到任意高维时空，作者希望在该推广框架下构造满足零复杂度条件的精确致密星模型，以超越四维广义相对论（General Relativity）的边界。

Method: 采用$(n+2)$维爱因斯坦场方程（Einstein field equations）与推广的复杂度形式相结合，在Finch–Skea几何中构造各向异性流体球内部的精确解；通过分析物质变量、压力各向异性、平衡条件、正则性、能量条件、因果性和稳定性来评估模型物理性质，并考察时空维数对星体结构特征的影响。

Result: 获得了描述零复杂度各向异性流体球的新一类精确内部解；额外维度的存在显著改变内部物质分布，同时保持物理可行性；这些解是推广复杂度框架下首批精确高维Finch–Skea致密星模型。

Conclusion: 该研究为零复杂度恒星结构从四维广义相对论到高维引力的自然推广提供了依据，为高维自引力系统的研究建立了稳健框架。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent extension of Herrera's gravitational complexity to arbitrary higher-dimensional spacetimes, we investigate exact compact-star models that satisfy the vanishing-complexity condition within the Finch--Skea geometry in $(n+2)$-dimensional Einstein gravity. By combining the higher-dimensional Einstein field equations with the generalized complexity formalism, we obtain a new class of exact interior solutions describing anisotropic fluid spheres with zero complexity. The physical properties of these models are evaluated by analyzing the behavior of the matter variables, pressure anisotropy, and equilibrium conditions, alongside the standard requirements of regularity, energy conditions, causality, and stability. We further explore the influence of spacetime dimensionality on the structural characteristics of the stellar configurations, demonstrating that the presence of extra dimensions significantly modifies the internal matter distribution while preserving physical viability. The solutions presented here represent the first exact higher-dimensional Finch--Skea compact-star models constructed within this recently developed generalized complexity framework \cite{choudas}. These results provide a natural extension of zero-complexity stellar configurations beyond four-dimensional General Relativity and offer a robust framework for investigating self-gravitating systems in higher- dimensional gravity.

</details>


### [152] [Scalar Field Model for Dark Matter Spikes Surrounding Sgr A$^*$ and M87$^*$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01900)
*Ramin G. Daghigh,Michael D. Green,Gabor Kunstatter*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过逆向工程设计（reverse engineering）一个具有非标准势能项（non-standard potential）的实标量场（real scalar field），来模型化黑洞周围暗物质尖峰（dark matter spike），其密度满足幂律形式 ρ(r) ∝ r^{-γ_sp}。所构造的解决方案来源于协变作用量，使得可以研究包含暗物质尖峰反作用效应（backreaction）的黑洞引力微扰（gravitational perturbations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论模型表明，暗物质中黑洞的绝热增长（adiabatic growth）可在事件视界附近形成高密度暗物质区域（即“尖峰”，spike），其密度呈幂律分布。尖峰可能对引力波及中心黑洞的阴影半径（shadow radius）产生可探测影响，因此需要一种能在协变框架下描述该现象的模型。

Method: 采用反向工程方法：通过逆向求解运动方程，构建一个允许所选能量密度及合理背景几何（background geometry）成为解的标量场势能函数，从而将暗物质尖峰建模为非标准势能项的实标量场。

Result: 所得几何结构具有可检验性（testable）。由于解源自协变作用量，可以在存在尖峰的条件下研究黑洞的引力微扰，并计入尖峰本身的反作用效应（backreaction）。

Conclusion: 该协变标量场模型为研究暗物质尖峰对黑洞引力波和阴影等可观测量的影响提供了可行框架，并可进一步推广至含反作用效应的引力微扰分析。

Abstract: Theoretical models suggest that the adiabatic growth of a black hole immersed in dark matter can lead to the formation of high density regions of dark matter, known as ``spikes'', near the black hole event horizon. The density of these spikes is determined theoretically and observationally to be a power law of the form $ρ(r) \propto r^{-γ_\text{sp}}$. It has been shown that the spike can potentially have a detectable impact on the emitted gravitational waves and shadow radius of the central black hole. In this work, we model the dark matter spike using a real scalar field with a non-standard potential. More specifically we ``reverse engineer'' the equations of motion to find a potential for the scalar field that permits a solution to the equations of motion with desired energy density and reasonable background geometry. We show that the emerging geometry is testable. In addition, the fact that the solution is derived from a covariant action makes it possible to study gravitational perturbations of the black hole in the presence of a spike including the backreaction of the spike.

</details>


### [153] [A Tale of Two Calibrators: Comparing Newtonian and Photon Calibration in Advanced LIGO after the Third Observing Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01917)
*Dana Jones,Michael P. Ross,Ling Sun,Jeffrey S. Kissel,Dripta Bhattacharjee,Anthony Sanchez,Bram J. J. Slagmolen,Jens Gundlach*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对LIGO的两种独立直接力校准方法——牛顿校准器（NCal）与光子校准器（PCal）——进行了对比分析。在LIGO Hanford站点第三次观测运行结束后进行的直接力注入实验中，两种系统在30Hz频带内的应变幅度比值差异约为1%，表明两者之间存在系统性偏差，凸显了维持多种独立校准基准以验证LIGO校准准确性的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIGO应变读数的精确校准对从引力波探测中提取信息至关重要，目前LIGO仅依赖PCal系统作为绝对校准参考。为验证单一校准基准的可靠性，需通过对比独立校准方法来评估系统间的一致性。

Method: 在LIGO Hanford天文台第三次观测运行结束后，使用NCal和PCal系统执行了一系列直接力注入实验；对每次注入计算实测应变幅度与预期应变幅度的比值，并在30Hz频带内对各注入频率进行比较。

Result: 在每个注入频率下，NCal与PCal的幅度比值存在约1%的差异，表明两种独立校准系统之间存在小型系统性偏差。

Conclusion: 这一系统性差异强调了维持多个独立校准参考的重要性，以确保LIGO校准结果的可靠性与有效性。

Abstract: Precise calibration of LIGO's strain readout plays a vital role in our ability to extract information from gravitational-wave detections. We present a comparative analysis of two independent direct-force calibration methods: the Newtonian Calibrator (NCal) and Photon Calibrator (PCal) systems. Currently, LIGO relies solely on the PCal system as its absolute calibration reference. To facilitate comparison, a series of direct-force injections were performed using the NCal and PCal systems at the LIGO Hanford observatory just after the completion of the third observing run. We compute the ratio of the measured to expected strain amplitudes for every injection. For each injection frequency across a 30 Hz band, the ratios of NCal and PCal differ by $\sim$1%, demonstrating a small systematic discrepancy between the two systems. This difference highlights the importance of maintaining multiple independent calibration references to validate LIGO's calibration.

</details>


### [154] [Collective Onset of Matter-Induced Scalarization around a Black Hole with Two Thin Shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02066)
*Masahiro Kaminaga*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究一个被两层空间分离的薄物质壳（thin matter shells）包围的静态黑洞发生物质诱导标量化（matter-induced scalarization）的线性起始条件。研究发现两个单独亚临界的壳可以共同使标量自由黑洞失稳，并推导出临界标量云（critical scalar cloud）的闭合解析形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多聚焦于单物质壳背景下的黑洞标量化，但双层壳系统中标量场微扰是否可通过壳间协同时诱发失稳尚不清楚。为了揭示多层物质分布对黑洞标量化起始的集体效应，需要系统分析线性起始条件与临界云结构。

Method: 假定标量自由背景上共形物质耦合（conformal matter coupling）为单位值且其对数导数在该处消失，从而标量微扰在一阶处与度规及物质微扰解耦。对壳间无非零有效质量项（bulk effective-mass term）的静态球对称黑洞外部，将起始条件约化为有限秩条件 1-a1-a2+(1-χ)a1a2=0，其中 a_j 为第 j 个壳的吸引强度按单壳阈值归一化，χ 为两个壳处静态径向电阻（static radial resistance）之比。数值上在 Schwarzschild 探针问题中验证了阈值之上的增长模；对由两个 Israel 壳连接的三段 Schwarzschild 区域构成的精确标量自由背景，另行推导了静态起始条件与临界云。 

Result: 得到双层壳系统标量化线性起始的解析条件，表明两个单独亚临界的壳可以集体地不稳定化标量自由黑洞；临界标量云可闭合解析求解。数值验证显示超越阈值时出现具有有限增长率的增长模；精确 Israel 示迹（Israel trace）存在符号转变，其光壳极限出现在 Schwarzschild 光子球处。

Conclusion: 双层壳系统可显著降低黑洞标量化的阈值，源于不同物质壳对静态径向电阻的联合抑制效应，为多层物质环/壳结构驱动标量化的机制提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: We study the linear onset of matter-induced scalarization for a static black hole surrounded by two spatially separated thin matter shells. We assume that the conformal matter coupling is unity on the scalar-free background and that its logarithmic derivative vanishes there. The scalar perturbation then decouples from the metric and matter perturbations at first order, while the invariant surface traces of the shells give singular terms in the scalar equation.
  For a static spherical black-hole exterior with no bulk scalar effective-mass term between the shells, the onset reduces to the finite-rank condition $$ 1-a_1-a_2+(1-χ)a_1a_2=0, \qquad χ=\frac{{\cal S}(R_2)}{{\cal S}(R_1)}, $$ where $a_j$ is the attractive strength of shell $j$ normalized by its one--shell threshold on the same background and ${\cal S}$ is the static radial resistance. This relation shows that two individually subcritical shells can collectively destabilize the scalar-free black hole.
  We derive the critical scalar cloud in closed form and verify numerically, in the Schwarzschild probe problem, that a growing mode with a finite growth rate appears beyond the threshold. For an exact scalar-free background consisting of three Schwarzschild regions joined by two Israel shells, we separately derive the static onset condition and the corresponding critical cloud. The exact Israel trace also exhibits a sign transition whose light-shell limit occurs at the Schwarzschild photon sphere.

</details>


### [155] [Cosmological implications for hairy black holes via spontaneous symmetry breaking: Are Hairy Black Holes Primordial?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02102)
*Suruj Jyoti Das,Miok Park*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-标量-高斯-博内（ESGB）理论中通过自发对称破缺产生的带毛发黑洞能否与宇宙学演化兼容。研究发现，在演化过程中，GB项的时间依赖性会改变标量场有效势的行为，产生一个从有界到无界再到对称性恢复的阶段转换，由此对耦合强度、截断尺度和黑洞质量施加严格约束。在允许宇宙学演化和标量毛发形成的截断尺度下，仅有质量在几克量级的极轻黑洞（即所谓带毛发的原初黑洞）能够形成标量毛发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究试图回答一个根本性问题：ESGB理论中通过自发对称破缺生成的带毛发黑洞（hairy black hole）是否能与宇宙学演化（cosmological evolution）共存？此前的研究通常仅针对静态黑洞背景，而忽略了其在动态FLRW宇宙学背景下的自洽性问题。

Method: 作者引入带有标量自相互作用的ESGB理论，其中自相互作用在宇宙学尺度上变得重要而在黑洞附近可忽略。由于GB项在宇宙学尺度上的时间依赖性，标量场在演化的FLRW背景与近乎静态的黑洞背景中的动力学行为存在质的差异。作者通过分析有效势在不同膨胀阶段（暴胀期、减速膨胀期以及GB贡献衰减后）的行为变化，考察对称性破缺真空、GB项符号反转导致的有效势无界性，以及对称性恢复的整个过程。

Result: 当标量-GB耦合与带毛发黑洞形成相容时，暴胀期间有效势支撑对称性破缺真空；但暴胀后的减速膨胀会改变GB项的符号，使有效势暂时性地从下方无界。随着GB贡献衰减，标量自相互作用最终占据主导并恢复对称性。由此推导出对耦合强度、截断尺度（cutoff scale）和黑洞质量的严格约束，这些约束主要源于避免无界阶段中标量场扰动的有效放大机制（tachyonic amplification）。在该框架下，只有质量约几克的超轻黑洞（被识别为带毛发的原初黑洞）能够形成标量毛发。

Conclusion: 论文表明，ESGB理论中带毛发黑洞的形成与宇宙学演化之间存在根本张力，仅在截断尺度与耦合参数处于特定狭窄区间、且黑洞质量仅为几克量级时才能实现共存的兼容性。这为带毛发的原初黑洞提供了独特的识别特征和理论约束。

Abstract: We investigate whether hairy black holes generated through spontaneous symmetry breaking in Einstein-Scalar-Gauss-Bonnet (ESGB) theory, involving a complex scalar field with a global $U(1)$ symmetry, can be compatible with cosmological evolution. To this end, we introduce the ESGB theory with a scalar self-interaction that becomes relevant on cosmological scales while remaining negligible near the black hole. Owing to the time dependence of the GB term on cosmological scales, the scalar field dynamics in the evolving FLRW background differ qualitatively from those in the nearly static black hole background. In particular, for scalar-GB couplings compatible with hairy black hole formation, the effective potential supports a symmetry-broken vacuum throughout inflation. However, after inflation, a decelerated expansion changes the sign of the GB term, temporarily making the effective potential unbounded from below. As the GB contribution subsequently decreases, the scalar self-interaction eventually dominates and restores the symmetry. Within this schematic framework, we derive stringent constraints on the coupling strengths, the cutoff scale, and the black hole mass, which primarily arise for avoiding efficient tachyonic amplification of the scalar field perturbations during the unbounded phase. For cutoff scales compatible with both cosmological evolution and scalar hair formation, we find that only ultralight black holes with masses of the order of a few grams can develop scalar hair, identifying them as hairy primordial black holes.

</details>


### [156] [Accretion dynamics of thin and thick disks in charged Kalb-Ramond black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02223)
*Ednaldo L. B. Junior,José Tarciso S. S. Junior,Francisco S. N. Lobo,Jorde A. A. Ramos,Manuel E. Rodrigues,Diego Rubiera-Garcia,Luís F. Dias da Silva,Henrique A. Vieira*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kalb-Ramond（KR）引力中带电静态球对称黑洞的吸积性质。通过对比事件视界望远镜（Event Horizon Telescope）对人马座A*（Sagittarius A*）的观测约束参数空间(Q,l)，发现电荷引入使Q与l之间存在简并，扩大了与观测兼容的参数范围。随后在约束内分析了相对论性吸积盘：几何薄盘的辐射输出随l增大而增强，且电荷会放大该效应；几何厚盘则因洛伦兹对称性破缺而导致轨道半径偏移、平衡态径向范围压缩及稳态流体拓扑发生改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索KR引力（一种包含自发洛伦兹对称性破缺的理论）在强引力机制下是否可通过黑洞吸积现象与观测约束得到检验，并理解电荷与洛伦兹对称性破缺二者如何联合影响黑洞的观测特征与吸积流结构。

Method: 首先利用Sgr A*的阴影半径观测界对KR引力中带电黑洞的自由电荷与KR参数(Q,l)进行联合约束；随后在满足约束的参数区内，分别对几何薄盘（采用时空平均能量通量、温度分布、发射谱、力矩及质量-辐射转换效率等量）与几何厚等角动量环形盘（分析轨道半径、平衡态范围与流体拓扑）进行相对论性吸积流理论计算。

Result: 观测约束给出-0.3900≤l≤0.1898的较宽范围，部分兼容参数区延伸至裸奇点扇区。几何薄盘辐射输出随l增大而增强、随l减小而抑制，且电荷耦合会放大该效应；几何厚盘中洛伦兹对称性破缺使特征轨道半径偏移、平衡构型径向范围压缩、支持有限平衡环的区间变窄，并使区间外的稳态流体拓扑向开放无约束构型转变。

Conclusion: 电荷与自发洛伦兹对称性破缺联合影响黑洞的观测约束、吸积盘结构及辐射性质，为在强引力区域探测KR引力提供了潜在的可观测信号。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the accretion properties of an electrically charged, static, spherically symmetric black hole in Kalb-Ramond (KR) gravity, where a dimensionless parameter $l$ characterizes spontaneous Lorentz symmetry-breaking. We first constrain the free charge and KR parameter space, $(Q,l)$, by comparing the predicted shadow radius of this family of solutions with the bounds inferred from Event Horizon Telescope observations of Sagittarius A*. The inclusion of electric charge introduces a degeneracy between $Q$ and $l$, enlarging the region compatible with the observed shadow size and yielding the broad constraint $-0.3900 \leq l \leq 0.1898$, with part of the compatible parameter space extending into the naked-singularity sector. We then investigate relativistic accretion disks around the charged KR black hole within the above constraints. For geometrically thin disks, we compute the time averaged energy flux, temperature distribution, emission spectrum, time averaged torque, and mass-to-radiation conversion efficiency, finding that the radiative output increases for higher values of $l$, while lower values of $l$ suppress it. Furthermore, when coupled with the electric charge this effect is amplified. For geometrically thick, constant-angular momentum tori-shaped disks, Lorentz symmetry-breaking shifts the characteristic orbital radii, compresses the radial extent of equilibrium configurations, narrows the interval supporting finite equilibrium tori, and shifts the stationary-fluid topology toward open and unconfined configurations outside that interval. These results show how charge and spontaneous Lorentz symmetry-breaking jointly affect observational constraints and the structure and emission properties of black hole accretion flows, providing potential signatures of KR gravity in the strong-gravity regime.

</details>


### [157] [Boundary-Geometry-Driven Black Hole Formation in Vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02311)
*Dipanjan Dey,Puskar Mondal,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在真空广义相对论中识别出一种由边界几何驱动的动力学机制，使边界穿过Yau几何阈值，从而从初始未陷俘的数据迫使边际外陷俘面（MOTS）形成。该机制不依赖短脉冲引力辐射集中效应，并进一步将特征剪切构造解释为同一各向异性真空动力学的零测地线表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确给出动机，可推断为探索真空广义相对论中边际外陷俘面（MOTS）动力学形成的物理机制，寻找不依赖短脉冲引力辐射集中效应的纯真空实现途径。

Method: 该方法在紧致Cauchy域内演化温和的三维各向异性，保持有效各向同性厚度受控，同时允许广义边界平均曲率在边界收缩或膨胀过程中增大，从而驱动边界跨越Yau几何阈值，迫使MOTS从初始未陷俘数据中形成。

Result: 摘要指出，边界几何驱动的机制成功使边界穿过Yau几何阈值并迫使MOTS形成；同时将特征剪切构造解释为该各向异性真空动力学在零测地线（null）框架中的表现形式。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了一种仅通过全局几何效应实现MOTS形成的纯真空物理实现，无需引入短脉冲（short-pulse）机制的引力辐射集中假设。

Abstract: We identify a boundary-geometry-driven mechanism for the dynamical formation of marginally outer trapped surfaces (MOTSs) in vacuum general relativity. Mild three-dimensional anisotropies evolve inside a compact Cauchy domain whose effective isotropic thickness remains controlled, while the generalized boundary mean curvature can increase during either contracting or expanding boundary evolution. This drives the boundary across Yau's geometric threshold, forcing MOTS formation from initially untrapped data. We further interpret the characteristic shear construction of Ref.~\cite{MondalYau2026} as the null manifestation of the same anisotropic vacuum dynamics. The result provides a purely vacuum physical realization of MOTS formation through global geometric effects, without invoking a short-pulse concentration mechanism for gravitational radiation.

</details>


### [158] [Redshift Spectroscopy as a Probe of Regular Black Holes, Black Bounces, and Scalar-Hair Compact Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02313)
*Ali Övgün,Reggie C. Pantig,Joel Saavedra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一且模型无关的框架（unified, model-independent framework），用于分析一般静态球对称时空（static, spherically symmetric spacetimes）中光子频率位移的光谱学问题。框架涵盖质量与非质量探测粒子的守恒量、圆类时测地线条件、光子撞击参数、红移与蓝移分支，并引入源固有运动与色散等离子体效应。作者还给出围绕史瓦西（Schwarzschild）几何的二阶微扰展开，并将该形式应用于多个规则黑洞与类黑洞几何模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以Phys. Rev. D 107, 064019 (2023)为先导工作，作者旨在发展一个不依赖具体模型的一般性光谱学框架，以统一处理不同引力几何中光子频率位移的物理来源。该动机源于在多种候选黑洞与致密天体模型中，频率位移分析的表述各自分离、缺乏统一的几何语言。

Method: 采用含三个任意径向度规函数的线元，推导守恒量、圆测地线条件、依赖局域发射角（local emission angle）的光子撞击参数，以及远处静态观测者测得的红移/蓝移分支的精确表达式。扩展框架以纳入源的视线方向本动运动与非磁化冷等离子体（nonmagnetized cold plasma）中的光子传播；随后围绕史瓦西时空以无量纲形变参数展开至二阶，求取各物理量的显式修正项。

Result: 在真空中，若存在外部零临界轨道，则支配轨道光谱学的同一几何结构也可确定光子球（photon sphere）与阴影撞击参数（shadow impact parameter）。框架被成功应用于非线性电动力学规则黑洞、Simpson–Visser黑洞弹跳（black-bounce）时空及Fisher–Janis–Newman–Winicour–Wyman几何，表明该局域光谱学语言可横跨规则黑洞、黑洞弹跳、虫洞（wormhole）及标量场支撑的无视界模型。

Conclusion: 所发展的框架提供了一套统一的局域光谱学语言，能够在多种引力模型中一致地分解频率位移信号中的引力、轨道与色散贡献，并为形变致密天体模型的光谱学分析提供了可操作的微扰工具。

Abstract: Motivated by [Phys. Rev. D. 107, 064019 (2023)], we develop a unified and model-independent framework for the spectroscopy of photon frequency shifts in generic static, spherically symmetric spacetimes. Working with a line element characterized by three arbitrary radial metric functions, we derive exact expressions for the conserved quantities of massive and massless probes, the conditions for circular timelike geodesics, the local-emission-angle-dependent photon impact parameter, and the corresponding local redshift and blueshift branches measured by distant static observers. The formalism is further extended to include the line-of-sight peculiar motion of the source and the local propagation of photons in a nonmagnetized cold plasma, thereby identifying the gravitational, orbital, and dispersive factors entering the frequency-shift signal under the stated assumptions. We also show that, in vacuum, the same geometric structures governing orbital spectroscopy determine the photon sphere and the shadow impact parameter whenever an external null critical orbit is present. To make the framework suitable for deformed compact-object models, we construct a perturbative expansion around Schwarzschild geometry up to second order in a dimensionless deformation parameter, obtaining explicit corrections to the orbital energy, angular momentum, emitter four-velocity, photon impact parameter, and the vacuum and plasma frequency shifts. We apply the formalism to regular black holes from nonlinear electrodynamics, the Simpson--Visser black-bounce spacetime, and the Fisher--Janis--Newman--Winicour--Wyman geometry. These examples show that the same local spectroscopic language can be used across regular-black-hole, black-bounce, wormhole, and scalar-supported horizonless sectors.

</details>


### [159] [The Analytical Solutions of Dyonic Black Holes in Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02380)
*Haoyuan Luo,Nan Cao,Xiao Yan Chew,Kok-Geng Lim,Chao Chen,Dong-han Yeom*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在爱因斯坦-欧拉-海森堡（Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg）理论中构造了精确解析的带电双荷（dyonic）黑洞解，将此前仅在特殊耦合点 $b=a/2$ 成立的解析解推广到满足 $2b>a$ 的一般非线性耦合情形，质量函数以 Lauricella 超几何函数闭合形式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期研究已构造出纯电与纯磁黑洞的解析解及数值带电双荷解，而最近仅在特殊耦合条件 $b=a/2$ 下获得了带电双荷黑洞的解析构造。作者希望突破这一限制，探索更一般耦合参数空间中的解析带电双荷解。

Method: 作者对普适带电双荷扇区（general dyonic sector）进行重新考察，在不施加 $b=a/2$ 限制的条件下，证明对满足 $2b>a$ 的非线性耦合可以获得精确解析带电双荷黑洞解。质量函数以 Lauricella 超几何函数（Lauricella hypergeometric function）的闭合形式写出。

Result: 构造得到了精确带电双荷解，其适用于满足 $2b>a$ 的耦合参数范围；特别地，对欧拉-海森堡耦合 $b=7a/4$ 给出了精确解。极限情形 $2b\rightarrow a$ 时，解可还原为文献中已有的特殊解析解。

Conclusion: 带电双荷黑洞的精确解析解在爱因斯坦-欧拉-海森堡理论中的适用耦合范围由单点 $b=a/2$ 推广到了连续区间 $2b>a$，显著拓展了解析构造的适用范围。

Abstract: Recently, we constructed analytical purely electric and purely magnetic black-hole solutions in Einstein--Euler--Heisenberg theory, while the corresponding dyonic solutions were obtained numerically \cite{Luo:2026srx}. Motivated by the recent analytical construction of a dyonic black hole at the special coupling locus $b=a/2$ \cite{Ahmed:2026ufj}, we revisit the general dyonic sector. We show that exact analytical dyonic black-hole solutions can be constructed for nonlinear couplings satisfying $2b>a$, without imposing the restriction $b=a/2$. The mass function is expressed in closed form in terms of the Lauricella hypergeometric function. In particular, our construction yields an exact dyonic solution for the Euler--Heisenberg coupling $b=7a/4$. The solution of Ref.~\cite{Ahmed:2026ufj} is recovered in the limiting case $2b\rightarrow a$.

</details>


### [160] [Horizon energy fluctuations beyond Einstein's gravity: Gauss-Bonnet, Lovelock and Quantum deformed frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02456)
*P. B. Krishna,Lini Devassy,Titus K. Mathew*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在正则系综（canonical ensemble）框架下研究宇宙学视界（cosmological horizons）的热能量涨落，证明在渐近德西特（de Sitter）极限下，视界能量涨落收敛到与引力理论无关的常数，并建立了熵最大化、全息均分（holographic equipartition）与能量涨落抑制共同刻画渐近德西特宇宙平衡终态的统一热力学图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在探讨宇宙学视界的热力学性质，特别是检验渐近德西特宇宙是否在热力学意义上处于平衡态，并将视界热涨落行为与不同引力理论及量子修正下的普适性联系起来。

Method: 采用正则系综框架，将宇宙学视界视为由温度和熵表征的热力学系统，对一系列引力理论（包括(n+1)维爱因斯坦引力、高斯的博内引力、拉夫洛克引力以及含量子变形熵修正的模型）进行能量涨落的解析分析。

Result: 研究发现，在渐近德西特极限下视界能量涨落收敛于一个常数，该值不依赖底层引力理论，且对高阶曲率与量子修正具有鲁棒性。此外，宇宙的最终德西特态满足全息均分条件，视界熵达到最大常数值，与普通宏观系统的行为一致。

Conclusion: 这些结果建立了统一的热力学图景：熵最大化、全息均分与能量涨落抑制共同将渐近德西特宇宙刻画为宇宙演化的平衡终态（equilibrium end state）。

Abstract: We study thermal energy fluctuations of cosmological horizons within the canonical ensemble framework, treating the horizon as a thermodynamic system characterized by temperature and entropy. The analysis is performed for a class of gravitational theories, including (n+1)-dimensional Einstein gravity, Gauss-Bonnet gravity, Lovelock gravity, and models incorporating quantum deformed entropy corrections. We find that horizon energy fluctuations stabilize to a constant value in the asymptotic de Sitter limit, independent of the underlying gravity theory and robust against higher-curvature and quantum corrections. It is worth mentioning that in its final de Sitter state, the universe obeys holographic equipartition condition and in consequence the horizon entropy attains a maximum constant value, just like an ordinary macroscopic system. These findings establish a unified thermodynamic picture in which entropy maximization, holographic equipartition, and the suppression of energy fluctuations collectively characterize the asymptotic de Sitter universe as the equilibrium end state of cosmic evolution.

</details>


### [161] [Covariant linear response theory for a photon gas in curved spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02615)
*Jianan Wang,Long Cui,Bin Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个弯曲时空（curved spacetime）中光子气体的协变输运理论：从玻尔兹曼方程出发，采用弛豫时间近似，将输运系数计算至二阶。二阶结果表明，引力场使系数变为张量值并诱导横向通量（与霍尔效应类比），并由此推导出相对论化的菲克定律、傅里叶定律及球对称吸积盘的辐射扩散方程，为强引力场下的高能天体物理辐射输运提供了协变描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非相对论统计力学描述在强引力场（strong gravitational field）中失效，本文旨在发展一个适用于弯曲时空的光子气体协变（covariant）统计描述框架，以解决这一根本性困难。

Method: 从玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equation）出发，采用弛豫时间近似（relaxation time approximation），在弯曲时空中构建光子气体的协变输运理论，并将输运系数系统计算至二阶。之所以不能将有质量粒子结果取无质量极限，是因为无质量（null）粒子具有根本不同的相空间几何。

Result: 在二阶上，引力场使输运系数呈现张量值结构，并诱导出横向通量，这与霍尔效应（Hall effect）现象相类似。利用一阶结果，推导出菲克定律（Fick's law）和傅里叶定律（Fourier's law）的相对论推广，并获得球对称吸积盘（spherically symmetric accretion disk）的辐射扩散方程。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了强引力场中辐射输运的协变描述，可直接应用于高能天体物理（high-energy astrophysics）场景，弥补了传统非相对论统计方法在此类极端条件下的不足。

Abstract: To overcome the failure of the conventional non-relativistic description of statistics in strong gravitational fields, we develop a covariant theory for a photon gas in curved spacetime, starting from the Boltzmann equation and employing the relaxation time approximation. The transport coefficients are computed up to second order, which are not a massless limit of the massive-particle results due to the fundamentally different phase-space geometry of null particles. At second order, the gravitational field renders the coefficients tensor-valued and induces transverse fluxes, analogous to the Hall effect. Using the first-order results, we derive relativistic generalizations of Fick's and Fourier's laws and obtain the radiation diffusion equation for spherically symmetric accretion disks. This provides a covariant description of radiative transport in strong gravitational fields for high-energy astrophysical applications.

</details>


### [162] [Frequency-domain extended-effective-source gravitational self-force for eccentric Schwarzschild orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02635)
*Xuchen Lu,Yungui Gong,Bokai Zhang,Chao Zhang,Wenting Zhou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了频率域扩展有效源（extended effective-source, EES）公式化方法，用于求解Schwarzschild时空中偏心轨道上一阶Lorenz规范引力自力（gravitational self-force, GSF）问题，通过解析延拓径向分支克服谱收敛缓慢的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效源公式是当奇异推迟场无法逐模直接处理时进行引力自力计算的实用途径，但此前该方法仅实现了Schwarzschild时空中圆轨道的频率域一阶GSF计算，偏心轨道尚未被覆盖。

Method: 构建并实现了频率域下偏心轨道的一阶Lorenz规范GSF的扩展有效源公式化处理。为克服径向摆动导致物理穿刺点（puncture）与有效源在内、外分支间切换的困难，对两个分支在摆动（libration）区域进行解析延拓，生成光滑的扩展源，并在此基础上求解耦合的Lorenz规范扰动方程。

Result: 提供了偏心运动情形下端到端的引力扩展有效源数值实现，验证了将频率域有效源计算推广至二阶所需的计算组件可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了有效源方法在偏心轨道GSF计算上的空白，为后续向二阶扩展频率域有效源计算奠定了基础。

Abstract: Effective-source formulations provide a practical route to gravitational self-force (GSF) calculations when singular retarded fields cannot be handled directly mode by mode. Up to now, only frequency-domain first-order GSF for circular orbits in Schwarzschild spacetime was obtained with the effective-source method. We construct and implement a frequency-domain extended effective-source (EES) formulation for first-order Lorenz-gauge GSF on eccentric orbits in Schwarzschild spacetime. The central obstacle is that radial libration forces the physical puncture and effective source to switch between interior and exterior branches, limiting their differentiability and producing slow spectral convergence. We overcome this obstruction by analytically extending both branches across the libration region and solving the coupled Lorenz-gauge perturbation equations for the resulting smooth extended sources. This provides an end-to-end gravitational EES implementation for eccentric motion and validates a computational ingredient needed for extending frequency-domain effective-source calculations toward second order.

</details>


### [163] [Relativistic stellar collapse of initially static configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02655)
*Keshlan S. Govinder,Megandhren Govender,Sunil Maharaj*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究存在电磁场与宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）时辐射相对论恒星的动力学行为，利用相平面（phase plane）分析研究非线性二阶方程，揭示了由非零电荷与宇宙学常数的存在所引发的折叠分岔（fold bifurcation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前针对此类恒星演化的研究结果尚不完整，作者希望构建更一般化的解空间，以澄清早期结论并获得对剪切自由（shear-free）构型在塌缩过程中时间演化的更全面理解。

Method: 研究从初始静态构型出发的演化模型，其时间行为由非线性二阶方程控制，作者采用相平面分析来研究系统的动力学性质，并覆盖包含全部物理参数的一般情形。

Result: 结果表明，在包含全部物理参数的普遍情形下，非零电荷与宇宙学常数的存在可导致折叠分岔现象的出现。

Conclusion: 研究澄清了此前文献中某些结论，并将分析推广至一般的解空间，从而更全面地刻画了辐射相对论恒星在剪切自由塌缩过程中的时间演化特性。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of a radiating relativistic star in the presence of the electromagnetic field and the cosmological constant. The evolution of the model originates from an initially static configuration. The temporal behaviour of the system is governed by a nonlinear second order equation which is studied using a phase plane analysis. We show that in the general case, with all physical parameters present, fold bifurcations arise which are related to nonzero charge and cosmological constant. Our treatment clarifies earlier results and we extend our study to the general solution space. We obtain a more comprehensive understanding of the temporal evolution for a shear-free configuration during collapse.

</details>


### [164] [Thermal Quantum Fluctuations in Einstein-Nonlinear Maxwell-Yukawa Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02674)
*M. Mangut*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究考察了一个由Yukawa屏蔽电磁势生成的Einstein-非线性电动力学黑洞，通过计算基本热力学势与变量分析其热学性质。研究在Bekenstein-Hawking熵上分别添加对数修正项和指数修正项，用以刻画大事件视界与小事件视界情况下的热涨落效应，并通过图形方式探讨了各类情形下黑洞的热力学函数与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在深入理解Yukawa屏蔽电磁势背景下Einstein-非线性电动力学黑洞的热力学特性，并量化热涨落对其标准热力学量修正的影响。

Method: 通过计算基本热力学势与变量，在Bekenstein-Hawking熵上分别引入对数修正项（描述恒背景中量子场产生的修正，适用于大事件视界情形）和指数修正项（反映非微扰量子效应，适用于小事件视界情形），并对两类修正下的热力学函数进行图形分析。

Result: 获得了黑洞在不同事件视界尺度下经对数与指数修正后的热力学函数，并通过图形比较揭示了两类修正对热力学行为的影响模式。

Conclusion: 所有情形下的黑洞热力学稳定性性质均得到详细讨论，研究为理解非线性电动力学黑洞在量子涨落影响下的热力学行为提供了全面的分析框架。

Abstract: This study investigates an Einstein-nonlinear electrodynamic black hole generated by a Yukawa-screened electromagnetic potential and examines its thermal properties through the calculation of the fundamental thermodynamic potentials and variables. The effect of thermal fluctuations on the calculated standard thermodynamic quantities is analyzed through two different correction terms added to the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy. In this work, the thermodynamic behavior in cases where the black hole has a relatively large event horizon is investigated using a logarithmic correction term representing corrections arising from quantum fields in constant backgrounds. In addition, thermal fluctuations occurring in cases where the event horizon is small are analyzed through an exponential correction term added to the entropy, reflecting non-perturbative quantum effects. Finally, the thermodynamic functions for all obtained cases are examined graphically, and the stability properties of the black hole are discussed in detail.

</details>


### [165] [Ultralight Bosons Explain the Mass-Spin Correlations in the Merging Binary Black Hole Population](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02678)
*Xiao-Xiao Kou,Vuk Mandic,Ran Ding,Chi Tian*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究构建了考虑极端轻玻色子（ultralight bosons）超辐射（superradiance）机制的黑洞自旋分布模型，并将其预测与引力波瞬变目录（GWTCs）中的并合双黑洞样本比较。结果显示标量玻色子质量约为10^-12 eV的模型的质—旋关系与观测数据一致，贝叶斯因子在GWTC-5.0中达到ln B≈7.8，为该机制提供了有说服力的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端轻玻色子可能在快速旋转黑洞中触发超辐射不稳定性，形成振荡云并提取转动能；作者希望检验这一物理机制是否能解释观测到的并合双黑洞种群的自旋特征。

Method: 作者提出了一个扩展的、基于超辐射信息的自旋分布模型（superradiance-informed spin distribution model），用于刻画环境引起的自旋变化，并将其预测与引力波瞬变目录（GWTCs）中观测到的并合双黑洞双星样本进行统计比较。

Result: 研究发现，质量为m_b~10^-12 eV的标量玻色子（scalar boson）所预测的质—自旋关系与GWTC观测数据相符，显著性随GWTC版本从3.0到5.0逐步增强；GWTC-5.0的贝叶斯因子达到ln B≈7.8。该质量范围与此前基于GW190728引力波事件波形分析的研究结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 研究结果为超辐射机制启发的自旋分布模型与不断扩大的双黑洞种群数据的强兼容性提供了有说服力的证据，支持极端轻标量玻色子可能在实际天体物理过程中发挥作用。
