Abstract: Ultralight bosons could trigger superradiant instabilities in rapidly spinning black holes, forming oscillating clouds while extracting rotational energy. We consider an extended, superradiance-informed spin distribution model that characterizes possible environment-induced spin variations and compare its predictions with the observed population of merging black hole binaries in the Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalogs (GWTCs). We find that the mass-spin relation predicted by a scalar boson with mass $m_b\sim 10^{-12} \,\rm eV$ is consistent with the GWTCs, with increasing significance from GWTC-3.0 to 5.0. The Bayes factor reaches $\ln B \approx 7.8$ for GWTC-5.0. Intriguingly, this mass range largely coincides with a previous study based on a waveform analysis of the GW190728 gravitational wave event. Our findings provide compelling evidence that a superradiance-informed spin distribution model is highly compatible with the expanding binary black hole population dataset.

</details>


### [166] [Charged black-hole binary radiation at second post-Newtonian order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02825)
*Andrea Placidi,Elisa Grilli,Matteo Pegorin,Marta Orselli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在先前推导的荷电双星系统2PN阶动力学基础上，进一步将分析推广至电磁和引力辐射发射领域，计算了非自旋、准绝热圆轨道双星的总能量通量的2PN阶表达式，并精确推导了矢量场和引力场的辐射多极矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性双星系统的辐射理论已十分成熟，但带电双星系统的辐射行为尚缺乏系统性的高阶后牛顿（post-Newtonian, PN）框架。该工作旨在填补这一空白，为评估引力波信号中电荷的观测特征奠定理论基础。

Method: 将PN匹配的后闵可夫斯基多极展开（PN-matched multipolar post-Minkowskian）和有效场论（effective field theory）两种形式体系同时扩展到带电双星的辐射扇区，并在2PN精度下独立计算相关贡献以交叉验证。推导了矢量场和引力场的无迹对称（symmetric-trace-free）辐射多极矩。

Result: 导出了2PN阶的总能量通量表达式，发现电荷不仅引入新的偶极（dipolar）性质的电磁主导阶贡献，还带来引力扇区中额外的电荷依赖修正。计算了2PN精度的波形球谐多极电荷依赖修正，并发现了由电磁辐射的应力-能量张量（stress-energy tensor）所驱动的新的记忆（memory）贡献。两种形式体系独立计算的结果完全一致。

Conclusion: 这些结果为开发精确的波形模型奠定了基础，有望用于评估引力波信号中电荷的观测特征，推动对带电双星系统的观测辨识与理论理解。

Abstract: In this work, we build on Ref.~[Phys.~Rev.~D 112, 124060 (2025)], where we derived the dynamics of an electrically charged binary system at second post-Newtonian (2PN) order, by extending the analysis, at the same PN accuracy, to the associated emission of electromagnetic and gravitational radiation. We focus on non-spinning binaries that evolve quasi-adiabatically along a sequence of circular orbits, and compute the 2PN expression for the corresponding total energy flux at infinity. With respect to the well-known case of a neutral binary, the presence of charge induces both a new leading-order electromagnetic contribution of dipolar nature and additional charge-dependent corrections in the gravitational sector. To account for this richer phenomenology, we extend both the PN-matched multipolar post-Minkowskian and the effective field theory approaches to the radiation sector of charged binaries, with exact agreement for the contributions computed independently in both formalisms. We derive the radiative symmetric-trace-free multipole moments of the vector and gravitational fields at 2PN order. In doing so, we also compute 2PN-accurate charge-dependent corrections to the spherical multipoles of the waveform emitted by the binary, including a new memory contribution sourced by the stress-energy tensor of the electromagnetic radiation. These results lay the foundation for developing accurate waveform models capable of assessing the observational signatures of electric charge in gravitational-wave signals.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Tuning Boron-Vacancy Qubit Coherence through Layer Number in hBN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01812)
*Juan M. Florez,Eric Suárez Morell*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用广义团簇关联展开（generalized cluster-correlation expansion）方法，计算了h¹¹B¹⁵N中带负电硼空位（VB⁻）缺陷在不同层数下的Hahn回波相干时间T₂，发现T₂从体相极限的199 ns提升至单层的653 ns（增益3.3倍），且增益在低场条件下、三层时即达到饱和。

但其对层数的依赖在动力学退耦（dynamical decoupling）下的1/e阈值测量中消失，表明该增益来自第一壳层调制的零点而非实际衰减。据此提出层数可作为VB⁻传感的设计参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride）中的负电硼空位VB⁻是首个在范德华晶体（van der Waals crystal）内被识别出的可光学寻址自旋量子比特（spin qubit），可在原子尺度上相对于目标定位。然而其在体相中的相干性受邻近缺陷层两侧硼核（boron nuclei）的限制，而薄片可消除这一限制，因此有必要定量研究层数对该缺陷相干时间的影响。

Method: 采用广义团簇关联展开方法，并扩展中央自旋块（extended central-spin block），计算h¹¹B¹⁵N中VB⁻的Hahn回波相干时间T₂随层数的变化；同时进行子晶格分解（sublattice decomposition）以归因各层的贡献，并对堆叠配准（stacking registry）、扭转角（twist angle）及外场方向的对齐角度等参数进行系统性考察。

Result: T₂由体相极限的199 ns提升至单层的653 ns，为3.3倍，且在三层时即饱和；子晶格分解表明该增益完全源自硼核。增益局限于低场条件，对堆叠配准及扭转角不敏感，但要求对齐角度在约2°以内（该onset可由无自由参数模型再现）。动力学退耦下，若采用1/e阈值判断，各层厚度均得206 ns——但此结果源于第一壳层调制的零点而非真实衰减；不经阈值测量时，层间对比依然存在且增益可达170倍。

Conclusion: 层数因此被确立为基于VB⁻的量子传感（sensing）应用中可调控的设计参数（design parameter），其相干增强在恰当测量条件下可得到显著的利用。

Abstract: The negatively charged boron vacancy ($\VB$) in hexagonal boron nitride (\hBN{}) was the first optically addressable spin qubit identified inside a van der Waals crystal, allowing atomically defined placement relative to a target. Its coherence in bulk \hBN{} is limited by the boron nuclei of the layers flanking the defect plane, which a thin flake removes. Here, we use a generalized cluster-correlation expansion with an extended central-spin block to calculate the Hahn-echo coherence time of $\VB$ in h$^{11}$B$^{15}$N as a function of layer number. Our results show that $T_2$ rises from $199\ns$ in the bulk limit to $653\ns$ in a monolayer, a factor of $3.3$ that is already saturated at three layers and that a sublattice decomposition attributes entirely to boron. The enhancement is confined to low field, is insensitive to stacking registry and twist angle, and requires alignment to within about two degrees, an onset we reproduce with no free parameters. Under dynamical decoupling, a $1/e$ threshold returns $206\ns$ for every thickness, but this is a zero of the first-shell modulation rather than a decay: measured without a threshold, the layer contrast survives and reaches a factor of $170$. Layer number thus emerges as a design parameter for $\VB$-based sensing.

</details>


### [168] [Magnons in Metallic Altermagnetic $\text{KV}_2\text{Se}_2\text{O}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01853)
*Daniel Lourenço R. Santos,António T. Costa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过从头算（ab initio）导出的费米子哈密顿量，在随机相位近似（random phase approximation）框架下计算了金属准二维化合物KV₂Se₂O中交变磁（altermagnetic）自旋激发的横磁磁化率，提取了磁振子（magnon）色散关系，揭示交变磁对称性导致电子能带与磁振子谱的简并和方向性劈裂，并发现自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）打开约6 meV的Γ点磁振子能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究交变磁（altermagnetism）如何影响金属性氧钒硒化物中的自旋激发行为，特别是理解交变磁对称性对磁振子色散、各向异性衰减及潜在输运应用的控制作用。

Method: 基于从头算（ab initio）计算导出的费米子哈密顿量，在随机相位近似（random phase approximation）框架内计算横向自旋磁化率（transverse spin susceptibility），并从中提取磁振子色散关系；通过固定波矢大小并改变面内方向来分析磁振子能量与寿命的各向异性；同时纳入自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）考察磁各向异性效应。

Result: 交变磁对称性在电子能带与磁振子谱的高对称路径上引起特征性简并与方向性劈裂；金属性KV₂Se₂O中磁振子因耦合至粒子-空穴连续体（particle-hole continuum）而具有有限线宽；磁振子能量与寿命表现出显著的面内角度依赖，两个磁振子分支呈互补阻尼行为；加入自旋轨道耦合后系统呈现c轴易轴（easy c-axis）面外各向异性，在Γ点打开约6 meV的磁振子能隙，可能使磁序在室温下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 交变磁不仅控制自旋激发的色散，还控制其各向异性衰减，表明交变磁金属可作为方向选择性磁振子输运（directionally selective magnon transport）的 promising 平台。

Abstract: We investigate the spin excitation in altermagnetic vanadium oxyselenides, focusing on the metallic quasi-two-dimensional compound $\text{KV}_2\text{Se}_2\text{O}$. Using a fermionic Hamiltonian derived from ab initio calculations, we compute the transverse spin susceptibilities within the random phase approximation and extract the magnon dispersion relation. We show that the altermagnetic symmetry leads to characteristic degeneracies and directional splittings in both the electronic bands and the magnon spectra along high-symmetry paths of the Brillouin zone. The metallic $\text{KV}_2\text{Se}_2\text{O}$ exhibits finite magnon linewidths arising from the coupling to the particle-hole continuum. Furthermore, by fixing the magnitude of the wave vector and varying its in-plane direction, we uncover a pronounced angular dependence of the magnon energies and lifetimes, with complementary damping behavior between the two magnon branches. Upon including spin-orbit coupling, the system exhibits an out-of-plane easy c-axis anisotropy, which opens a magnon gap of approximately 6 meV at the $Γ$-point, possibly rendering the magnetic order stable at room temperature. Our results demonstrate that altermagnetism controls not only the dispersion but also the anisotropic decay of spin excitations, highlighting altermagnetic metals as promising platforms for directionally selective magnon transport.

</details>


### [169] [Complete Topological Classification with P and T Symmetries: Revealing a Topological Invariant Invisible to K-Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02025)
*Chen Zhang,Y. X. Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文证明K理论（K-theory）框架无法完整分类同时具有P和T对称性的无能隙自旋系统（spinless systems），并通过第一性原理分析对称擂合数据（symmetric clutching data）获得了完整分类，发现了一个从K理论视角不可见的拓扑不变量，从而揭示了K理论分类中缺失的晶格拓扑相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K理论框架为十折对称类提供了完整的拓扑分类，并已被推广到包含晶体对称性的一般情形。然而作者指出，即便在最基本的兼具P和T对称性的无能隙自旋系统中，该分类仍不完整，因此需要更细致的分析来补全分类。

Method: 作者通过对P对称和T对称能带（P- and T-symmetric bands）的拓扑类进行第一性原理分析，即对这些系统对应的对称擂合数据（symmetric clutching data）进行分类，从而获得完整分类。该方法不依赖K理论的预判框架，而是直接从对称性约束出发穷举可能的拓扑类别。

Result: 识别出一个K理论视角下不可见的拓扑不变量，该不变量出现在每个反演不变动量点（inversion-invariant momentum）处的占据态具有均匀正或均匀负宇称（parity）的情形。在特殊情况下（如所有反演不变动量点均匀正宇称时），该不变量可解释为定义在反演基本域（inversion fundamental domain）——即布里渊区一半——上的第二Stiefel–Whitney类或欧拉数（Euler number）。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了一个无法从P和T对称下反演不变动量点处的宇称谱（parity spectra）确定的新拓扑不变量，同时证明了存在K理论分类中缺失的晶体拓扑相（crystalline topological phases），表明K理论框架需要进一步扩展。

Abstract: The K-theoretic framework provides a complete topological classification of the tenfold symmetry classes and has been generalized to incorporate crystalline symmetries. Here, we show that this classification is incomplete even in the elementary case of spinless systems possessing both P and T symmetries. We obtain the complete classification through a first-principles analysis of the topological classes of P- and T-symmetric bands, namely, by classifying the corresponding symmetric clutching data. We identify a topological invariant that is invisible from the K-theoretic perspective when the occupied states at each inversion-invariant momentum have uniformly positive or uniformly negative parity. In special cases, such as when all inversion-invariant momenta have uniformly positive parity, this invariant can be interpreted as the second Stiefel--Whitney class or the Euler number defined over an inversion fundamental domain, namely, half of the Brillouin zone. Our work not only reveals a new topological invariant that cannot be determined from the parity spectra at inversion-invariant momenta under P and T symmetries, but also demonstrates the existence of crystalline topological phases that are absent from the K-theoretic classification.

</details>


### [170] [Persistence and emergence of quantum defects through pressure-induced phase changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02100)
*Alexander J Healey,Alan Salek,Christopher T-K Lew,Zsolt Benedek,Brett C Johnson,Josiah E Hsi,Islay O Robertson,Kaijian Xing,Hiroshi Abe,Takeshi Ohshima,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Mehran Kianinina,Viktor Ivady,Igor Aharonovich,Dougal McCulloch,Jean-Philippe Tetienne,David A Broadway*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文基于六方氮化硼（hBN）中的光学活性自旋对缺陷，展示了它们在高压下的量子传感能力以及压力本身作为量子缺陷工程工具的潜力。这些自旋-1/2系统在高达20 GPa的压力下保持与压力无关的自旋共振，同时维持甚至增强光学发射；此外，压缩可诱导hBN不可逆转变为纤锌矿氮化硼（wBN），在结构转变过程中缺陷景观被重构，并观察到新的高荧光缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端压力可以改变材料及其性质，但由于金刚石压砧需要极小样品体积并施加严格的空间限制，原位探测面临巨大挑战。量子缺陷为在此类条件下进行局部测量提供了途径，但在压力工程和稳定新兴量子缺陷方面仍需系统性探索。

Method: 以hBN中的光学活性自旋对缺陷为统一平台开展研究。一方面，测量其在高压下作为量子传感器的自旋共振频率与光学发射行为；另一方面，利用压缩诱导hBN向wBN的不可逆相变，观察缺陷景观的重新配置并追踪新型高荧光缺陷的出现。

Result: 自旋对缺陷在高达20 GPa的压力下保持压力无关的自旋共振，并维持甚至增强光学发射，性能显著优于同材料中的自旋-1硼空位中心。在hBN向wBN的结构相变中，自旋对传感器得以存续，同时根据起始材料的不同还观察到wBN相中新型高荧光缺陷的形成。

Conclusion: 氮化硼中的自旋对缺陷被确立为抗压的量子传感器，同时高压本身也被证明是制造和调控量子发射器的通用途径，为量子传感与缺陷工程提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Extreme pressures can transform materials and their properties, but probing these in-situ is made challenging by the small sample volumes and access requirements demanded by diamond anvil cells. Quantum defects offer a route to local measurements under such conditions, yet their sensing performance can be dictated by pressure-induced changes in their own host material. On the other hand, pressure may also be harnessed as a tool to engineer and stabilize new quantum defects with emergent functionalities. Here, we demonstrate both aspects within a unified platform based on optically active spin-pair defects in hexagonal boron nitride (hBN). As robust quantum sensors under pressure, these spin-1/2 systems retain pressure-independent spin resonances up to 20 GPa while maintaining or even enhancing their optical emission, in stark contrast to the spin-1 boron-vacancy centre in the same material. Simultaneously, we show that compression acts as a means of quantum defect engineering: the starting hBN undergoes an irreversible transformation into wurtzite boron nitride (wBN), during which the defect landscape is reconfigured. Spin-pair sensors are seen to persist across this structural transition, however, depending on the starting material we also observe new, highly fluorescent defects in the wBN phase. These results establish spin-pair defects in boron nitride as pressure-resilient quantum sensors while highlighting high pressure itself as a versatile pathway for creating and tuning quantum emitters.

</details>


### [171] [In silico design of magnonic lasing in constricted waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02338)
*Jean F. Oliveira da Silva,Rai M. Menezes,Milorad V. Milosevic*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulation）方法，研究了在受自旋极化电流驱动的收缩铁磁波导（constricted ferromagnetic waveguide）中磁子黑洞（magnonic black-hole）现象的实现与表征，展示了双洞腔体对自旋波的共振放大、自旋波激射器（spin-wave laser）的实现，以及界面Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用对临界电流密度的降低作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟黑洞系统（analogue black-hole systems）已在包括磁性材料在内的多种物理平台上被提出，具有如波激射（wave lasing）等诱人应用前景，但实际实现与表征仍未被充分探索。

Method: 采用结合二维泊松方程求解器以获得真实电流密度分布的微磁模拟方法，在收缩铁磁波导中研究自旋极化电流驱动的磁子黑洞现象；系统改变磁场和几何参数以表征共振特性，并引入渐变锥形结构及界面Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用以优化传输效率和降低临界电流。

Result: 成功展示了由模拟事件视界（event horizon）限定的双洞腔体（double-hole cavity）的形成及其对自旋波的共振放大；渐变锥形结构通过抑制自旋波反射增强了传输；界面Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用可模仿电流诱导的多普勒频移（Doppler shift），显著降低临界电流密度并减少焦耳热；最终实现了热激发自旋波经选择性放大后以确定共振频率相干发射的自旋波激光器。

Conclusion: 研究为磁子引力系统模拟体（magnonic analogues of gravitational systems）提供了设计原则，并指明了其在先进自旋电子学（spintronics）应用中的潜在价值。

Abstract: Analogue black-hole systems have been proposed in various physical platforms, including magnetic materials, offering rich physics and promising applications such as wave lasing. However, their practical realization and characterization remain largely unexplored. Here, we present an in silico study of magnonic black-hole phenomena in constricted ferromagnetic waveguides driven by spin-polarized currents. Using micromagnetic simulations with a two-dimensional Poisson solver to obtain realistic current-density profiles, we demonstrate the formation of a double-hole cavity bounded by analogue event horizons, enabling resonant spin-wave amplification. We characterize the resonances as a function of magnetic and geometric parameters and identify the corresponding spin-wave modes. We further show that gradual tapers enhance transmission by suppressing spin-wave reflections, while interfacial Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction can mimic the current-induced Doppler shift, substantially reducing the critical current density required for applications and, consequently, Joule heating. Finally, we demonstrate a spin-wave laser in which thermally excited spin waves undergo selective amplification and coherent emission at well-defined resonance frequencies. These results provide design principles for magnonic analogues of gravitational systems and point toward their potential for advanced spintronic applications.

</details>


### [172] [Room-temperature local strain control of moiré excitons in MoS$_2$/WSe$_2$ heterobilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02340)
*T. O. Oskolkova,Y. Koo,A. A. Shubnic,I. Yu. Chestnov,I. Choi,C. Cheng,D. Huang,T. Jiang,K. -D. Park,I. V. Iorsh,V. Kravtsov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究通过在 MoS₂/WSe₂ 异质双层中使用原子力显微镜（AFM）针尖施加局部应变，实现了室温下莫尔激子（moiré exciton）的激活与调控，并将其发射波长连续调谐至第二电信窗口中心，为室温量子激子器件奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔超晶格为研究量子多体物理提供了多功能平台，但其莫尔激子态此前仅在低温下被研究，严重限制了实际应用。作者旨在探索一种能在室温下激活并调控莫尔激子的方法。

Method: 采用改进的原子力显微镜针尖在 MoS₂/WSe₂ 异质双层上施加机械应变；通过功率依赖的光致发光（power-dependent photoluminescence）测量探究莫尔激子态的布居动力学（population dynamics）；结合受控局部应变连续调谐激子发射波长；并构建理论模型解释所有实验观测特征，包括莫尔激子发射的非常规光谱形状。

Result: 通过应变的施加，观察到一系列归属于莫尔势阱中受限层间激子态的共振；机械应变实现了莫尔激子发射波长向第二电信窗口（second telecom window)中心的连续调谐，且理论模型成功复现了全部实验特征。

Conclusion: 局部针尖诱导应变被确立为可按需操控莫尔激子的强大工具，为室温量子激子器件的开发铺平了道路。

Abstract: Moiré superlattices in heterobilayers of atomically thin transition metal dichalcogenides provide a versatile platform for exploring quantum many-body physics as they can trap excitons, leading to the formation of quantized moiré exciton states. However, such moiré excitons have been predominantly studied at cryogenic temperatures, which severely limits their practical applications. Here, we demonstrate room-temperature activation and control of moiré excitons in MoS$_2$/WSe$_2$ heterobilayers using local strain engineering. Applying mechanical strain with a modified atomic force microscopy tip, we observe a series of resonances attributed to interlayer exciton states confined in the moiré potential. Power-dependent photoluminescence measurements elucidate the population dynamics of the moiré exciton states, while controlled local strain enables continuous tuning of their emission wavelength to the center of the second telecom window. We provide a theoretical model that captures all experimentally observed features, including the unconventional spectral shape of the moiré exciton emission. Our findings establish local tip-induced strain as a powerful tool for the on-demand manipulation of moiré excitons, paving the way for room-temperature quantum excitonic devices.

</details>


### [173] [Relaxation effects on Hartree-Fock ground states in twisted bilayer graphene at even integer fillings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02815)
*Tianyu Kong,Alexander B. Watson,Lin Lin,Mitchell Luskin,Kevin D. Stubbs*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统推导魔角扭曲双层石墨烯（MATBG）连续模型的新方法，在拉格朗日坐标系中纳入结构弛豫（structural relaxation）效应，并利用该模型计算ν=±2填充下的Hartree-Fock多体基态，发现弛豫效应使ν=-2态进入半金属相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准方法通常将库仑相互作用投影到仅包含平带及少量邻近带的有效模型中，但已有方法未系统纳入结构弛豫的影响，这可能影响相关电子相图的预测准确性。

Method: 作者提出了一种全新的系统化推导方式，构建纳入结构弛豫并保持在拉格朗日坐标系（Lagrangian frame）下的单粒子连续模型；随后将库仑相互作用投影到该模型的平带电子上，并在填充数ν=±2处计算Hartree-Fock多体基态。

Result: 研究发现弛豫效应的加入使模型在ν=-2处呈现半金属相（semi-metallic phase），其原因在于弛豫模型单粒子色散中的粒子-空穴不对称性（particle-hole asymmetry），以及平带波函数更集中导致的Hartree势增强。

Conclusion: 该结果与近期从头算密度泛函理论（ab initio DFT）研究一致，两者均发现ν=-2处为半金属相；作者进一步讨论了实验尚未观察到此类相的可能解释。

Abstract: A standard approach for studying magic angle twisted bilayer graphene (MATBG)'s correlated electronic phase diagram is to project the Coulomb interaction down to effective models only involving electrons in single-particle flat bands and some nearby remote bands. We provide a novel systematic derivation of a single-particle continuum model of MATBG's single-particle properties which incorporates structural relaxation while remaining in the Lagrangian frame. We project the Coulomb interactions down to electrons occupying the flat bands of this model and compute the Hartree-Fock many-body ground states at fillings $ν= \pm 2$. We find that incorporating relaxation effects drives the model into a semi-metallic phase at $- 2$ because of particle-hole asymmetry in the relaxed model's single-particle dispersion and because the flat band wavefunctions become more concentrated leading to an enhanced Hartree potential. Our results corroborate recent ab initio density functional theory studies which also found semi-metallic phases at $-2$. We discuss potential explanations for why such phases have not been seen in experiments.

</details>


### [174] [Direct observation of flat bands in near-magic-angle twisted bilayer CVD graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02403)
*Gianluigi Baiardi,Alex Boschi,Giulia Piccinini,Vaidotas Mišeikis,Lorenzo Cavicchi,Aaron Bostwick,Chris Jozwiak,Eli Rotenberg,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Marco Polini,Fabio Beltram,Antonio Rossi,Stiven Forti,Sergio Pezzini,Camilla Coletti*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究通过室温纳米角分辨光电子能谱（nano-ARPES）在基于化学气相沉积（CVD）生长的石墨烯堆叠而成的魔角（MA）扭角双层石墨烯（TBG）样品中观测到了平带（flat bands），其电子能带与基于机械剥离法的样品完全可比，且近魔角畴区尺寸足以支持电子输运实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学气相沉积（CVD）生长技术的进步已将石墨烯晶体质量提升至前所未有的水平，但尚不清楚该路线能否实现魔角（MA）扭角双层石墨烯（TBG）中脆弱的平带及相关态，这一不确定性构成了本研究要回答的核心问题。

Method: 采用基于铜上低压CVD石墨烯的生长-堆叠（grow-and-stack）方案组装接近魔角的TBG样品，并通过室温纳米角分辨光电子能谱（nano-ARPES）对其电子结构进行实验观测。

Result: 实验观测到与机械剥离法制备样品中完全可比的电子能带结构，并确定近魔角畴区的尺寸足以兼容电子输运实验的要求。

Conclusion: 研究表明CVD路线能够实现魔角附近的平带物理，这为进一步在CVD石墨烯中开展平带物理的实验研究提供了动机和基础。

Abstract: Advances in chemical vapor deposition (CVD) growth have driven graphene crystal quality to unprecedented levels, yet it is still unknown whether this route can realize the fragile flat-band and correlated states of the magic-angle (MA) twisted bilayer graphene (TBG). Here, we report on the experimental observation by room-temperature nano-angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (nano-ARPES) of flat bands in a TBG sample close to the MA, assembled via a grow-and-stack protocol based on low-pressure CVD of graphene on copper. Our study indicates electronic bands fully comparable to those measured in exfoliation-based samples and determines the size of the largest near-MA domain to be compatible with electronic transport experiments, motivating further experiments on flat band physics in CVD-graphene.

</details>


### [175] [Rank-Selective Optical Tomography of Higher-Wave Altermagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02429)
*Meysam Bagheri Tagani,Carmine Autieri,Sahar Izadi Vishkayi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出利用有限光子动量实现自旋分辨吸收的秩选择性层析成像（rank-selective tomography），从而在光学上区分高阶波（higher-wave）交替磁序（altermagnetic order）的不同空间谐波，并以d波、g波、i波的q次幂层级及MnTe第一性原理计算加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学上识别高阶波交替磁序的空间秩（spatial rank）具有挑战性，因为局域电偶极响应无法唯一分辨不同的连续谐波。作者旨在建立一种能选择特定磁场谐波（magnetic harmonic）从而实现秩分辨（rank-selective）的光学方法。

Method: 利用有限光子动量将单光子自旋分辨吸收（spin-resolved absorption）转化为秩选择性层析成像；对偶宇称ℓ波分量的平面|m|=ℓ扇区，采用偏振与动量角度的联合傅里叶投影，提取不变量τ_ℓ∝η_ℓ q^{ℓ−2}；并通过满足Ward恒等式（Ward identity）的有限q微观计算加以验证，无需预设幂次。另以MnTe第一性原理计算检验该项方法。

Result: 联合傅里叶投影得到层级关系d:q^0、g:q^2、i:q^4，相变可追踪所选磁性谐波的旋转。离散晶体对称性虽可产生低阶局域混叠（alias），但因其与所选动量谐波正交，在q^{ℓ−2}以下不会造成污染。MnTe计算表明，Néel投影的A区自旋能量对比度超过99.9%的傅里叶功率仍保留在g波衍生的m=3谐波中；结构化近场可将所需动量窗口置于实验可及范围。

Conclusion: 自旋分辨吸收结合有限光子动量可作为高阶交替磁序的秩选择性光学探针，无需依赖局域偶极响应；该方法在MnTe等真实材料中具有可行性，分层结构近场使相关实验窗口可达到。

Abstract: Identifying the spatial rank of higher-wave altermagnetic order optically is challenging because local electric-dipole response does not uniquely resolve distinct continuum harmonics. We show that finite photon momentum turns one-photon spin-resolved absorption into a rank-selective tomography. For the planar $|m|=\ell$ sector of an even-parity $\ell$-wave component, a joint Fourier projection in polarization and momentum angle isolates $\mathcal T_\ell\proptoη_\ell q^{\ell-2}$, yielding the hierarchy $d:q^0$, $g:q^2$, and $i:q^4$; phase changes track rotations of the selected magnetic harmonic. A Ward-consistent finite-$q$ microscopic calculation reproduces these powers without imposing them. Although discrete crystal symmetry can generate lower-order local aliases, they are orthogonal to the selected momentum harmonic and cannot contaminate it below $q^{\ell-2}$. In MnTe, whose nonrelativistic parent order is three-dimensional $g$ wave while spin--orbit coupling lowers the exact relativistic spin-momentum-locking symmetry, first-principles calculations show that more than $99.9\%$ of the Fourier power of the Néel-projected $A$-region spin-energy contrast remains in the parent $g$-wave-derived $m=3$ harmonic. Structured near fields place the required momentum window within experimental reach.

</details>


### [176] [Direct Observation of the Zigzag Edge States of a Supramolecular Diatomic Kagome Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02567)
*Ryohei Nemoto,Xiangzhi Meng,Behzad Mortezapour,Alexander Weismann,Saya Nakano,Masahisa Tsuchiizu,Ryuichi Arafune,Noriaki Takagi,Sho Nakamura,Katsunori Wakabayashi,Rie Suizu,Richard Berndt,Takashi Uchihashi,Kunio Awaga*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过自组装三蝶烯衍生物分子（Trip-Phz）在 Pb(111) 表面上构筑了双原子 Kagome 晶格，结合扫描隧道显微镜（STM）与紧束缚（tight-binding）计算提供了该晶格边缘态的实验证据，这些拓扑边缘态由 Zak 相量子化和体边对应关系决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双原子 Kagome 晶格被理论预测可产生涌现（emergent）和拓扑（topological）现象，但此前缺乏系统的实验研究。

Method: 利用具有 C₃ 对称性、非平面 π 共轭结构的 triptycene 衍生物 Trip-Phz 分子，通过自组装方法在 Pb(111) 表面制备双原子 Kagome 晶格；使用扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM）观测晶格结构，并结合紧束缚（tight-binding）计算进行分析。

Result: STM 观测显示 Trip-Phz 在 Pb(111) 表面形成了高度有序、以锯齿型（zigzag）边缘终止的双原子 Kagome 晶格。STM 测量与紧束缚计算结果共同直接证实了与石墨烯边缘态相对应的边缘态的存在。

Conclusion: 这些边缘态是由 Zak 相（Zak phase）的量子化与体边对应关系（bulk-edge correspondence）所决定的拓扑边缘态。该工作利用超分子技术为探索具有独特晶格几何的量子材料提供了一个理想平台。

Abstract: Lattice geometry plays a fundamental role in the behavior of Bloch electrons in a crystal. The diatomic Kagome lattice, an extension of the honeycomb and Kagome lattices, is predicted to give rise to emergent and topological phenomena, but its experimental investigation has been limited thus far. Here, we fabricate a diatomic Kagome lattice through self-assembly of a triptycene derivative with phenazine moieties (Trip-Phz)---a $\mathrm{C_3}$-symmetric, non-planar $π$-conjugated molecule. Our scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) observations show that Trip-Phz forms a highly ordered diatomic Kagome lattice terminated by zigzag-type edges on the Pb(111) surface. Combined STM measurements and tight-binding calculations provide direct evidence for the existence of the edge states that correspond to those of graphene. These states are topological edge states dictated by the quantization of the Zak phase and the bulk-edge correspondence.This work reveals an ideal platform for exploring quantum materials with unique lattice geometries using supramolecular technology.

</details>


### [177] [Nonreciprocal Magnon Hanle Effect in Antiferromagnetic $α$-Fe$_2$O$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02631)
*Janine Gückelhorn,Sebastián de-la-Peña,Monika Scheufele,Matthias Grammer,Matthias Opel,Stephan Geprägs,Juan Carlos Cuevas,Rudolf Gross,Hans Huebl,Akashdeep Kamra,Matthias Althammer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在α-Fe2O3反铁磁绝缘体中，利用两个空间分离的Pt电极作为自旋注入器和探测器，观测到磁振子（magnon）Hanle信号的非互易性（non-reciprocity），即互换电极角色会改变检测到的磁振子自旋信号，且该差异随外加磁场变化并在补偿场（compensation field）处反转符号，展示了反铁磁magnonics在实现类电子学现象方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁振子赝自旋（pseudospin）在平衡赝场周围进动产生的磁振子Hanle效应，可通过电注入与探测的自旋输运在反铁磁绝缘体中实现，其有望作为探测反铁磁中磁振子本征模式及底层自旋相互作用的便捷探针，并具备器件应用潜力。进一步探索该效应的非互易性特征有助于揭示更多物理机制并拓展应用前景。

Method: 在α-Fe2O3反铁磁绝缘体中，使用两个空间分离的Pt电极分别作为自旋注入器和自旋探测器，通过电学方式注入并探测自旋输运信号；通过互换两个电极的角色，测量磁振子Hanle信号并记录其差异，同时扫描外加磁场以观察该差异的磁场依赖性。

Result: 实验观测到α-Fe2O3中磁振子Hanle信号的非互易性：互换两个Pt电极的注入与探测角色会改变所检测到的磁振子自旋信号；该信号差异依赖外加磁场，并且在信号经过其标称最大值——即所谓补偿场——时发生符号反转。

Conclusion: 该发现揭示了反铁磁magnonics在实现类电子学（electronics-inspired）现象方面的巨大潜力，为反铁磁中磁振子本征模式和自旋相互作用的探测提供了新的实验途径。

Abstract: The precession of the magnon pseudospin about the equilibrium pseudofield, the latter capturing the nature of magnonic eigenexcitations in an antiferromagnet, gives rise to the magnon Hanle effect. Its realization via electrically injected and detected spin transport in an antiferromagnetic insulator demonstrates its high potential for devices and as a convenient probe for magnon eigenmodes and the underlying spin interactions in the antiferromagnet. Here, we observe a non-reciprocity in the Hanle signal measured in alpha-Fe2O3 using two spatially separated Pt electrodes as spin injector and detector. Interchanging their roles alters the detected magnon spin signal. The recorded difference depends on the applied magnetic field and reverses sign when the signal passes its nominal maximum at the so-called compensation field. Our findings unlock the high potential of antiferromagnetic magnonics towards the realization of electronics-inspired phenomena.

</details>


### [178] [Twist as a Mechanical Switch for Reconfigurable Stacking in h-BN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02723)
*Gautham Vijayan,Zhaoheng Zhang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Michael Urbakh,Oded Hod,Xiang Gao,Elad Koren*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种通过外部施加的扭矩在六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride, hBN）层间可重复地切换两种不同稳定堆叠构型（AA与AB模式）的方法，并结合实验与计算建模确认了堆叠序与其动力学特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状材料的扭电子学（twistronics）因其对量子电子学和材料工程的深远影响而成为高度活跃的研究领域，但层间堆叠的可控操纵仍是重大实验挑战。

Method: 通过实验与计算建模相结合的方法，对六方氮化硼层间的均匀接触施加外部扭矩，观察其在两种不同稳定堆叠构型之间的可重复切换，并测量扭矩最大值、接触面积和扭转角度等参数。

Result: 确认两种稳定状态分别为共度（commensurate）AA与AB堆叠模式；两种状态对应的旋转扭矩最大值不同，不对称比为0.7，并随扭转角度呈现不同动力学特征；峰值扭矩与接触面积线性相关，表明边缘弹性（edge elasticity）在扭转过程中起主导作用。

Conclusion: 由于AA与AB堆叠模式对应于不同的面外电极化（out-of-plane electric polarization）状态，该发现为可重构的纳米与微机电（nano- and micro-electromechanical）器件提供了实现路径。

Abstract: Twistronics of layered materials has emerged as a highly active field due to its profound implications for quantum electronics and materials engineering. However, the controllable manipulation of interlayer stacking remains a significant experimental challenge. Here, the homogeneous contact between hexagonal boron nitride layers is shown to be reproducibly switched between two distinct stable stacking configurations via an externally applied torque. Combining experiments and computational modelling, we identify the stacking order of these stable states as the commensurate AA and AB modes. These two states are associated with different rotational torque maxima, exhibiting an asymmetry ratio of 0.7, and distinct dynamics as a function of the twist angle. Moreover, the peak torque values scale linearly with contact area, highlighting the dominant role of edge elasticity in the twisting process. Given that the AA and AB stacking modes correspond to different out-of-plane electric polarization states, our findings offer a pathway for reconfigurable nano- and micro-electromechanical devices.

</details>


### [179] [QArray+: A physics-informed GPU-accelerated simulator for quantum dot arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02736)
*Pranav Vaidhyanathan,Barnaby van Straaten,Alice Petrillo,Rahul Marchand,Edwin De Nicolo,Menno Veldhorst,Brucek Khailany,Taylor L. Patti,Natalia Ares*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出QArray+，它是QArray框架的扩展，引入依赖栅极的隧穿耦合（gate-dependent tunnel coupling）和量子开放系统描述（quantum open-system description），用于统一模拟相干电荷态杂化和非平衡闩锁动力学，从而实现大规模半导体量子点阵列的自劂调谐控制。在100×100栅极电压网格、64个点的规模下，多GPU运行可在约0.17秒完成电荷稳定图计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体量子点阵列是构建可扩展量子技术（scalable quantum technologies）的重要平台，但大规模器件的调谐复杂度高。现有自动化工具依赖简化物理模型（如等电容近似和平衡Hubbard模型），假设系统瞬时弛豫到稳态，无法适用于测量速率超过隧穿动力学（tunneling dynamics）的实验关键区域，因此需要更复杂的非平衡控制策略。

Method: QArray+在原有QArray框架基础上引入依赖栅极的隧穿耦合，并采用量子开放系统的耗散过程描述。该方法能够统一模拟相干子点间电荷态杂化（coherent interdot charge-state hybridization）与非平衡闩锁动力学（non-equilibrium latching dynamics），为训练鲁棒的机器学习模型以支持自动化器件操作提供数据。框架基于JAX实现并支持GPU加速，可跨GPU和多节点系统扩展。

Result: 在多个GPU上，对包含64个点的100×100栅极电压网格计算电荷稳定图（charge stability diagram）约需0.17秒。由于子点间相互作用是短程的（short-ranged）且对应的调谐修正具有局域性，此规模的模拟足以捕捉更大器件相关的物理特性。

Conclusion: QArray+通过精确的非平衡量子开放系统建模，将模拟能力扩展到现有简化模型失效的实验区域，支持高通量数据集生成，为自动化器件调谐（automated device tuning）提供可扩展的高效计算基础。

Abstract: Semiconductor quantum-dot arrays are a compelling platform for scalable quantum technologies, yet their practical operation is hindered by the complexity of tuning large-scale devices. Existing automation tools rely on simplified physical models---such as constant-capacitance approximations and equilibrium Hubbard models---which assume instantaneous relaxation to a steady state. These frameworks fail in experimentally critical regimes where measurement rates exceed tunneling dynamics, necessitating more sophisticated non-equilibrium control strategies. To bridge this gap, we introduce QArray+, an extension of the QArray framework that incorporates gate-dependent tunnel coupling and a quantum open-system description of dissipative processes. This approach enables the unified simulation of coherent interdot charge-state hybridization and the non-equilibrium latching dynamics essential for training robust machine-learning models for automated device operation. Implemented in JAX with GPU acceleration, QArray+ scales across GPUs and multi-node systems. For example, a charge stability diagram for a 100X100 grid of gate voltages over 64 dots can be computed in $\sim0.17\,\mathrm{s}$ on multiple GPUs. Since interdot interactions are short-ranged and the corresponding tuning corrections are local, simulations at these scales capture the physics relevant to even larger devices. These capabilities support high-throughput dataset generation for automated device tuning.

</details>


### [180] [Emergence of the magnetic octupole Rashba-Edelstein effect from spin-orbit entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02768)
*Hojun Lee,Seungyun Han*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文表明自旋-轨道纠缠的多轨道态可以承载磁八极（magnetic octupole）自由度，在Rashba系统中形成磁八极Rashba织构，并可通过电场所诱导的磁八极Rashba-Edelstein效应将织构转化为非平衡八极极化，从而实现电学产生多极磁极化的界面路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁多极作为非常规磁性材料中的序参量和动力学自由度日益受到关注，但其能否广泛成为活跃的电子自由度尚不明确，本文旨在澄清这一问题。

Method: 作者在理论上分析自旋-轨道纠缠（spin-orbit-entangled）的多轨道态，研究其磁八极自由度在Rashba系统中的织构行为，并考察自旋-轨道纠缠能否实现纯磁八极极限（pure-MO limit），进而通过电场驱动磁八极Rashba-Edelstein效应产生非平衡八极极化。

Result: 发现在Rashba系统中磁八极织构伴随J-Rashba织构出现；自旋-轨道纠缠可实现自旋与轨道角动量织构消失而八极织构仍有限的纯磁八极极限；电场可诱导磁八极Rashba-Edelstein效应产生非平衡八极极化，且在纯八极区域该响应超过常规自旋响应。

Conclusion: 多极响应并非对偶极自旋物理的小修正，结果确立了基于界面的电学产生非平衡多极磁极化的有效途径，拓展了自旋-轨道耦合系统中多极自由度作为可调电子自由度的可能性。

Abstract: Magnetic multipoles have attracted growing interest as order parameters and dynamical degrees of freedom in unconventional magnets, yet it remains unclear how broadly they can emerge as active electronic degrees of freedom. Here, we show that spin-orbit-entangled multiorbital states can host magnetic octupole (MO) degrees of freedom. In Rashba systems, this gives rise to an MO Rashba texture accompanying the J-Rashba texture. Remarkably, spin-orbital entanglement can realize a pure-MO limit in which the spin and orbital-angular-momentum textures vanish while the MO texture remains finite. An applied electric field converts this texture into a nonequilibrium MO polarization through an MO Rashba-Edelstein effect. Around the pure-MO regime, the MO Edelstein response dominates over the conventional spin response, showing that multipolar responses need not be small corrections to dipolar spin physics. Our results establish an interface-based route for electrically generating nonequilibrium multipolar magnetic polarization in spin-orbit-coupled systems.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [SN1987A constraints on the neutrino-dark-fermion interaction from resonant scattering with C$ν$B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01716)
*Christina Gao,Ke Hu,Kun-Feng Lyu,Vincent Zou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由轻标量介导的中微子自相互作用（neutrino self-interaction），并利用SN1987A超新星数据对中微子-标量耦合强度进行贝叶斯约束。研究表明，新物理上限不能独立于重味发射、味转换（flavor conversion）或耦合结构来给出，同时预测Hyper-Kamiokande未来高统计事件将恢复有意义的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻标量介导的中微子自相互作用有望解决宇宙学张力（cosmological tensions），并可能自然出现在中微子质量产生机制中。当标量同时耦合到轻暗区费米子时，超新星中微子可与宇宙中微子背景（CνB）发生共振湮灭为不可见暗辐射，耗尽传播过程中的通量，这为约束中微子-标量耦合提供了途径。

Method: 利用SN1987A数据中的通量耗尽效应来约束中微子-标量耦合。在贝叶斯框架（Bayesian framework）下，分析了两种超新星中微子发射模型（参数化模型与二维流体动力学模拟）以及两种耦合类型（质量无关型与质量比例型）。

Result: 结果显示，SN1987A得出的新物理极限不能独立于重味发射、味转换或耦合结构而给出。此外，预测未来Hyper-Kamiokande记录的高统计爆发事件即使包含味转换，也能恢复有意义的上界约束。

Conclusion: 来自SN1987A的中微子-标量耦合约束具有模型依赖性，需综合考虑发射模型与耦合结构。未来Hyper-Kamiokande等高统计探测器有望突破这一限制，提供更具普适性的约束。

Abstract: Neutrino self-interactions mediated by a light scalar offer a compelling resolution to cosmological tensions and may naturally arise in neutrino mass generation mechanisms. When the scalar also couples to a light dark-sector fermion, supernova neutrinos can resonantly annihilate with the cosmic neutrino background (C$ν$B) into invisible dark radiation, depleting the flux en route to Earth. We use this depletion to constrain the neutrino-scalar coupling from the SN1987A data. Within a Bayesian framework we analyze the data for two supernova neutrino emission models, a parameterized model and a 2D hydrodynamic simulation, and for two coupling types, a mass-independent one and a mass-proportional one. Our results show that new-physics limits from SN1987A cannot be quoted independently of the heavy-flavor emission, the flavor conversion, or the coupling structure. Finally, we forecast that a future high-statistics burst recorded by Hyper-Kamiokande would restore a meaningful upper bound even when flavor conversion is included.

</details>


### [182] [A PQ-Symmetric High-Scale SUSY Interpretation of the LZ High-Energy Recoil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01892)
*Wen Yin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将LZ实验观测到的248 keV核反冲类事件解释为1.08 TeV热Higgsino暗物质（Higgsino dark matter）的近阈值上散射事件，并论证该解释所需的超对称（supersymmetry）能谱与高能标引力介导超对称及Kim–Nilles Peccei–Quinn扇区相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ暗物质实验报告了一个248 keV的核反冲类事件，需要寻找可能的暗物质物理解释。作者试图确定该事件是否可归因于一种特定的弱相互作用大质量粒子候选体——热Higgsino暗物质。

Method: 将LZ事件解释为中性质子型Higgsino的近阈值暗物质-核子上散射（upscattering），设定中性Higgsino劈裂质量约0.3–0.35 MeV，从而将其弱相互作用截面（weak-interaction cross section）置于LZ观测区间附近；随后推导电弱规范子（electroweak gauginos）质量标度约为10^7 GeV，并检验该谱与高能标引力介导超对称及Kim–Nilles PQ扇区的兼容性，同时分析质子衰变、axion与混合暗物质等后果。

Result: 该解释要求电弱规范子位于约10^7 GeV能标，与高能标引力介导超对称及Kim–Nilles PQ扇区相容；尽管预测的GUT标度低于常规值，但由于超对称标度很高，质子衰变得以抑制并可能在未来被探测到；PQ对称性将Higgsino质量降至SUSY标度以下，同时产生QCD轴子（axion）解决强CP问题；若轴子与Higgsino构成混合暗物质，则可放松依赖于晕轮廓的H.E.S.S.约束。

Conclusion: LZ 248 keV事件可作为热Higgsino暗物质的近阈值上散射信号得到自洽解释，其超对称谱同时解决常规的CP、味、引力微子（gravitino）及模（moduli）问题，并可对未来质子衰变探测和暗物质间接探测实验提供可检验预言。

Abstract: The 248-keV nuclear-recoil-like event reported by LZ can be interpreted as near-threshold upscattering of 1.08-TeV thermal Higgsino dark matter. A neutral-Higgsino splitting of order $0.3$--$0.35$ MeV places the fixed weak-interaction cross section close to the observed LZ interval and implies electroweak gauginos at ${O}(10^7)$ GeV. This spectrum is compatible with gravity-mediated high-scale supersymmetry and a Kim--Nilles Peccei--Quinn sector. Although the GUT scale is predicted to be lower than the conventional one, proton decay is suppressed due to the high SUSY scale which also solves the conventional CP, flavor, gravitino and moduli problems. Indeed, proton decay may be probed in the future. The PQ symmetry lowers the Higgsino mass below the SUSY scale and also yields a QCD axion that solves the strong-$CP$ problem. If the axion and Higgsino constitute mixed dark matter, the halo-profile-dependent H.E.S.S. limit can be relaxed.

</details>


### [183] [Recoil Geometry Unmasks Gluon Saturation in Forward $Z^0$ Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02356)
*Wanchen Li,Ding Yu Shao,Shu-Yi Wei,Jian Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过“参考系反冲扣除”（fiducial recoil subtraction）技术，将探测器接受度转化为横向动量投影器的方法，从而在前向Z⁰产生过程中揭示胶子饱和（gluon saturation）导致的横向动量展宽（transverse-momentum broadening）信号，并利用色玻璃凝聚（CGC）与软共线有效理论（SCET）的联合框架进行定量分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶子饱和在原子核中产生特征性的横向动量展宽，但QCD辐射在很大程度上抹除了这一特征信号。作者旨在寻找一种方式去屏蔽QCD辐射的干扰，使饱合信号得以显露。

Method: 作者提出参考系反冲扣除方案：将所选快度（rapidity）区间内的强子反冲动量从玻色子横向动量中扣除，定义残余动量。在领头幂次下，该区间内的辐射性反冲被抵消，而残余动量仍保留对小x核场（small-x nuclear field）的敏感性。方法上结合了CGC对小x靶的描述与SCET对有限快度覆盖的求和（resummation）。

Result: 基准快度覆盖|η^lab|<2.5可将Sudakov演化的有效硬标度从M_Z≈91.2 GeV降至约7.5 GeV。增大饱和尺度（saturation scale）会加宽残余动量分布并减弱反冲对齐（recoil alignment）；更宽的快度覆盖则使质子—核区分在这两种观测量中更清晰。

Conclusion: 探测器几何结构为微扰反冲提供了可调控制手段，使非线性小x QCD得以通过前向Z⁰产生实验进行探测量测。

Abstract: Gluon saturation produces characteristic transverse-momentum broadening in nuclei, but QCD radiation largely washes out this signature. We show that fiducial recoil subtraction turns detector acceptance into a transverse-momentum projector that unmasks the broadening in forward $Z^0$ production. Subtracting the hadronic recoil measured in a chosen rapidity interval from the boson transverse momentum defines a residual momentum. At leading power, the radiative recoil in this interval cancels, while the residual momentum retains sensitivity to the small-$x$ nuclear field. Combining a CGC description of the small-$x$ target with soft-collinear effective theory (SCET) resummation for finite rapidity coverage, we find that a benchmark rapidity coverage $|η^{\rm lab}|<2.5$ lowers the effective hard scale from $M_Z\simeq 91.2$ GeV to about $7.5~\mathrm{GeV}$ of the Sudakov evolution. Increasing the saturation scale broadens the residual-momentum distribution and weakens recoil alignment, whereas wider coverage makes the proton--nucleus separation clearer in both observables. Detector geometry thus provides tunable control over perturbative recoil, enabling a probe of nonlinear small-$x$ QCD.

</details>


### [184] [Strong Constraints on Higgsino Dark Matter from Solar Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02775)
*Maxim Pospelov,Harikrishnan Ramani*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了准狄拉克（quasi-Dirac）区的Higgsino暗物质，利用太阳对暗物质的弹道俘获增强非弹性散射，评估暗物质在太阳内湮灭产生的中微子通量，并与IceCube实验高能中微子零观测对比，得到参数空间下限δ>566 keV，排除了LZ事件用放热非弹性散射解释的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Higgsino暗物质是最受认可的主流暗物质候选体之一，当两个马约拉纳（Majorana）分量之间的质量劈裂δ处于几MeV量级或更小时，其可存在于"准狄拉克"（quasi-Dirac）区域。作者需要由此约束该模型空间并检验其与近期LZ实验事件的兼容性。

Method: 利用太阳中心强引力使暗物质粒子加速至约1400 km/sec，从而允许在重元素上发生非弹性散射并有效俘获到太阳束缚轨道；随后评估宇宙学偏好质量mχ≃1.08 TeV下χχ→W+W−, ZZ湮灭所产生的中微子通量，将质量劈裂δ作为自由参数与IceCube实验高能中微子观测结果作比较。

Result: 通过与IceCube未观测到来自太阳的高能中微子对比，获得稳健的下限δ>566 keV；该下限排除了近期LZ实验事件归因于Higgsino暗物质在氙核上发生放热（endothermic）非弹性散射的解释。

Conclusion: 准狄拉克区的Higgsino暗物质在冰立方（IceCube）约束下需满足δ>566 keV，此条件直接排除了LZ事件由放热非弹性散射导致的暗物质解释，因此该模型在观测层面受到显著限制。

Abstract: Higgsino dark matter remains one of the most convincing dark matter candidates. It can exist in a ``quasi-Dirac" regime, when the splitting between two Majorana components is on the order of $O(\rm few~\,MeV)$ or less. Owing to the strong gravitational pull of the Sun, the DM particles accelerate to up to $\sim 1400$\,km/sec in the solar core, which allows for inelastic scattering on heavy elements and very effective capture onto solar-bound trajectories. We evaluate the expected flux of the neutrinos from the expected $χχ\to W^+W^-,ZZ$ annihilation for the cosmologically preferred mass, $m_χ\simeq 1.08$\,TeV while keeping the mass splitting $δ$ as a free parameter. Confronting it with the non-observation of high-energy neutrinos from the Sun by the IceCube experiment, we obtain a robust limit, $δ>566$\,keV. These limits exclude the interpretation of the recent LZ event in terms of endothermic inelastic scattering of Higgsino dark matter on xenon nuclei.

</details>


### [185] [Radiative and Dalitz decays of $Υ(1S)$ in the light of the ATOMKI X17 anomaly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02874)
*Chien-Thang Tran,Mikhail A. Ivanov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在协变约束夸克模型（Covariant Confined Quark Model）框架下研究Υ(1S)→η_b γ辐射衰变和Υ(1S)→η_b e⁺e⁻ Dalitz衰变，给出强子形状因子、辐射衰变常数和分支比的理论预言，并考察假想的ATOMKI X17矢量玻色子对Dalitz通道的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究底偶素（bottomonium）系统的辐射与Dalitz衰变过程，为理解量子色动力学（QCD）中重夸克偶素的衰变机制提供理论预言；同时检验假想的新轻矢量玻色子X17是否存在。

Method: 采用协变约束夸克模型（Covariant Confined Quark Model）作为理论框架，计算Υ(1S)→η_b γ与Υ(1S)→η_b e⁺e⁻过程的强子形状因子（hadronic form factor）与辐射衰变常数，并在标准模型（Standard Model）框架下给出衰变分支比的预言，同时引入X17玻色子作为超出标准模型的贡献来源进行考察。

Result: 标准模型预言Γ(Υ(1S)→η_b γ) = 9.3(9) eV，Γ(Υ(1S)→η_b e⁺e⁻) = 4.8(5)×10⁻² eV。

Conclusion: 给出了Υ(1S)辐射衰变与Dalitz衰变的理论预言结果，并探索了X17玻色子对Dalitz通道的潜在贡献，为将来实验检验提供参考。

Abstract: We study the radiative decay $Υ(1S)\to η_bγ$ and the Dalitz decay $Υ(1S)\to η_b e^+ e^-$ within the framework of the Covariant Confined Quark Model. We provide theoretical predictions for the hadronic form factor, the radiative decay constant, and the decay branching fractions. Within the Standard Model we predict $Γ(Υ(1S)\toη_b γ) = 9.3(9)\, \textrm{eV}$ and $Γ(Υ(1S)\toη_b e^+ e^-) = 4.8(5)\times 10^{-2}\, \textrm{eV}$. The contribution of the hypothetical ATOMKI $X17$ vector boson to the Dalitz channel is also investigated.

</details>


### [186] [Axion Quality from Exact Proton Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01712)
*Joe Davighi,Admir Greljo,Xavier Ponce Díaz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种离散的无反常Z₉规范对称性可在深红外区存续，从而精确禁止质子衰变；该对称性可通过希格斯化一个轻子味非普适U(1)X理论产生，该理论经高标度跷跷板机制生成现实的中微子质量与混合，并预言一个额外轻戈德斯通玻色子；加入手性重夸克扇区后可将其转化为QCD轴子。作者构造了PQ破缺发生在暴胀之前或之后的显式可行模型，并分析了规避稳定重粒子残留与消解弦-畴壁网络等约束，同时给出了高热子生成仍可行且高质量轴子可构成全部暗物质的参数空间区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子衰变在标准模型框架之外被视为验证大统一理论（GUT）的重要信号，如何从理论上精确禁止质子衰变并同时解释暗物质与中微子质量，是粒子物理学前沿的核心问题。作者旨在构建一个统一的框架，将质子稳定性、中微子质量生成以及QCD轴子暗物质三者自然结合。

Method: 通过希格斯机制破缺一个轻子味非普适的U(1)X规范对称性，离散的Z₉剩余对称性自发涌现并被用于精确禁止质子衰变。中微子质量经高标度跷跷板（high-scale seesaw）机制生成，而加入无反常的手性重夸克扇区将戈德斯通玻色子转变为QCD轴子。作者分别构造了PQ（Peccei-Quinn）破缺发生在暴胀之前与之后的显式模型，并分析重夸克谱受稳定重粒子残留与弦-畴壁网络的约束条件，以及热轻子生成（thermal leptogenesis）与轴子暗物质共存的参数空间。

Result: 发现同一套选择定则既禁止质子衰变，又在维度d_PQ≥10以上禁止破坏PQ对称性的算符，包括经由辐射匹配进入标量势的重夸克相关算符。在暴胀后PQ破缺的情形下，需规避稳定重粒子残留并消解弦-畴壁网络，这严格约束了重夸克谱。识别出受限参数区域中热轻子生成仍然可行，且当d_PQ≥13时，高质量轴子可以与热轻子生成共存并构成全部暗物质。

Conclusion: 该框架表明，一个U(1)X起源的离散Z₉对称性能同时实现质子稳定性、现实的中微子质量与混合以及QCD轴子暗物质；通过精细调控模型结构，可将PQ破缺的时点选择与重夸克谱设计作为可行模型的判别条件，进而限定热轻子生成与全暗物质轴子的共存区域。

Abstract: A discrete, anomaly-free $\mathbb Z_9$ gauge symmetry may persist to the deep infrared, exactly forbidding proton decay. This $\mathbb Z_9$ can emerge from Higgsing a lepton-flavour non-universal $U(1)_X$ that generates realistic neutrino masses and mixings through a high-scale seesaw and predicts an additional light Goldstone. Adding an anomaly-free chiral heavy-quark sector turns this mode into the QCD axion. The same selection rules that ban proton decay also forbid PQ-violating operators below a suitably high dimension, $d_{\rm PQ}\gtrsim10$, including operators involving the heavy quarks that radiatively match onto the scalar potential. We construct explicit models viable when PQ breaking occurs before or after inflation; in the latter case, avoiding stable heavy relics and eliminating the string-wall network tightly constrain the heavy-quark spectrum. We identify restricted regions of parameter space where thermal leptogenesis remains viable and, for $d_{\rm PQ}\geq13$, can coexist with a high-quality axion constituting all of the dark matter.

</details>


### [187] [Primordial Black Hole Assisted Dirac Leptogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01717)
*Marco Manno,Anish Ghoshal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了蒸发中的原初黑洞（primordial black holes, PBHs）如何改变Dirac轻子生成（Dirac leptogenesis）机制。通过在最小带电标量中介子模型中求解耦合的Boltzmann方程组，研究发现Hawking辐射可以在热产生效率低的区域填充中介子X，从而增强重子不对称性；PBHs可将成功的参数区域从中介子质量约10^9 GeV扩展到约10^6 GeV，同时部分可观测重子不对称性区域将在未来CMB-S4实验中可检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dirac轻子生成提供了一种与标准机制性质不同的重子不对称性生成路径：总轻子数守恒，但等量且反号的轻子不对称性分别存储于标准模型扇区和右手型中微子扇区。此前缺乏对蒸发原初黑洞如何影响或修改这一机制的专门研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者在最小带电标量中介子（charged-scalar mediator）框架下，构建并求解了耦合的Boltzmann方程组（coupled Boltzmann system），系统纳入热产生、Hawking辐射（Hawking emission）对中介子X及右手型中微子的贡献、原初黑洞对膨胀率的贡献以及熵注入（entropy injection）等过程。

Result: 研究发现，Hawking辐射可以在热产生效率低下的区域有效布居中介子X，其随后的CP破坏衰变可增强重子不对称性。对代表性基准参数而言，PBHs将成功产生重子不对称性的中介子质量范围从约10^9 GeV下扩至约10^6 GeV，并可补偿较小有效CP不对称度。然而这一效应不具有普适性：在热Dirac轻子生成已高效的区域，PBHs反而会降低最终重子不对称性。PBH蒸发还在右手型中微子扇区注入能量，并贡献ΔN_eff。在PBH参数扫描中，部分能复现观测重子不对称性的区域低于Planck参考值ΔN_eff=0.284，高于投影的CMB-S4灵敏度ΔN_eff=0.06。

Conclusion: 蒸发原初黑洞能够在Dirac轻子生成机制中既充当增强器又充当削弱器的双重角色，取决于具体参数区域。研究识别出部分PBH参数区域有望被未来CMB-S4实验检验，为该机制的可测性提供了线索。

Abstract: Dirac leptogenesis offers a qualitatively different route to the baryon asymmetry: total lepton number is conserved, but equal and opposite asymmetries are stored in the Standard Model and right handed neutrino sectors, with only the visible component processed by electroweak sphalerons. We present the first dedicated study of how evaporating primordial black holes (PBHs) modify this mechanism. In a minimal charged-scalar mediator setup, we solve the coupled Boltzmann system including thermal production, Hawking emission of the mediator $X$ and of right handed neutrinos, the PBH contribution to the expansion rate, and entropy injection. We find that Hawking emission can populate $X$ in regions where thermal production is inefficient, allowing its subsequent CP-violating decays to enhance the baryon asymmetry. For representative benchmarks, PBHs extend the successful region from mediator masses around $10^9\,{\rm GeV}$ down to about $10^6\,{\rm GeV}$ and can compensate for smaller effective CP asymmetries. This effect is not universal: where thermal Dirac leptogenesis is already efficient, PBHs can instead reduce the final asymmetry. Their evaporation also deposits energy in the right handed neutrino sector and contributes to $ΔN_{\rm eff}$. In the PBH parameter scan, part of the region that reproduces the observed baryon asymmetry lies below the Planck reference level $ΔN_{\rm eff}=0.284$ and above the projected CMB-S4 sensitivity $ΔN_{\rm eff}=0.06$.

</details>


### [188] [Generalized Foldy-Wouthuysen approach for the derivation of non-relativistic effective field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01980)
*Tobias Asano,Fabio Di Pumpo,Enno Giese,Motoaki Bamba*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种构建非相对论（NR）有效场论（EFT）的系统方法：先构建相对论性有效场论，再基于推广的Foldy-Wouthuysen变换进行广义非相对论约化。该方法从量子色电动力学（QCD+QED）EFT出发，可直接还原出NR量子色动力学与电动力学的拉格朗日量，且隐Lorentz不变性作为构造的直接结果自然涌现，并可用于处理非惯性时空背景及超越标准模型物理（如轴子场耦合）的非相对论极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非相对论有效场论（NR EFT）在惯性平直时空中构建时需额外约束（即隐Lorentz不变性），但在涉及超越标准模型的物理或非惯性时空背景时，这些约束的推导变得十分复杂，隐藏对称结构不够透明。因此需要一种更具普适性的构建路径。

Method: 方法分两步：首先构建一个相对论性有效场论（relativistic EFT），然后基于推广的Foldy-Wouthuysen变换（extended Foldy-Wouthuysen transformation）执行广义非相对论约化。以包含电磁与强相互作用的量子色电动力学EFT在惯性平直时空为例验证方法，并将其应用到一个含赝标量轴子场耦合的简化模型中。

Result: 该广义约化方法成功将量子色电动力学EFT还原为已确立的NR量子色动力学与电动力学拉格朗日量，且隐Lorentz不变性（hidden Lorentz invariance）作为构造的直接结果自动涌现。方法还可推广至非惯性时空中的Dirac场及超越标准模型的扩展理论，并在含轴子场的简化模型中成功推导出其非相对论极限。

Conclusion: 该方法为求解更复杂理论（如非惯性时空背景或超越标准模型扩展）的非相对论极限提供了一条通用路径，隐Lorentz不变性不再是额外施加的约束，而是构造过程中自然产生的性质。

Abstract: Effective field theories (EFTs) are a powerful framework for performing high-precision calculations at reduced complexity compared to their fundamental counterparts. A particularly important class of EFTs arises in the non-relativistic (NR) regime. Their construction relies on a different realization of the underlying symmetries, since Lorentz invariance is no longer manifest in covariant form in the NR regime. This behavior imposes a link between certain matching coefficients, and therefore additional constraints, commonly referred to as hidden Lorentz invariance. These constraints are established in quantum field theories on inertial flat spacetime, such as NR quantum electrodynamics. However, deriving these constraints becomes considerably more involved for theories involving physics beyond the Standard Model or formulated in non-inertial spacetime backgrounds, where the hidden symmetry structure is less transparent. In this work, we present an approach to obtain the NR EFT by first constructing a relativistic EFT and then performing a generalized NR reduction based on an extended Foldy-Wouthuysen transformation. We illustrate this method by a quantum chromo-electrodynamics EFT for inertial flat spacetime, describing both electromagnetic and strong interactions, and show how it reduces to the established Lagrangian of NR quantum chromodynamics and electrodynamics. The hidden Lorentz invariance emerges as a direct consequence of the construction. This approach provides a route to obtain the NR limits of more complex theories, \eg Dirac fields in non-inertial spacetime or extensions involving physics beyond the Standard Model. As an example, we apply the method to add the coupling of a pseudoscalar axion field in a simplified model and derive its NR limit.

</details>


### [189] [$D\rightarrow π$ transitions from QCD Light-Cone Sum Rules with the chiral currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02602)
*Di Gao,Jiangshan Lan,Duojie Jia,Xingbo Zhao,Yanjun Sun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用QCD光锥求和规则（QCD light-cone sum rules）与手征流（chiral currents），系统研究D介子半轻衰变D→P e+νe和D→P μ+ν̄μ中的跃迁形状因子（transition form factors），重点探讨π介子的非微扰结构。数值预言与BESⅢ实验测量和格点QCD计算一致，并在高动量转移区与数据吻合，为精确提取CKM矩阵元及探索标准模型之外的新物理提供机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确理解D介子到轻介子的半轻衰变形状因子，对于提取CKM矩阵元（Cabibbo–Kobayashi–Maskawa matrix element）和探测超出标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）的新物理至关重要，而这一目标取决于对π介子非微扰结构的准确刻画。

Method: 采用带手征流的QCD光锥求和规则计算D→π跃迁形状因子；对π介子分布振幅（distribution amplitudes）同时使用Gegenbauer多项式展开和基光前量子化（Basis Light-Front Quantization, BLFQ）方法，涵盖twist-2及更高twist分量。

Result: 推导出两个联系跃迁形状因子的关系，且与QCD手征对称性（chiral symmetry）一致；数值预言与BESⅢ测量及格点QCD计算结果符合良好；由形状因子得到的D→π微分衰变宽度在q²>1 GeV²的高动量转移区域与BESⅢ数据吻合。

Conclusion: BLFQ方法获得的分布迅速收敛到渐近形式，验证了π介子分布振幅描述的可靠性；高q区域的一致性表明该方法可用于精确提取CKM矩阵元并为新物理探索开辟新途径。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of the transition form factors for the semileptonic decays $D\rightarrow Pe^{+}v_{e}$ and $D\rightarrow Pμ^{+}\bar{v}_μ$ using QCD light-cone sum rules with chiral currents, with emphasis on the nonperturbative structure of the pion. The distribution amplitudes of the meson $π$, including twist-2 and higher-twist components, are analyzed and incorporated in our computation, employing both Gegenbauer polynomial expansions and the Basis Light-Front Quantization (BLFQ) method, where the latter yields a distribution that rapidly converges to the asymptotic form. Two relations linking transition form factors are derived and found to be consistent with the chiral symmetry of QCD. Our numerical predictions for the $D$-to-pion form factors agee well with BES\uppercase\expandafter{\romannumeral3} measurements and lattice QCD calculations. Furthermore, the differential decay widths obtained thereby for the $D$-to-pion decay also show consistency with the BES\uppercase\expandafter{\romannumeral3} data in the high-momentum-transfer ($q$) region with the $q^{2}>1\ \rm{GeV^2}$, implying new opportunities for the precise extraction of the CKM matrix element and the exploration of physics beyond the Standard Model.

</details>


### [190] [Dark Matter at the Kinematic Edge: Interpreting the 248 keV LZ Nuclear-Recoil Candidate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02608)
*Mattia Di Mauro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）合作组报道的一个248 keV核反冲事件的可能暗物质（dark matter, DM）解释，通过结合直接探测、残留丰度（relic abundance）和间接探测约束，指出弹性自旋无关散射难以解释该事件，而热产生的赝狄拉克费米子（pseudo-Dirac fermion）通过非对角矢量相互作用的端热散射（endothermic scattering）机制以及热希格斯子（Higgsino）模型可自然解释该事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验报告了一个248 keV核反冲候选事件，局域显著度3.4σ、全局显著度2.6σ；作者希望探究何种粒子暗物质模型能够同时解释这一高能事件、重现观测到的残留丰度，并满足间接探测约束。

Method: 利用已发表的LZ效率与算子显著度数据，作者分别检验了弹性自旋无关（spin-independent, SI）散射、弹性自旋相关（spin-dependent, SD）O4散射以及端热散射对孤立高能反冲事件的解释能力；进而构建了具有非对角矢量相互作用的赝狄拉克费米子模型和热希格斯子模型，并结合残留密度与间接探测约束进行参数拟合与预测。

Result: 弹性SI散射因能谱集中于低能而难以解释孤立高能事件，而SD O4散射仍然可行；端热散射可自然压低低能率并将谱移向观测能量。对于mχ≈1 TeV的赝狄拉克费米子，残留密度要求σN≈6.5×10⁻⁴³ cm²，劈裂δ≈297 keV可将谱移入LZ事件区域；热希格斯子模型则将质量定于~1.1 TeV，需δ≈371 keV重现该事件。

Conclusion: 结合直接探测、残留密度和间接探测可显著收窄LZ事件的可能解释空间，为未来搜索提供具体目标；该解释可通过伴随的伽马射线线信号（gamma-ray line signal）进行检验。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) Collaboration recently reported one event consistent with a $248$ keV nuclear recoil in a $2.84$ tonne-year exposure, with a maximum local significance of $3.4σ$ and a global significance of $2.6σ$. We investigate whether the dark matter (DM)--nucleon interactions favored by this high-energy event can arise from particle DM models that simultaneously reproduce the observed relic abundance and satisfy indirect-detection constraints. Using the published LZ efficiency and operator significances, we show that elastic spin-independent (SI) scattering poorly explains an isolated high-energy recoil because its spectrum is concentrated at lower energies, whereas elastic spin-dependent (SD) $\mathcal{O}_4$ scattering remains viable. Endothermic scattering instead naturally suppresses the low-energy rate and shifts the recoil spectrum toward the observed energy. A thermal pseudo-Dirac fermion with an off-diagonal vector interaction provides a simple realization of this mechanism. For $m_χ\simeq1$ TeV, the relic-density requirement predicts $σ_N\simeq6.5\times10^{-43}\,\mathrm{cm}^2$, while a splitting $δ\simeq297$ keV shifts the recoil spectrum into the LZ event region. Present-day indirect-detection signals can be strongly suppressed because freeze-out proceeds mainly through coannihilation, while the excited state is depleted at late times. A thermal Higgsino provides a more predictive realization: its relic abundance fixes the mass near $1.1$ TeV, while a splitting $δ\simeq 371$ keV is required to reproduce the event. This interpretation is testable through the associated gamma-ray line signal. Overall, combining direct detection, relic density, and indirect detection significantly restricts the viable interpretations of the LZ event and provides concrete targets for future searches.

</details>

### [191] [Probing Criticality Using GMM-Based Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02522)
*Shashank Sharma,Dipankar Chakrabarti,Vipul Arora*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出一类基于高斯混合模型（Gaussian Mixture Models, GMMs）的标量势函数，使标量场模型既能像自旋模型那样高效采样，又能保留径向涨落以研究自发对称破缺现象（如希格斯现象）。作者为全局 Z₂ 对称性、U(1) 规范对称性和无序系统构建了相应模型，并通过数值实验验证了二维 Z₂ 对称情形属于二维伊辛（Ising）普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋模型（spin model）虽然因其变量的约束性可实现廉价的浴算法（heat-bath）更新从而易于采样，但这种约束抑制了径向涨落（radial fluctuations），使其无法用于研究自发对称破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking）现象，如希格斯（Higgs）现象。因此需要一类既保留径向自由度又保持采样效率的标量理论模型。

Method: 提出基于 GMMs 构造的标量势函数族。该类势函数可以设计为与目标理论属于同一普适类（universality class），从而复现其临界性质的同时实现高效采样。具体针对全局 Z₂ 对称性与 U(1) 规范对称性、无序系统进行了构造。

Result: 通过数值实验验证，二维 Z₂ 对称性情形属于二维伊辛普适类。

Conclusion: 所提出的 GMM 标量势提供了一种兼顾采样效率与径向涨落保留的方案，能够模拟超出自旋模型适用范围的自发对称破缺相关现象，且不损失临界行为的正确性。

Abstract: Spin models with a given symmetry are easier to sample than scalar theories with the same symmetry on a lattice, as the constrained nature of spin variables enables cheap heat-bath updates. However, this constraint suppresses radial fluctuations, and consequently, spin models cannot be used to study the phenomena of spontaneous symmetry breaking, such as the Higgs phenomenon. To address this, we propose a class of scalar potentials based on Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) that are as easy to sample as spin models with a given symmetry. These potentials can be designed to belong to the same universality class as the theory of interest, thereby reproducing its critical properties while enabling efficient sampling. We construct such models for global $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry, $U(1)$ gauge symmetry, and disordered systems. We also verify by numerical experiment that the case of $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry in two dimensions lies in the two-dimensional Ising universality class.

</details>

### [192] [Neural Logic, Invariance, and the Retina---McCulloch and Pitts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02183)
*Nima Dehghani*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文重构了McCulloch-Pitts纲领，将其理解为"神经计算物理学"而非传统的二元神经元图景，系统梳理了从1943年逻辑演算到1959年蛙视网膜研究的发展脉络，涵盖阈值逻辑、递归动力学、线性可分性、异质性、通用识别、充分刺激等问题，并使用现代数学工具分析而避免将现代记号投影到历史文献上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为McCulloch-Pitts纲领常被简化为"二元神经元"的卡通式描述，忽视了其作为神经计算物理学的深层内涵；同时历史文献中许多概念（如异质性网络的效用阻碍、充分刺激的实验化等）在现有的简化叙述中未能得到忠实再现，有必要结合现代数学工具进行系统性重构。

Method: 沿双向路径展开1943年逻辑演算：给定网络可刻画由活动实现的命题，给定可容许逻辑表达式可构造实现它的网络；再现阈值化兴奋求和与绝对抑制性否决之间的原始区分，并将单位时间延迟解读为逻辑深度（logical depth）的物理实现；对递归网络采用有限状态空间精确处理（每一条轨迹最终进入周期轨道）；对线性可分性、有限前馈网络的布尔函数完备性进行分析；综合考察1945年异质性（heterarchy）论文、1947年普遍项（universals）论文及1959年蛙视网膜研究，并引入群平均（group averaging）与反馈正则化（feedback canonicalization）等现代工具。

Result: 研究表明：加权阈值形式无法对任意大的兴奋性输入保持绝对抑制性否决；单个阈值单元只能实现线性可分布尔函数，而有限前馈网络可实现任意有限域上的布尔函数；有限状态网络的行为受有限状态动力学而非无界图灵计算约束；异质性论文表明循环偏好构成标量效用的表示障碍；蛙视网膜研究揭示大脑之前即存在并行不变操作，并通过脉冲触发分析（spike-triggered analysis）指出非线性驱动神经元可具有消失的一阶平均，而二阶统计量可恢复其隐藏选择性。

Conclusion: McCulloch-Pitts纲领应被修复为一种神经计算物理学，其理论遗产涵盖从逻辑演算到实验神经科学的完整谱系；方法论失败可能伪装为生理学缺失，而现代数学工具有助于以不投射现代记号于历史文本的方式澄清原始思想的逻辑结构与局限。

Abstract: This chapter reconstructs the McCulloch-Pitts program as a physics of neural computation rather than the familiar cartoon of a binary neuron. The 1943 logical calculus is developed in both directions: given a net, characterize the propositions realized by its activity; given an admissible logical expression, construct a net that realizes it. We recover the original distinction between thresholded excitatory summation and absolute inhibitory veto-one the weighted-threshold form cannot preserve for arbitrarily large excitatory inputs-and read unit-time delay as the physical realization of logical depth. Recurrence is treated exactly: an autonomous, deterministic network of finitely many binary units has a finite state space, so every trajectory eventually enters a periodic orbit-a fact about finite-state dynamics, not unbounded Turing computation. A single threshold element realizes only linearly separable Boolean functions, whereas finite feedforward networks of them synthesize any Boolean function on a finite domain. We then follows McCulloch and Pitts beyond threshold logic. The 1945 heterarchy paper turns cyclic preference into an obstruction to representation by a scalar utility. The 1947 work on universals asks how a physical network can identify inputs related by nuisance transformations, developed here via group averaging and feedback canonicalization. The 1959 frog-retina study makes the adequate-stimulus question experimental, revealing parallel invariant operations before the brain proper. Spike-triggered analysis shows how a nonlinearly driven neuron can have a vanishing first-order average while second-order statistics recover its hidden selectivity: methodological failure can masquerade as physiological absence. Modern mathematical tools are used without projecting their notation onto the historical papers, and limitations of the idealization are stated explicitly.

</details>

### [193] [Support of Dyson Brownian Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01770)
*Jiaoyang Huang,Shengjing Xu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究β≥1的β-Dyson布朗运动（beta-Dyson Brownian motion），证明在任意固定时间区间内，粒子几乎必然保持在初始经验测度的半圆自由卷积流支撑集的ε-邻域内，且该结论自时间零点成立，无需初始谱边缘的正则性假设，适用于具有宏观内部间隙的多割集（multi-cut）支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机矩阵理论的动力学过程中，需要理解粒子轨迹的谱限制行为。以往结果往往依赖于初始谱边缘的正则性条件或受限的支撑结构，本文旨在建立更普适、自时间零点起即成立的谱禁闭（spectral confinement）结果，覆盖多割集及初始无正则性假设的情形。

Method: 核心工具是确定性的局部预解算子排除原理（deterministic local resolvent exclusion principle）：通过在实轴上某点上方复圆盘中建立Stieltjes变换的 o((nη)^(-1)) 量级比较，可将谱从对应实区间中排除。该原理将局部预解估计转化为谱禁闭，无需模型特定假设。

Result: 对固定的 T, ε>0，以概率至少 1 - C exp(-(log n)^2)，所有粒子在 0≤t≤T 期间始终位于 S_t 的 ε-邻域内，其中 S_t 为初始经验测度的半圆自由卷积流支撑（semicircular free-convolution flow）随时间演化的集合。

Conclusion: 该结果在时间零点即成立，不要求初始谱边缘的正则性，并能处理具宏观内部间隙的多割支撑。所提出的局部预解排除原理提供了一种模型无关的通用机制，可将局部预解估计系统性地转化为谱禁闭结论。

Abstract: We consider beta-Dyson Brownian motion, with $β>= 1$, started from a deterministic configuration with uniformly bounded support. Let $μ_t$ be the semicircular free-convolution flow issued from the initial empirical measure, and set $S_t = supp(μ_t)$. For every fixed $T, ε> 0$, with probability at least $1 - C \exp(-(log n)^2)$, every particle remains within an epsilon-neighborhood of $S_t$ for all $0 <= t <= T$. The result holds from time zero, requires no regularity assumption at the initial spectral edges, and applies to multi-cut supports with macroscopic interior gaps. A key ingredient is a deterministic local resolvent exclusion principle: an $o((n η)^(-1))$ comparison of Stieltjes transforms on a complex disc above a real point separated from the reference support excludes eigenvalues from the corresponding real interval. This gives a model-independent mechanism for converting local resolvent estimates into spectral confinement.

</details>


### [194] [Coalescence and fluctuations of O'Connell$\unicode{x2013}$Yor polymers via the free-energy correlation profile](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02064)
*Firas Rassoul-Agha,Xiao Shen,Ruixuan Zhang,Guangqu Zheng*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文建立了半离散聚合物模型中两点自由能关联的空间导数与两个几何量之间的恒等式，并通过利用模型的内在对称性和无记忆性（memoryless property），规避了连续情形中依赖伊藤公式（Itô's formula）的技术难点，最终恢复水平 Burke 性质并在零温极限下得到布朗末程渗透（Brownian last-passage percolation）的对应恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受到 Gu 和 Quastel 关于 KPZ 方程（Kardar-Parisi-Zhang equation）的积分（integration-by-parts）工作的启发，作者希望在半离散（semi-discrete）聚合物模型中建立类似的恒等式。然而连续情形中关键的伊藤公式在半离散设定下没有直接对应物，因此需要在离散框架内寻找替代技术路径。

Method: 作者建立了空间自由能相关导与两个几何量的恒等关系：同一环境中两条独立采样聚合物在到达指定末端水平之前相遇的退火概率，以及单条聚合物的出口点位置。技术核心在于利用聚合物模型的内在对称性和无记忆性，替代连续设定中伊藤公式的作用。

Result: 提供了两个应用：(i) 通过证明锚定平稳水平自由能剖面（anchored stationary horizontal free-energy profile）是双边布朗运动（two-sided Brownian motion），恢复水平 Burke 性质；(ii) 在零温极限（zero-temperature limit）下，推导出布朗末程渗透对应的恒等式。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，半离散聚合物模型中虽然缺乏连续设定中伊藤公式的直接类比，但通过对模型内禀对称结构的利用仍可建立等价的积分恒等式，并由此推导出重要的统计性质。

Abstract: We establish an identity relating the spatial derivative of a two-point free-energy correlation to two fundamental geometric quantities: the annealed probability that two independently sampled polymers, in the same environment, meet before reaching the prescribed terminal level, and the exit-point location of a single polymer. Our result is inspired in part by recent integration-by-parts work of Gu and Quastel for the KPZ equation. A crucial step in their continuous setting relies on an ingenious application of Itô's formula, which has no direct analogue in our semi-discrete setting. Instead, we exploit intrinsic symmetries of the polymer model together with the memoryless property. We also provide two applications: (i) we recover the horizontal Burke property by proving that the anchored stationary horizontal free-energy profile is a two-sided Brownian motion; (ii) in the zero-temperature limit, we obtain the corresponding identity for Brownian last-passage percolation.

</details>


### [195] [Paracontrolled approach to the stochastic Cahn-Hilliard equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02197)
*Joe Ghafari*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明了在空间维度1至5中，随机Cahn-Hilliard方程（stochastic Cahn-Hilliard equation）局部时间解的存在性与唯一性。方法上，在四维情形下采用Da Prato-Debussche技巧，在五维情形下采用paracontrolled分布技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机Cahn-Hilliard方程是一类重要的随机偏微分方程，其在高维空间中的适定性（well-posedness）问题一直悬而未决。本文的目标是填补空间维度4和5这一关键空白，因为传统方法在这些高维情形下因重整化（renormalization）导致的奇异性而失效。

Method: 本文针对不同维度采用两类方法：在四维（d=4）情形下利用Da Prato-Debussche技巧（Da Prato-Debussche trick）处理重整化奇异性；在五维（d=5）情形下借助paracontrolled分布（paracontrolled distributions）这一更精细的正则性结构工具。

Result: 成功证明了在空间维度1、2、3、4和5中，随机Cahn-Hilliard方程均存在局部时间的解，且该解在相应意义下是唯一的。

Conclusion: 该结果表明随机Cahn-Hilliard方程在所有相关空间维度（1至5）上均具备局部适定性（local well-posedness），为更高维度的推广和全局解的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We prove the existence and uniqueness of a local-in-time solution to the stochastic Cahn-Hilliard equation in space dimensions 1, 2, 3, 4 and 5. Our method relies on the Da Prato-Debussche trick for $d=4$ and paracontrolled distributions for $d=5.$

</details>


### [196] [Metastability of interacting stochastic systems with countably many metastable states: beyond positive recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02331)
*Seonwoo Kim,Jungkyoung Lee*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文将在有限亚稳态数且正递归（positive recurrent）背景下发展的亚稳性（metastability）分解框架，推广到具有可数多个亚稳态、且包含零递归（null-recurrent）与瞬态（transient）动力学的马尔可夫过程。作者证明了在温和的边界正则性假设下，微观分解解的渐近平坦性加上两个紧致性条件，等价于投影示踪过程的分布收敛及亚稳态外停留时间的可忽略性。该框架被应用于两个非紧系统：凝聚包含过程与一维朗之万扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统亚稳性理论通常假设系统仅有有限个亚稳态且处于正递归设定，适用范围受限。作者旨在将基于分解的（resolvent-based）亚稳性理论扩展到可数无穷多亚稳态、涵盖零递归和瞬态动力学的场景，以拓宽该理论的应用边界。

Method: 作者基于分解框架（resolvent framework）提出新判据：在局部紧波兰空间（locally compact Polish space）上，对一族马尔可夫过程，在温和边界正则性假设下，证明微观分解解（microscopic resolvent solutions）的渐近平坦性（asymptotic flatness）加上两个紧致性条件，可等价刻画投影示踪过程（projected trace processes）收敛于极限马尔可夫链，以及系统在亚稳态外的停留时间可忽略。该一般框架随后分别应用于两族具体的非紧随机系统：定义在可数无穷且一致局部有限图上的凝聚包含过程（condensing inclusion process），以及具有可数个稳定平衡点的带小噪声一维朗之万扩散（Langevin diffusion）。

Result: 对凝聚包含过程，作者在过程可能为零递归或瞬态的设定下，证明了凝聚位置收敛于加权随机游走，且远离完全凝聚构型的时间可忽略。对一维朗之万扩散，在最小能垒对应的Eyring-Kramers时间尺度下，建立了阱内局部平衡、阱指标过程收敛于\mathbb Z上的显式最近邻马尔可夫链，以及阱间穿行时间可忽略等结论。

Conclusion: 这些结果将基于分解理论的亚稳性理论从有限亚稳态空间和正递归假设拓展到更一般的可数无穷状态与零递归/瞬态情形，显著扩大了该理论的应用范围。

Abstract: Metastability is typically formulated for systems with finitely many metastable states, most often in positively recurrent settings. In this article, we extend the resolvent framework for metastability to Markov processes with countably many metastable states, thereby encompassing null-recurrent and transient dynamics. For a family of processes on locally compact Polish spaces, we prove, under a mild boundary regularity assumption, that the asymptotic flatness of microscopic resolvent solutions, supplemented by two compactness conditions, is equivalent to convergence in law of the projected trace processes to a limiting Markov chain and to the negligibility of the time spent outside the metastable sets. We apply this framework to two non-compact stochastic systems. First, we study a condensing inclusion process on a countably infinite, uniformly locally finite graph, in a setting where the process may be null recurrent or transient. We prove that the condensate location converges to a weighted random walk on the underlying graph, while the time spent away from the fully condensed configurations is negligible. Second, we study a small-noise one-dimensional Langevin diffusion with countably many stable equilibria, without assuming ergodicity. In the Eyring-Kramers time scale associated with the minimal energy barrier, we establish local equilibration inside each well, convergence of the well-index process to an explicit nearest-neighbor Markov chain on $\mathbb Z$, and negligibility of inter-well excursions. Together, these results broaden the scope of resolvent-based metastability theory beyond finite metastable state spaces and positive recurrence.

</details>


### [197] [Generalised Random Parking for Trapeziums on a Strip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02491)
*David Kramer-Bang,Stjepan Šebek*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究Rényi停车问题（Rényi's car-parking problem）的一种推广，即在带状区域上顺序沉积等腰梯形（isosceles trapezium）。作者推导了停车常数（parking constant）关于两个底边长度比的显式公式，将经典的矩形和三角形模型作为特例恢复，并得到了期望数量和方差在有限尺寸下的定量渐近行为。结果表明停车常数关于两底边长度之比呈非单调依赖，且存在唯一极小值，即最低效率的沉积形状是真正的梯形而非三角形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Rényi停车问题研究随机顺序沉积的一维对象，本文将其推广到等腰梯形沉积的二维修正场景，旨在探索更一般几何形状下停车常数的解析表达与几何参数的依赖关系。此外，研究有限尺寸下的统计渐近行为以及形状对填充效率的非平凡影响，是对已有矩形和三角形特殊情形的自然拓展。

Method: 通过递归构造（recursive construction）分析等腰梯形在带状区域上的顺序沉积过程，推导停车常数的显式公式作为两个底边长度的函数。进一步利用递推关系得到沉积梯形数量的期望值和方差的有限尺寸渐近展开，并计算收敛速率随粒子几何形状的显式依赖。最后对停车常数关于底边长度比的函数性质进行分析，以确定其极值行为。

Result: 得到了停车常数的显式公式，将经典矩形和三角形模型作为其特例恢复。期望数量和方差的有限尺寸渐近带明确且依赖于粒子几何的收敛速率；尽管递归构造涉及两种不同的基底几何（substrate geometries），其方差具有相同的领先阶渐近密度。停车常数关于两底边长度之比是非单调的，并且具有唯一的极小值点，该极小值点对应一个真正的梯形而非三角形。

Conclusion: 本文建立的解析框架揭示了梯形停车问题的完整解，表明在适当的几何参数范围内梯形比三角形填充效率更低，并给出了该现象的普适性描述。该结果为随机顺序填充模型在更一般几何下的理论分析提供了新的视角和工具。

Abstract: In this article, we study a generalisation of Rényi's car-parking problem in which isosceles trapeziums are sequentially deposited on a strip. We derive an explicit formula for the parking constant in terms of the lengths of the two bases, recovering the classical rectangular and triangular models as special cases. We further obtain quantitative finite-size asymptotics for both the expected number and the variance of deposited trapeziums, with convergence rates that depend explicitly on the geometry of the deposited particle. In particular, although the recursive construction involves two different substrate geometries, their variances have the same leading asymptotic density. Finally, we show that the parking constant depends non-monotonically on the ratio of the two base lengths and possesses a unique minimiser, so that the least efficient shape is a genuine trapezium rather than a triangle.

</details>


### [198] [Sharp mean-field estimates for diffusive log/Riesz gases in the Hilbert--Schmidt regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02743)
*Matias G. Delgadino,Rishabh Gvalani,Matthew Rosenzweig*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在扣除主平均场贡献后的固定温度对数气（logarithmic gas）与里斯气（Riesz gas）的微扰分区函数，在Hilbert–Schmidt区间下证明了其一致有界并收敛到由Carleman–Fredholm行列式给出的归一化形式，同时建立了对应的对易子估计与线统计量的中心极限定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在统计力学中，对数气与里斯气模型的微扰分区函数是理解系统精细统计行为的关键对象，但其在高频区间的有界性与收敛性此前尚未被完整刻画，尤其是在Hilbert–Schmidt阈值及其以上的发散速率方面缺乏定量结果。

Method: 方法结合正定截断（positive-definite truncations）与低/高频分解（low/high-frequency decomposition），辅以指数不等式及经典二次U-统计量（U-statistics）的高斯混沌（Gaussian chaos）渐近分析；对吸引对数相互作用则在足够小的逆温度下证明类似结果。

Result: 证明了在Hilbert–Schmidt框架下微扰分区函数的N一致有界性以及对由居中相互作用算子的Carleman–Fredholm行列式给出的归一化的定量收敛；证明了Hilbert–Schmidt阈值的最优性，并在该阈值及之上给出发散速率的显式下界；由此推导出带O(N^{-1})锐利加性尺度的熵对易子估计、静态与动态联合线统计量中心极限定理。

Conclusion: 该工作将此前仅适用于对数气模型的微扰分区函数估计推广到完整Hilbert–Schmidt区间的里斯气模型，并识别出极限行列式归一化形式；对足够低逆温度下的吸引对数相互作用也建立了类似结论，同时指出所得发散速率下界可能并非最优。

Abstract: We study fixed-temperature logarithmic and Riesz gases after subtracting the leading mean-field contribution relative to a prescribed background law. For repulsive interactions in the Hilbert--Schmidt regime, we prove $N$-uniform bounds and quantitative convergence of the resulting modulated partition function to a normalization expressed by the Carleman--Fredholm determinant of the centered interaction operator. We show that the Hilbert--Schmidt threshold is sharp and obtain explicit lower bounds on the rate of divergence at and above it; these rates are expected to be nonoptimal. The proof combines positive-definite truncations and a low/high-frequency decomposition with exponential inequalities and Gaussian-chaos asymptotics for canonical degree-two $U$-statistics. As consequences, we establish entropic commutator estimates with the sharp $O(N^{-1})$ additive scale in the modulated-free-energy method, a static joint linear-statistics central limit theorem, and a dynamical central limit theorem for joint linear statistics at finitely many times. This extends the logarithmic partition-function estimates of the first two authors to the full Riesz Hilbert--Schmidt range and identifies the limiting determinant normalization. For the attractive logarithmic interaction at sufficiently small inverse temperature, we also prove analogous results.

</details>
